diff --git a/packages/cli/src/delivery-recovery-lesson.ts b/packages/cli/src/delivery-recovery-lesson.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..7f3fd266
--- /dev/null
+++ b/packages/cli/src/delivery-recovery-lesson.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,230 @@
+import { deliveryCheckReference, deliveryContractDigest } from "@osstack/core";
+import { writeLessonDraft, type LoopRunOptions } from "@osstack/loop";
+import type {
+  DeliveryReceiptReferenceV2,
+  FrozenDeliveryContractV2,
+} from "@osstack/protocol";
+import {
+  persistDeliveryHostRecord,
+  readDeliveryHostRecord,
+  verifyPersistedDeliveryReceipt,
+} from "./delivery-contract-host.js";
+import { join } from "node:path";
+
+type Observation = Parameters<
+  NonNullable<LoopRunOptions["observeVerification"]>
+>[0];
+interface Attempt {
+  schemaVersion: 1;
+  source: "osstack_verifier_observer";
+  loopId: string;
+  taskId: string;
+  runId: string;
+  iteration: number;
+  contractDigest: string;
+  policyDigest: string;
+  workspaceDigest: string;
+  pass: boolean;
+  checks: Array<{
+    reference: string;
+    exitCode: number;
+    exitConfirmed: boolean;
+  }>;
+}
+/** In-process host port only. No CLI/JSON import endpoint can insert observations.
+ * Durable references are evidence, never authorization; successful recovery is emitted only by this live execution closure.
+ */
+export function createDeliveryRecoveryLesson(input: {
+  frozen: FrozenDeliveryContractV2;
+  stateDirectory: string;
+  assertVerified: (workspace: string) => Promise<string>;
+}) {
+  const attempts: Array<{ record: Attempt; ref: DeliveryReceiptReferenceV2 }> =
+    [];
+  const root = join(input.stateDirectory, "recovery-evidence");
+  let activeLoop: string | undefined;
+  let activeWorkspace: string | undefined;
+  let finalized = false;
+  return {
+    async observe(observation: Observation): Promise<void> {
+      if (
+        finalized ||
+        attempts.length >= 10 ||
+        observation.task.task_id !== input.frozen.contract.task_id ||
+        observation.iteration !== attempts.length + 1
+      )
+        throw Error("lesson_attempt_sequence_invalid");
+      if (
+        activeLoop !== undefined &&
+        (activeLoop !== observation.loopId ||
+          activeWorkspace !== observation.workspacePath)
+      )
+        throw Error("lesson_execution_scope_changed");
+      activeLoop = observation.loopId;
+      activeWorkspace = observation.workspacePath;
+      const workspaceDigest = await input.assertVerified(
+        observation.workspacePath,
+      );
+      const checks = (observation.verdict.verify_commands ?? []).map(
+        (check) => ({
+          reference: deliveryCheckReference(check.command),
+          exitCode: check.exit_code,
+          exitConfirmed: check.exit_confirmed === true,
+        }),
+      );
+      if (
+        checks.length === 0 ||
+        checks.length > 200 ||
+        checks.some((check) => !Number.isSafeInteger(check.exitCode)) ||
+        new Set(checks.map((check) => check.reference)).size !== checks.length
+      )
+        throw Error("lesson_verifier_checks_invalid");
+      for (const check of input.frozen.contract.required_checks)
+        if (!checks.some((actual) => actual.reference === check.registry_ref))
+          throw Error("lesson_verifier_check_missing");
+      const record: Attempt = {
+        schemaVersion: 1,
+        source: "osstack_verifier_observer",
+        loopId: observation.loopId,
+        taskId: observation.task.task_id,
+        runId: observation.run.run_id,
+        iteration: observation.iteration,
+        contractDigest: input.frozen.digest,
+        policyDigest: input.frozen.contract.policy_digest,
+        workspaceDigest,
+        pass: observation.verdict.pass,
+        checks,
+      };
+      const ref = await persistDeliveryHostRecord(root, record);
+      attempts.push({ record, ref });
+    },
+    async finish(inputReceipt: {
+      root: string;
+      ref: DeliveryReceiptReferenceV2;
+      runId: string;
+    }) {
+      if (finalized) throw Error("lesson_already_finalized");
+      finalized = true;
+      const last = attempts.at(-1);
+      if (!last?.record.pass || attempts.length < 2)
+        return {
+          state: "not_qualified" as const,
+          reason: "no_verified_repair_history",
+        };
+      // Re-read every create-only host record; stale/tampered evidence cannot be silently replaced by caller JSON.
+      for (const attempt of attempts)
+        if (
+          deliveryContractDigest(
+            await readDeliveryHostRecord(root, attempt.ref),
+          ) !== deliveryContractDigest(attempt.record)
+        )
+          throw Error("lesson_attempt_evidence_changed");
+      if (!activeWorkspace) throw Error("lesson_workspace_missing");
+      const { receipt } = await verifyPersistedDeliveryReceipt({
+        root: inputReceipt.root,
+        ref: inputReceipt.ref,
+        frozen: input.frozen,
+        runId: inputReceipt.runId,
+        workspace: activeWorkspace,
+      });
+      if (
+        last.record.checks.some((check) => !check.exitConfirmed) ||
+        receipt.run_id !== last.record.runId ||
+        receipt.workspace_state_digest !== last.record.workspaceDigest ||
+        !activeWorkspace ||
+        (await input.assertVerified(activeWorkspace)) !==
+          last.record.workspaceDigest
+      )
+        throw Error("lesson_success_binding_mismatch");
+      const successfulChecks = receipt.checks.map((check) => ({
+        reference: check.registry_ref,
+        exitCode: check.exit_code,
+      }));
+      if (
+        deliveryContractDigest(successfulChecks) !==
+        deliveryContractDigest(
+          last.record.checks.map(({ reference, exitCode }) => ({
+            reference,
+            exitCode,
+          })),
+        )
+      )
+        throw Error("lesson_success_check_mismatch");
+      const failures = attempts
+        .slice(0, -1)
+        .filter(
+          (attempt) =>
+            !attempt.record.pass &&
+            attempt.record.workspaceDigest !== last.record.workspaceDigest &&
+            attempt.record.checks.some(
+              (check) =>
+                check.exitConfirmed &&
+                check.exitCode > 0 &&
+                check.exitCode < 124 &&
+                input.frozen.contract.required_checks.some(
+                  (required) => required.registry_ref === check.reference,
+                ) &&
+                successfulChecks.some(
+                  (success) =>
+                    success.reference === check.reference &&
+                    success.exitCode === 0,
+                ),
+            ),
+        );
+      if (failures.length === 0)
+        return {
+          state: "not_qualified" as const,
+          reason: "no_required_check_failure_with_changed_source",
+        };
+      const key = deliveryContractDigest({
+        contract: input.frozen.digest,
+        attempts: attempts.map((attempt) => attempt.ref.digest),
+        success: inputReceipt.ref.digest,
+      });
+      const result = await writeLessonDraft(
+        {
+          task: {
+            task_id: `recovery-${key.slice(0, 48)}`,
+            capability: "code.fix",
+            source: "manual",
+            priority: 50,
+            prompt: `Host-verified recovery for contract ${input.frozen.digest}. Canonical JSON task identity digest ${deliveryContractDigest(input.frozen.contract.task_id)}. Candidate tree ${receipt.candidate.tree_id}. This is review material; no automatic skill change or publication.`,
+          },
+          iterations: last.record.iteration,
+          feedback: failures.map((attempt) => ({
+            attempt: attempt.record.iteration,
+            verdict_summary: `Required local check rejected source ${attempt.record.workspaceDigest}; host evidence ${attempt.ref.digest}`,
+            blocking_issues: [
+              {
+                severity: "error" as const,
+                message: `Actual verifier command failed. Canonical JSON run identity digest ${deliveryContractDigest(attempt.record.runId)}`,
+              },
+            ],
+            failed_evaluations: attempt.record.checks
+              .filter(
+                (check) =>
+                  check.exitConfirmed &&
+                  check.exitCode > 0 &&
+                  check.exitCode < 124,
+              )
+              .map((check) => check.reference),
+            suggested_focus: [
+              "Keep the same registered check while changing source; independently review the final candidate.",
+            ],
+          })),
+          changedFiles: [],
+          diffSummary: `Changed source: ${failures.map((attempt) => attempt.record.workspaceDigest).join(", ")} -> ${last.record.workspaceDigest}. Successful verification receipt ${inputReceipt.ref.digest}; policy ${input.frozen.contract.policy_digest}.`,
+        },
+        { workspacePath: input.stateDirectory },
+      );
+      return {
+        state: "draft" as const,
+        ...result,
+        evidence: attempts.map((attempt) => attempt.ref),
+        successReceipt: inputReceipt.ref,
+        qualification: "host_verified_required_check_recovery",
+        promoted: false,
+      };
+    },
+  };
+}

+import { createHash, createPublicKey, type KeyObject } from "node:crypto";
+import { constants, closeSync, fstatSync, lstatSync, openSync, readSync, realpathSync } from "node:fs";
+import { lstat, open, realpath } from "node:fs/promises";
+import { dirname, isAbsolute, join, relative, resolve, sep } from "node:path";
+import { z } from "zod";
+import { FollowUpDeliveryTarget } from "@osstack/protocol";
+import type { FollowUpDeliveryOptions, FollowUpDeliveryTrust } from "@osstack/core";
+import { deliveryCanonical } from "@osstack/core";
+import { createGithubDeliveryTransport } from "./follow-up-github-transport.js";
+const ref = z.string().regex(/^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_.-]{0,127}$/);
+const baseName = z
+  .string()
+  .regex(/^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_.-]{0,127}$/)
+  .refine((value) => value !== "." && value !== "..");
+export const HostDeliveryConfig = z
+  .object({
+    schema_version: z.literal(1),
+    enabled: z.boolean(),
+    qualification: z.enum(["sandbox_qualified", "production_qualified"]),
+    qualification_note: z.string().min(10).max(1024),
+    targets: z.array(FollowUpDeliveryTarget).min(1).max(32),
+    accounts: z
+      .array(z.object({ account_id: ref, token_file: baseName }).strict())
+      .min(1)
+      .max(8),
+    keys: z
+      .array(
+        z
+          .object({
+            key_id: ref,
+            public_key_pem: z.string().min(1).max(4096),
+            repo_path: z.string().max(4096),
+            destination_refs: z.array(ref).min(1).max(32),
+            max_validity_ms: z.number().int().min(1000).max(300000),
+          })
+          .strict(),
+      )
+      .min(1)
+      .max(16),
+  })
+  .strict();
+export type HostDeliveryConfig = z.infer<typeof HostDeliveryConfig>;
+const hash = (value: Buffer) => createHash("sha256").update(value).digest("hex");
+function inside(root: string, path: string): boolean {
+  const rel = relative(root, path);
+  return rel === "" || (!isAbsolute(rel) && rel !== ".." && !rel.startsWith(`..${sep}`));
+}
+/** Ancestors cannot let another uid replace the private home. Sticky root-owned tmp is allowed. */
+function privateAncestors(path: string): void {
+  const uid = process.getuid?.();
+  if (
+    uid === undefined ||
+    !isAbsolute(path) ||
+    resolve(path) !== path ||
+    realpathSync(dirname(path)) !== dirname(path)
+  )
+    throw Error("delivery_host_config_invalid");
+  for (let current = dirname(path); ; current = dirname(current)) {
+    const entry = lstatSync(current);
+    const privateParent = current === dirname(path);
+    const stickyRoot = entry.uid === 0 && (entry.mode & 0o1000) !== 0;
+    if (
+      !entry.isDirectory() ||
+      entry.isSymbolicLink() ||
+      (entry.uid !== 0 && entry.uid !== uid) ||
+      (privateParent
+        ? entry.uid !== uid || (entry.mode & 0o077) !== 0
+        : (entry.mode & 0o022) !== 0 && !stickyRoot)
+    )
+      throw Error("delivery_host_config_invalid");
+    if (dirname(current) === current) break;
+  }
+}
+/** Bounded synchronous fence needed immediately before a grant is persisted or consumed. */
+function assertRegistryFingerprint(path: string, fingerprint: string): void {
+  privateAncestors(path);
+  const fd = openSync(path, constants.O_RDONLY | constants.O_NOFOLLOW | constants.O_NONBLOCK);
+  try {
+    const before = fstatSync(fd),
+      bytes = Buffer.alloc(131073);
+    let length = 0;
+    if (
+      !before.isFile() ||
+      before.uid !== process.getuid?.() ||
+      (before.mode & 0o077) !== 0 ||
+      before.nlink !== 1 ||
+      before.size > 131072
+    )
+      throw Error("delivery_host_config_invalid");
+    while (length < bytes.length) {
+      const n = readSync(fd, bytes, length, bytes.length - length, null);
+      if (!n) break;
+      length += n;
+    }
+    const after = fstatSync(fd),
+      current = lstatSync(path);
+    if (
+      length > 131072 ||
+      !current.isFile() ||
+      current.isSymbolicLink() ||
+      current.ino !== before.ino ||
+      current.dev !== before.dev ||
+      current.ctimeMs !== before.ctimeMs ||
+      after.ctimeMs !== before.ctimeMs ||
+      hash(bytes.subarray(0, length)) !== fingerprint
+    )
+      throw Error("delivery_host_config_changed");
+    privateAncestors(path);
+  } finally {
+    closeSync(fd);
+  }
+}
+/** Host private files: canonical directory, same uid, no symlinks, strict modes, finite reads and identity checks. */
+export async function readPrivateDeliveryFile(path: string, maximum = 131072): Promise<Buffer> {
+  privateAncestors(path);
+  if (!isAbsolute(path) || resolve(path) !== path || (await realpath(dirname(path))) !== dirname(path))
+    throw Error("delivery_host_config_invalid");
+  const directory = await lstat(dirname(path));
+  if (
+    !directory.isDirectory() ||
+    typeof process.getuid !== "function" ||
+    directory.uid !== process.getuid() ||
+    (directory.mode & 0o077) !== 0
+  )
+    throw Error("delivery_host_config_invalid");
+  const file = await open(path, constants.O_RDONLY | constants.O_NOFOLLOW | constants.O_NONBLOCK);
+  try {
+    const before = await file.stat();
+    if (
+      !before.isFile() ||
+      before.uid !== process.getuid() ||
+      (before.mode & 0o077) !== 0 ||
+      before.nlink !== 1 ||
+      before.size > maximum
+    )
+      throw Error("delivery_host_config_invalid");
+    const bytes = Buffer.alloc(maximum + 1);
+    let length = 0;
+    while (length < bytes.length) {
+      const result = await file.read(bytes, length, bytes.length - length, null);
+      if (!result.bytesRead) break;
+      length += result.bytesRead;
+    }
+    const after = await file.stat(),
+      current = await lstat(path);
+    if (
+      length > maximum ||
+      !current.isFile() ||
+      current.isSymbolicLink() ||
+      before.dev !== current.dev ||
+      before.ino !== current.ino ||
+      before.ctimeMs !== current.ctimeMs ||
+      before.mtimeMs !== current.mtimeMs ||
+      before.size !== after.size ||
+      before.ctimeMs !== after.ctimeMs ||
+      (await realpath(dirname(path))) !== dirname(path)
+    )
+      throw Error("delivery_host_config_changed");
+    privateAncestors(path);
+    return bytes.subarray(0, length);
+  } finally {
+    await file.close();
+  }
+}
+export async function readHostDeliveryConfig(configPath: string): Promise<{
+  config: HostDeliveryConfig;
+  fingerprint: string;
+  keys: Map<string, KeyObject>;
+}> {
+  const raw = await readPrivateDeliveryFile(configPath);
+  const config = HostDeliveryConfig.parse(JSON.parse(new TextDecoder("utf-8", { fatal: true }).decode(raw)));
+  for (const array of [
+    config.targets.map((t) => t.destination_ref),
+    config.accounts.map((a) => a.account_id),
+    config.keys.map((k) => k.key_id),
+  ])
+    if (new Set(array).size !== array.length) throw Error("delivery_host_config_invalid");
+  const keys = new Map<string, KeyObject>();
+  for (const target of config.targets) {
+    if (
+      (await realpath(target.repo_path)) !== target.repo_path ||
+      inside(target.repo_path, configPath) ||
+      !config.accounts.some((a) => a.account_id === target.account_id)
+    )
+      throw Error("delivery_host_config_invalid");
+  }
+  for (const key of config.keys) {
+    if (
+      (await realpath(key.repo_path)) !== key.repo_path ||
+      key.destination_refs.some(
+        (ref) => !config.targets.some((t) => t.destination_ref === ref && t.repo_path === key.repo_path),
+      )
+    )
+      throw Error("delivery_host_config_invalid");
+    if (!key.public_key_pem.startsWith("-----BEGIN PUBLIC KEY-----"))
+      throw Error("delivery_host_config_invalid");
+    const publicKey = createPublicKey(key.public_key_pem);
+    if (publicKey.type !== "public" || publicKey.asymmetricKeyType !== "ed25519")
+      throw Error("delivery_host_config_invalid");
+    keys.set(key.key_id, publicKey);
+  }
+  return { config, fingerprint: hash(raw), keys };
+}
+export type HostFollowUpDelivery =
+  | { state: "ready"; options: FollowUpDeliveryOptions; fingerprint: string }
+  | { state: "disabled" | "invalid"; options: FollowUpDeliveryOptions };
+/** Call only with the daemon's fixed OSSTACK_HOME/follow-up-delivery.json, never a repository/RPC path. */
+export async function loadHostFollowUpDelivery(configPath: string): Promise<HostFollowUpDelivery> {
+  try {
+    const loaded = await readHostDeliveryConfig(configPath),
+      { config, keys } = loaded;
+    if (!config.enabled) return { state: "disabled", options: {} };
+    const targets = new Map(config.targets.map((t) => [t.destination_ref, t]));
+    const trustedKeys = new Map<string, FollowUpDeliveryTrust>(
+      config.keys.map((k) => [
+        k.key_id,
+        {
+          publicKey: keys.get(k.key_id)!,
+          purpose: "follow_up_delivery",
+          repo_path: k.repo_path,
+          provider_id: "github_comment",
+          destination_refs: k.destination_refs,
+          max_validity_ms: k.max_validity_ms,
+        },
+      ]),
+    );
+    const credentialFingerprints = new Map<string, { path: string; fingerprint: string; token: string }>();
+    const transport = createGithubDeliveryTransport({
+      qualification: config.qualification,
+      assertCredential: (envelope, token) => {
+        assertRegistryFingerprint(configPath, loaded.fingerprint);
+        const entry = credentialFingerprints.get(envelope.destination.account_id);
+        if (!entry || entry.token !== token) throw Error("delivery_host_account_unavailable");
+        assertRegistryFingerprint(entry.path, entry.fingerprint);
+      },
+      credential: async (envelope) => {
+        const current = await readHostDeliveryConfig(configPath);
+        if (
+          !current.config.enabled ||
+          current.fingerprint !== loaded.fingerprint ||
+          deliveryCanonical(targets.get(envelope.destination.destination_ref)) !==
+            deliveryCanonical(envelope.destination)
+        )
+          throw Error("delivery_host_config_changed");
+        const account = config.accounts.find((a) => a.account_id === envelope.destination.account_id);
+        if (!account) throw Error("delivery_host_account_unavailable");
+        const raw = await readPrivateDeliveryFile(join(dirname(configPath), account.token_file), 4097);
+        const token = new TextDecoder("utf-8", { fatal: true }).decode(raw).trim();
+        if (!/^[\x21-\x7e]{1,4096}$/.test(token)) throw Error("delivery_host_account_unavailable");
+        credentialFingerprints.set(account.account_id, {
+          path: join(dirname(configPath), account.token_file),
+          fingerprint: hash(raw),
+          token,
+        });
+        return token;
+      },
+    });
+    return {
+      state: "ready",
+      fingerprint: loaded.fingerprint,
+      options: {
+        assertHostConfiguration: () => assertRegistryFingerprint(configPath, loaded.fingerprint),
+        targets,
+        trustedKeys,
+        transports: new Map([["github_comment", transport]]),
+      },
+    };
+  } catch (error) {
+    return {
+      state: error instanceof Error && "code" in error && error.code === "ENOENT" ? "disabled" : "invalid",
+      options: {},
+    };
+  }
+}

+import { createHash, createPrivateKey, createPublicKey, randomUUID, sign } from "node:crypto";
+import { constants } from "node:fs";
+import { lstat, open, realpath } from "node:fs/promises";
+import { dirname, isAbsolute, join } from "node:path";
+import { createInterface } from "node:readline/promises";
+import { FollowUpDeliveryResult, SignedFollowUpDeliveryGrant } from "@osstack/protocol";
+import { deliveryCanonical, serializeFollowUpDelivery } from "@osstack/core";
+import { githubDeliveryBody } from "./follow-up-github-transport.js";
+import { readHostDeliveryConfig, readPrivateDeliveryFile } from "./follow-up-delivery-host.js";
+
+export const FOLLOW_UP_DELIVERY_ISSUER_HELP = `follow-up-delivery issue-grant ID --repo PATH --default-tenant --revision N --key KEY --output FILE
+Local operator only: requires interactive stdin/stderr, displays the exact public wire body and fixed target,
+and requires typing APPROVE plus the entire preview digest. There is no --yes or RPC signer.
+The host private config pins trust; issuer-KEY.pem must match its Ed25519 public key.
+Creates a new private grant file; does not authorize or send. Same-uid hostile processes are outside this authority boundary.\n`;
+export interface DeliveryIssuerDependencies {
+  home: string;
+  rpc(method: string, params: unknown): Promise<unknown>;
+  stdout?: (text: string) => void;
+  stderr?: (text: string) => void;
+  /** Host test seam only, never CLI/RPC input. The real implementation requires a local interactive terminal. */
+  confirm?: (preview: string, phrase: string) => Promise<boolean>;
+}
+async function terminalConfirm(preview: string, phrase: string): Promise<boolean> {
+  if (!process.stdin.isTTY || !process.stderr.isTTY) throw Error("delivery_issuer_terminal_required");
+  process.stderr.write(preview);
+  const terminal = createInterface({
+    input: process.stdin,
+    output: process.stderr,
+    terminal: true,
+  });
+  try {
+    return (
+      (await terminal.question(`Type exactly ${phrase}\n> `, {
+        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(60000),
+      })) === phrase
+    );
+  } finally {
+    terminal.close();
+  }
+}
+export async function handleFollowUpDeliveryIssuerCommand(
+  args: string[],
+  deps: DeliveryIssuerDependencies,
+): Promise<number> {
+  const out =
+      deps.stdout ??
+      ((text) => {
+        process.stdout.write(text);
+      }),
+    err =
+      deps.stderr ??
+      ((text) => {
+        process.stderr.write(text);
+      });
+  if ((args.length === 1 && args[0] === "--help") || (args.length === 2 && args[0] === "issue-grant" && args[1] === "--help")) {
+    out(FOLLOW_UP_DELIVERY_ISSUER_HELP);
+    return 0;
+  }
+  try {
+    if (args[0] !== "issue-grant" || args.length > 14 || args.some((v) => Buffer.byteLength(v) > 4096))
+      throw Error();
+    const flags = new Map<string, string | true>();
+    for (let i = 2; i < args.length; i++) {
+      const flag = args[i]!;
+      if (
+        !["--repo", "--default-tenant", "--revision", "--key", "--output"].includes(flag) ||
+        flags.has(flag)
+      )
+        throw Error();
+      if (flag === "--default-tenant") flags.set(flag, true);
+      else {
+        const value = args[++i];
+        if (!value || value.startsWith("--")) throw Error();
+        flags.set(flag, value);
+      }
+    }
+    const repo = flags.get("--repo"),
+      keyId = flags.get("--key"),
+      output = flags.get("--output"),
+      revision = flags.get("--revision");
+    if (
+      typeof repo !== "string" ||
+      !isAbsolute(repo) ||
+      typeof keyId !== "string" ||
+      !/^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_.-]{0,127}$/.test(keyId) ||
+      typeof output !== "string" ||
+      !isAbsolute(output) ||
+      typeof revision !== "string" ||
+      !/^[1-9][0-9]*$/.test(revision) ||
+      flags.get("--default-tenant") !== true
+    )
+      throw Error();
+    const canonicalRepo = await realpath(repo),
+      configPath = join(deps.home, "follow-up-delivery.json"),
+      loaded = await readHostDeliveryConfig(configPath);
+    if (!loaded.config.enabled) throw Error();
+    const params = {
+      repo_path: canonicalRepo,
+      default_tenant: true,
+      attempt_id: args[1],
+    };
+    const inspect = async () =>
+      FollowUpDeliveryResult.parse(await deps.rpc("follow-up-delivery.inspect", params)).attempt;
+    const attempt = await inspect(),
+      key = loaded.config.keys.find((k) => k.key_id === keyId);
+    if (
+      attempt.repo_path !== canonicalRepo ||
+      attempt.attempt_id !== args[1] ||
+      attempt.revision !== Number(revision) ||
+      attempt.state !== "prepared" ||
+      attempt.authorization_status !== "missing" ||
+      !key ||
+      key.repo_path !== canonicalRepo ||
+      !key.destination_refs.includes(attempt.envelope.destination.destination_ref) ||
+      deliveryCanonical(
+        loaded.config.targets.find((t) => t.destination_ref === attempt.envelope.destination.destination_ref),
+      ) !== deliveryCanonical(attempt.envelope.destination)
+    )
+      throw Error();
+    const { envelope_digest: envelopeDigest, ...envelopePayload } = attempt.envelope;
+    const hash = (value: string) => createHash("sha256").update(value).digest("hex");
+    const subject = {
+      domain: "osstack.follow-up-delivery.v1",
+      repo_path: canonicalRepo,
+      default_tenant: true,
+      attempt_id: attempt.attempt_id,
+      provider_id: attempt.envelope.destination.provider_id,
+      account_id: attempt.envelope.destination.account_id,
+      destination_digest: attempt.envelope.destination_digest,
+      body_sha256: attempt.envelope.body_sha256,
+      envelope_digest: envelopeDigest,
+    };
+    if (
+      attempt.envelope.attempt_id !== attempt.attempt_id ||
+      attempt.envelope.repo_path !== canonicalRepo ||
+      hash(attempt.envelope.body) !== attempt.envelope.body_sha256 ||
+      hash(deliveryCanonical(attempt.envelope.destination)) !== attempt.envelope.destination_digest ||
+      hash(deliveryCanonical(envelopePayload)) !== envelopeDigest ||
+      deliveryCanonical(subject) !== deliveryCanonical(attempt.authorization_subject)
+    )
+      throw Error();
+    const preview = serializeFollowUpDelivery({
+      action: "approve_one_public_github_comment",
+      key_id: keyId,
+      attempt_id: attempt.attempt_id,
+      revision: attempt.revision,
+      repo_path: canonicalRepo,
+      target: attempt.envelope.destination,
+      wire_body: githubDeliveryBody(attempt.envelope),
+      authorization_subject: attempt.authorization_subject,
+      max_calls: 1,
+      valid_for_ms: key.max_validity_ms,
+    });
+    const digest = createHash("sha256").update(preview).digest("hex");
+    if (!(await (deps.confirm ?? terminalConfirm)(`${preview}\n`, `APPROVE ${digest}`))) throw Error();
+    const fresh = await inspect(),
+      current = await readHostDeliveryConfig(configPath);
+    if (current.fingerprint !== loaded.fingerprint || deliveryCanonical(fresh) !== deliveryCanonical(attempt))
+      throw Error();
+    const privateKey = createPrivateKey(
+      await readPrivateDeliveryFile(join(deps.home, `issuer-${keyId}.pem`), 8192),
+    );
+    if (
+      privateKey.asymmetricKeyType !== "ed25519" ||
+      createPublicKey(privateKey)
+        .export({ type: "spki", format: "der" })
+        .compare(loaded.keys.get(keyId)!.export({ type: "spki", format: "der" })) !== 0
+    )
+      throw Error();
+    if ((await readHostDeliveryConfig(configPath)).fingerprint !== loaded.fingerprint) throw Error();
+    const now = Date.now();
+    const body = {
+      ...attempt.authorization_subject,
+      grant_id: randomUUID(),
+      issued_at_ms: now,
+      expires_at_ms: now + key.max_validity_ms,
+      max_calls: 1 as const,
+    };
+    const grant = SignedFollowUpDeliveryGrant.parse({
+      body,
+      key_id: keyId,
+      signature: sign(null, Buffer.from(deliveryCanonical(body)), privateKey).toString("base64"),
+    });
+    const directory = await lstat(dirname(output));
+    if (
+      (await realpath(dirname(output))) !== dirname(output) ||
+      !directory.isDirectory() ||
+      directory.uid !== process.getuid?.() ||
+      (directory.mode & 0o077) !== 0
+    )
+      throw Error();
+    const file = await open(
+      output,
+      constants.O_WRONLY | constants.O_CREAT | constants.O_EXCL | constants.O_NOFOLLOW,
+      0o600,
+    );
+    try {
+      await file.writeFile(`${JSON.stringify(grant)}\n`);
+      await file.sync();
+    } finally {
+      await file.close();
+    }
+    const parent = await open(dirname(output), constants.O_RDONLY);
+    try {
+      await parent.sync();
+    } finally {
+      await parent.close();
+    }
+    out(
+      `${serializeFollowUpDelivery({ outcome: "grant_created_not_authorized", attempt_id: attempt.attempt_id, preview_sha256: digest, expires_at_ms: body.expires_at_ms, output })}\n`,
+    );
+    return 0;
+  } catch {
+    err("delivery_issuer_refused\n");
+    return 1;
+  }
+}

+import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
+import { request as httpsRequest } from "node:https";
+import { request as httpRequest } from "node:http";
+import type { FollowUpDeliveryEnvelope, FollowUpDeliveryReceipt } from "@osstack/protocol";
+import type { FollowUpTransport } from "@osstack/core";
+
+const sha = (value: string) => createHash("sha256").update(value).digest("hex");
+/** Included in the human preview. Correlation is not itself proof: author, target and exact body must match. */
+export function githubDeliveryBody(envelope: FollowUpDeliveryEnvelope): string {
+  return `${envelope.body}\n\n<!-- osstack-delivery:${envelope.attempt_id}:${envelope.envelope_digest} -->`;
+}
+export interface GithubDeliveryTransportOptions {
+  qualification: FollowUpTransport["qualification"];
+  /** Host-owned resolver rechecks current registration and reads a private credential for each operation. */
+  credential(envelope: FollowUpDeliveryEnvelope): Promise<string>;
+  /** Host synchronous private credential fence; no I/O awaits after grant consumption. */
+  assertCredential?(envelope: FollowUpDeliveryEnvelope, token: string): void;
+  /** Only local HTTP fixtures are accepted, and only under test_only qualification. */
+  fixtureBaseUrl?: string;
+}
+interface JsonResponse {
+  value: unknown;
+  requestId: string;
+  hasNext: boolean;
+}
+/** No redirects, retry middleware, shell, proxy environment, or provider error bodies are used. */
+export function createGithubDeliveryTransport(options: GithubDeliveryTransportOptions): FollowUpTransport {
+  const base = options.fixtureBaseUrl ? new URL(options.fixtureBaseUrl) : new URL("https://api.github.com");
+  if (
+    options.fixtureBaseUrl &&
+    (options.qualification !== "test_only" ||
+      base.protocol !== "http:" ||
+      base.hostname !== "127.0.0.1" ||
+      base.username ||
+      base.password ||
+      base.pathname !== "/" ||
+      base.search ||
+      base.hash)
+  )
+    throw Error("invalid_delivery_fixture");
+  const api = async (
+    path: string,
+    token: string,
+    signal: AbortSignal,
+    body?: string,
+  ): Promise<JsonResponse> => {
+    if (signal.aborted || !/^[\x21-\x7e]{1,4096}$/.test(token)) throw Error("github_delivery_unavailable");
+    const url = new URL(path, base);
+    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
+      const fail = () => reject(Error("github_delivery_unconfirmed"));
+      const request = (base.protocol === "https:" ? httpsRequest : httpRequest)(
+        url,
+        {
+          method: body === undefined ? "GET" : "POST",
+          signal,
+          headers: {
+            authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
+            accept: "application/vnd.github+json",
+            "x-github-api-version": "2026-03-10",
+            "user-agent": "osstack-follow-up-delivery",
+            ...(body === undefined
+              ? {}
+              : {
+                  "content-type": "application/json",
+                  "content-length": Buffer.byteLength(body),
+                }),
+          },
+        },
+        (response) => {
+          const chunks: Buffer[] = [];
+          let bytes = 0;
+          response.on("data", (chunk: Buffer) => {
+            bytes += chunk.length;
+            if (bytes > 4_194_304) {
+              response.destroy();
+              request.destroy();
+              fail();
+            } else chunks.push(chunk);
+          });
+          response.on("error", fail);
+          response.on("end", () => {
+            try {
+              if (response.statusCode !== (body === undefined || path === "/graphql" ? 200 : 201))
+                throw Error();
+              const requestId = response.headers["x-github-request-id"];
+              if (typeof requestId !== "string" || !/^[\x21-\x7e]{1,256}$/.test(requestId)) throw Error();
+              resolve({
+                value: JSON.parse(new TextDecoder("utf-8", { fatal: true }).decode(Buffer.concat(chunks))),
+                requestId,
+                hasNext: /rel="next"/.test(String(response.headers.link ?? "")),
+              });
+            } catch {
+              fail();
+            }
+          });
+        },
+      );
+      request.on("error", fail);
+      request.setTimeout(5000, () => request.destroy(Error("github_delivery_timeout")));
+      request.end(body);
+    });
+  };
+  function record(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
+    if (!value || typeof value !== "object" || Array.isArray(value))
+      throw Error("github_delivery_invalid_response");
+    return value as Record<string, unknown>;
+  }
+  const targetPath = (e: FollowUpDeliveryEnvelope) =>
+    `/repos/${encodeURIComponent(e.destination.owner)}/${encodeURIComponent(e.destination.repository)}/issues/${e.destination.issue_number}`;
+  const targetUrl = (e: FollowUpDeliveryEnvelope) => `https://api.github.com${targetPath(e)}`;
+  async function preflight(
+    e: FollowUpDeliveryEnvelope,
+    signal: AbortSignal,
+    forSend = true,
+  ): Promise<string> {
+    if (
+      e.destination.host !== "github.com" ||
+      e.destination.provider_id !== "github_comment" ||
+      sha(e.body) !== e.body_sha256
+    )
+      throw Error("github_delivery_invalid_envelope");
+    const token = await options.credential(e);
+    const actor = record((await api("/user", token, signal)).value);
+    if (actor.node_id !== e.destination.actor_node_id) throw Error("github_delivery_actor_mismatch");
+    const issue = record((await api(targetPath(e), token, signal)).value);
+    if (
+      issue.node_id !== e.destination.target_node_id ||
+      issue.url !== targetUrl(e) ||
+      issue.number !== e.destination.issue_number ||
+      (forSend && issue.locked === true)
+    )
+      throw Error("github_delivery_target_mismatch");
+    return token;
+  }
+  function receipt(e: FollowUpDeliveryEnvelope, value: unknown, requestId: string): FollowUpDeliveryReceipt {
+    const comment = record(value),
+      user = record(comment.user);
+    if (
+      !Number.isSafeInteger(comment.id) ||
+      Number(comment.id) < 1 ||
+      comment.issue_url !== targetUrl(e) ||
+      user.node_id !== e.destination.actor_node_id ||
+      comment.body !== githubDeliveryBody(e)
+    )
+      throw Error("github_delivery_receipt_mismatch");
+    return {
+      provider_id: "github_comment",
+      account_id: e.destination.account_id,
+      target_node_id: e.destination.target_node_id,
+      author_node_id: e.destination.actor_node_id,
+      destination_digest: e.destination_digest,
+      body_sha256: e.body_sha256,
+      attempt_id: e.attempt_id,
+      provider_message_id: String(comment.id),
+      provider_request_id: requestId,
+    };
+  }
+  const tickets = new Map<string, { token: string; expiresAt: number }>();
+  let preflights = 0;
+  const ticketKey = (e: FollowUpDeliveryEnvelope) => `${e.attempt_id}:${e.envelope_digest}`;
+  return {
+    qualification: options.qualification,
+    automatic_retries: false,
+    lookup_receipt: true,
+    async preflight(e, signal) {
+      for (const [key, ticket] of tickets) if (ticket.expiresAt <= performance.now()) tickets.delete(key);
+      if (tickets.size + preflights >= 64) throw Error("github_delivery_busy");
+      preflights++;
+      try {
+        const token = await preflight(e, signal);
+        if (signal.aborted) throw Error("github_delivery_cancelled");
+        tickets.set(ticketKey(e), { token, expiresAt: performance.now() + 30000 });
+      } finally {
+        preflights--;
+      }
+    },
+    assertReady(e) {
+      const ticket = tickets.get(ticketKey(e));
+      if (!ticket || ticket.expiresAt <= performance.now()) {
+        tickets.delete(ticketKey(e));
+        throw Error("github_delivery_preflight_required");
+      }
+      options.assertCredential?.(e, ticket.token);
+    },
+    async sendOnce(e, signal) {
+      const ticket = tickets.get(ticketKey(e));
+      tickets.delete(ticketKey(e));
+      if (!ticket) throw Error("github_delivery_preflight_required");
+      const token = ticket.token;
+      // Readiness, credential and configuration fences ran synchronously before consume.
+      // No awaited work precedes the sole POST.
+      // Once durably started, all exceptions remain unknown; never infer non-delivery from HTTP errors.
+      const response = await api(
+        "/graphql",
+        token,
+        signal,
+        JSON.stringify({
+          query:
+            "mutation OSStackFollowUp($input:AddCommentInput!){addComment(input:$input){clientMutationId subject{id} commentEdge{node{databaseId body author{... on User{id} ... on Bot{id}}}}}}",
+          variables: {
+            input: {
+              subjectId: e.destination.target_node_id,
+              body: githubDeliveryBody(e),
+              clientMutationId: e.attempt_id,
+            },
+          },
+        }),
+      );
+      const result = record(response.value);
+      if (result.errors !== undefined && (!Array.isArray(result.errors) || result.errors.length))
+        throw Error("github_delivery_graphql_error");
+      const payload = record(record(result.data).addComment),
+        subject = record(payload.subject),
+        comment = record(record(payload.commentEdge).node),
+        author = record(comment.author);
+      if (payload.clientMutationId !== e.attempt_id || subject.id !== e.destination.target_node_id)
+        throw Error("github_delivery_receipt_mismatch");
+      // The mutation is addressed by immutable subject ID, not a mutable owner/repository URL.
+      return receipt(
+        e,
+        { id: comment.databaseId, body: comment.body, user: { node_id: author.id }, issue_url: targetUrl(e) },
+        response.requestId,
+      );
+    },
+    async lookup(e, signal, context) {
+      const token = await preflight(e, signal, false);
+      let found: FollowUpDeliveryReceipt | undefined;
+      // Old busy threads do not hide a new attempt behind historical pages. Context comes from the durable ledger.
+      const since =
+        context && Number.isSafeInteger(context.createdAtMs) && context.createdAtMs >= 0
+          ? `&since=${encodeURIComponent(new Date(Math.max(0, context.createdAtMs - 300000)).toISOString())}`
+          : "";
+      // Fixed bounded pages, never follow a provider-supplied URL. Incomplete enumeration remains unknown.
+      for (let page = 1; page <= 20; page++) {
+        const response = await api(
+          `${targetPath(e)}/comments?per_page=10&page=${page}${since}`,
+          token,
+          signal,
+        );
+        if (!Array.isArray(response.value) || response.value.length > 10)
+          throw Error("github_delivery_invalid_list");
+        for (const raw of response.value) {
+          const comment = record(raw);
+          // A public marker copied by another author is not a candidate receipt and cannot veto one.
+          if (
+            !comment.user ||
+            typeof comment.user !== "object" ||
+            Array.isArray(comment.user) ||
+            (comment.user as Record<string, unknown>).node_id !== e.destination.actor_node_id ||
+            comment.issue_url !== targetUrl(e)
+          )
+            continue;
+          if (typeof comment.body !== "string" || !comment.body.includes(`osstack-delivery:${e.attempt_id}:`))
+            continue;
+          const match = receipt(e, raw, response.requestId);
+          if (found) throw Error("github_delivery_duplicate_correlation");
+          found = match;
+        }
+        if (!response.hasNext && response.value.length < 10) {
+          if ((await options.credential(e)) !== token || signal.aborted)
+            throw Error("github_delivery_registration_changed");
+          return found ?? null;
+        }
+      }
+      throw Error("github_delivery_lookup_incomplete");
+    },
+  };
+}

diff --git a/packages/cli/src/graph-check-suite.ts b/packages/cli/src/graph-check-suite.ts
new file mode 100644

--- /dev/null
+++ b/packages/cli/src/graph-check-suite.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,40 @@
+import type { CommandResult } from '@osstack/loop';
+import type { AcceptanceCommandSpec, GraphVerificationEnvelope } from './graph-verification-store.js';
+
+/** Host evidence contains one ordered observation per admitted command. A stopped
+ * prefix is retained only for infrastructure failures and can never pass. */
+export function validateGraphCommandSuite(
+  specs: readonly AcceptanceCommandSpec[], results: readonly CommandResult[], failed: boolean,
+): void {
+  if (specs.length < 2 || specs.length > 16 || results.length > specs.length
+    || (!failed && results.length !== specs.length)
+    || new Set(specs.map(spec => spec.ref)).size !== specs.length) {
+    throw Error('invalid command suite cardinality');
+  }
+  for (let index = 0; index < results.length; index++) {
+    const result = results[index]!;
+    const spec = specs[index]!;
+    if (result.command !== spec.command || !Number.isInteger(result.exitCode)
+      || typeof result.stdout !== 'string' || typeof result.stderr !== 'string'
+      || !Number.isFinite(result.durationMs) || result.durationMs < 0
+      || result.termination?.stop_confirmed !== true || !result.termination.evidence.trim()
+      || result.failure_code === 'process_exit_unconfirmed' || result.failure_code === 'surviving_descendants') {
+      throw Error('invalid suite command evidence');
+    }
+    if (!failed && (result.failure_code || result.cancellation
+      || result.termination.scope !== 'posix_process_group' || result.termination.trigger !== 'exit'
+      || result.termination.leader_exit_code !== result.exitCode
+      || ![...spec.passExitCodes, ...spec.failExitCodes].includes(result.exitCode))) {
+      throw Error('invalid suite acceptance outcome');
+    }
+  }
+}
+
+export function graphChecksPassed(envelope: GraphVerificationEnvelope): boolean {
+  if (envelope.acceptanceSuite) {
+    const results = envelope.evidence.commandSuite ?? envelope.evidence.containedSuite?.map(item => item.result);
+    return !!results && results.length === envelope.acceptanceSuite.length
+      && results.every((result, index) => envelope.acceptanceSuite![index]!.passExitCodes.includes(result.exitCode));
+  }
+  return envelope.acceptance.passExitCodes.includes(envelope.evidence.contained?.result.exitCode ?? envelope.evidence.command!.exitCode);
+}

+/** Ordered bounded host suite; commands are independently registered, never shell-concatenated. */
+export function resolveGraphContractChecks(frozen: FrozenDeliveryContractV2, registry: readonly AcceptanceCommandSpec[], primaryRef: string): AcceptanceCommandSpec[] {
+  const contract=assertFrozenDeliveryContract(frozen).contract;
+  if(contract.required_checks.length>16)throw Error('GRAPH_CONTRACT_CHECK_LIMIT');
+  const selected=contract.required_checks.map(required=>{
+    const matches=registry.filter(item=>deliveryCheckReference(item.command)===required.registry_ref);
+    if(matches.length!==1)throw Error('GRAPH_CONTRACT_REGISTRY_MISMATCH');
+    const check=acceptanceSchema.parse(matches[0]);
+    if(check.passExitCodes.length!==1||check.passExitCodes[0]!==0||check.failExitCodes.includes(0))throw Error('GRAPH_CONTRACT_REGISTRY_MISMATCH');
+    return check;
+  });
+  const primary=selected.filter(item=>item.ref===primaryRef);
+  if(primary.length!==1||new Set(selected.map(item=>item.ref)).size!==selected.length||selected.reduce((sum,item)=>sum+Buffer.byteLength(item.command),0)>16384)throw Error('GRAPH_CONTRACT_REGISTRY_MISMATCH');
+  return [primary[0]!,...selected.filter(item=>item!==primary[0])];
+}
+
 export function graphContractInputRef(envelope: GraphContractHostEnvelope): string {
   return `graph-contract-input:${deliveryContractDigest(envelopeSchema.parse(envelope))}`;
 }

+/** Suite collections are local files, not IPC. Validate every leaf with the
+ * unchanged wire validator without imposing its aggregate limit on the file. */
+function localVerificationObject(value: unknown, keys: readonly string[]): Record<string, unknown> {
+  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value)
+    || ![Object.prototype, null].includes(Object.getPrototypeOf(value))) throw Error('invalid local evidence object');
+  for (const key of Reflect.ownKeys(value)) {
+    if (typeof key !== 'string' || !keys.includes(key)) throw Error('unexpected local evidence field');
+    const descriptor = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(value, key)!;
+    if (!descriptor.enumerable || !('value' in descriptor)) throw Error('invalid local evidence property');
+    const item: unknown = descriptor.value;
+    if (['acceptanceSuite', 'commandSuite', 'containedSuite'].includes(key)) {
+      if (!Array.isArray(item) || Object.getPrototypeOf(item) !== Array.prototype || item.length > 16
+        || Reflect.ownKeys(item).length !== item.length + 1) throw Error('invalid local evidence collection');
+      for (let index = 0; index < item.length; index++) {
+        const entry = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(item, String(index));
+        if (!entry?.enumerable || !('value' in entry)) throw Error('invalid local evidence entry');
+        GraphJsonValue.parse(entry.value);
+      }
+    } else if (key === 'evidence') {
+      localVerificationObject(item, ['status', 'stopped', 'candidate', 'command', 'contained', 'commandSuite', 'containedSuite']);
+    } else GraphJsonValue.parse(item);
+  }
+  return value as Record<string, unknown>;
+}

+// A suite is local host storage, never a worker wire payload. Preserve the
+// 64KiB wire/single-check budget; independently bound each entry and cap the
+// complete local suite at 256KiB so mandatory stop receipts are never truncated.
+function assertEvidenceBudget(envelope: GraphVerificationEnvelope, bytes: string | Buffer): void {
+  const limit = envelope.acceptanceSuite ? 262144 : 65536;
+  if (Buffer.byteLength(bytes) > limit) throw Error('evidence exceeds local file size limit');
+  if (!envelope.acceptanceSuite) { GraphJsonValue.parse(envelope); return; }
+  const { acceptanceSuite, evidence, ...rest } = envelope;
+  const { commandSuite, containedSuite, ...primary } = evidence;
+  GraphJsonValue.parse({ ...rest, evidence: primary });
+  for (const item of [...acceptanceSuite, ...(commandSuite ?? []), ...(containedSuite ?? [])]) GraphJsonValue.parse(item);
+}

+import { closeSync, constants, existsSync, fstatSync, lstatSync, openSync, readSync, realpathSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { isAbsolute, join, resolve } from 'node:path';
+/** Small synchronous pre-DB guard. Full release/runtime evidence is verified lazily by daemon startup. */
+function privateJson(path:string,limit:number):Record<string,unknown>{
+  const fd=openSync(path,constants.O_RDONLY|constants.O_NOFOLLOW|constants.O_NONBLOCK);
+  try{const st=fstatSync(fd);if(!st.isFile()||st.nlink!==1||st.size>limit||(st.mode&0o077)||process.getuid&&st.uid!==process.getuid())throw Error('upgrade_managed_marker_invalid');const bytes=Buffer.alloc(st.size+1);let length=0;while(length<bytes.length){const n=readSync(fd,bytes,length,bytes.length-length,length);if(!n)break;length+=n;}const after=fstatSync(fd);if(length!==st.size||st.mtimeMs!==after.mtimeMs||st.ctimeMs!==after.ctimeMs)throw Error('upgrade_managed_marker_changed');const value:unknown=JSON.parse(bytes.subarray(0,length).toString());if(!value||typeof value!=='object'||Array.isArray(value))throw Error('upgrade_managed_marker_invalid');return value as Record<string,unknown>;}finally{closeSync(fd);}
+}
+export function assertManagedHomeMarker(home:string,entry:string|undefined=process.argv[1]):void{
+  const markerPath=join(home,'managed-consumer.json');if(!existsSync(markerPath))return;
+  const marker=privateJson(markerPath,8192);if(marker['schema_version']!==1||typeof marker['root']!=='string'||!isAbsolute(marker['root'])||typeof marker['home_id']!=='string'||!/^home_[a-f0-9]{32}$/.test(marker['home_id']))throw Error('upgrade_managed_marker_invalid');
+  const root=marker['root'];const directory=lstatSync(root);if(!directory.isDirectory()||realpathSync(root)!==resolve(root)||(directory.mode&0o077)||process.getuid&&directory.uid!==process.getuid())throw Error('upgrade_managed_root_invalid');
+  const config=privateJson(join(root,'installation.json'),262144);const relative=config['entry'];if(config['root']!==root||typeof relative!=='string'||relative.startsWith('/')||relative.split('/').some(p=>!p||p==='.'||p==='..')||!Array.isArray(config['consumers']))throw Error('upgrade_managed_registry_invalid');
+  const consumer=(config['consumers'] as Array<Record<string,unknown>>).find(c=>c&&c['home_id']===marker['home_id']&&c['home']===realpathSync(home));if(!consumer||!entry||realpathSync(entry)!==realpathSync(join(root,'current',relative)))throw Error('upgrade_managed_entry_required');
+  const current=realpathSync(join(root,'current'));if(!/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(current.slice((join(root,'releases')+'/').length))||!current.startsWith(join(root,'releases')+'/'))throw Error('upgrade_managed_release_invalid');
+  if(existsSync(join(root,'upgrade-operation.json'))){const operation=privateJson(join(root,'upgrade-operation.json'),8192),permit=privateJson(join(root,'restart-permit.json'),8192);if(typeof operation['plan_digest']!=='string'||permit['plan_digest']!==operation['plan_digest']||permit['build_digest']!==current.split('/').at(-1))throw Error('upgrade_start_blocked');}

+  usage: z.object({ input_tokens: z.number().int().nonnegative().max(1000000), output_tokens: z.number().int().nonnegative().max(1000000), cache_creation_input_tokens: z.number().int().nonnegative().max(1000000), cache_read_input_tokens: z.number().int().nonnegative().max(1000000) }).strict(),
+}).strict();
+export interface PairedRoutingProvider { invoke(prompt: string, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<unknown> }
+export interface PairedRoutingResult {
+  case_id: string; repetition: number; model_id: string; prompt_sha256: string;
+  status: "completed" | "incomplete"; correct: boolean | null; category: z.infer<typeof label> | null;
+  requested_model: string; reported_model: string | null; input_tokens: number | null; output_tokens: number | null;
+  api_reported_model: string | null; policy_fingerprint: string | null; process_exit_confirmed: boolean; cache_creation_input_tokens: number | null; cache_read_input_tokens: number | null; estimated_cost_usd: number | null; duration_ms: number; error: string | null;
+}
+
+/** Shadow comparison only. Labels and training digests never enter provider prompts.
+ * Caller must explicitly supply two models and their price source; no default is changed. */
+export async function evaluatePairedRouting(raw: unknown, createProvider: (model: string, perCallBudget: number) => PairedRoutingProvider,
+  externalSignal?: AbortSignal) {
+  const input = PairedRoutingInputSchema.parse(raw);
+  if (input.models[0].id === input.models[1].id) throw Error("distinct_models_required");
+  if (new Set(input.cases.map(row => row.id)).size !== input.cases.length) throw Error("duplicate_case");
+  if (new Set(input.cases.map(row => hash(row.excerpt))).size !== input.cases.length) throw Error("duplicate_prompt");
+  const train = new Set(input.training_prompt_sha256);
+  if (input.cases.some(row => train.has(hash(row.excerpt)) || train.has(hash(JSON.stringify({ excerpt: row.excerpt }))))) throw Error("holdout_overlap");
+  if (input.cases.some(row => Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify({ excerpt: row.excerpt })) > 6500)) throw Error("prompt_too_large");
+  const maxCalls = input.cases.length * input.repetitions * 2;
+  if (maxCalls > 256 || maxCalls * input.per_call_budget_usd > input.total_budget_usd + 1e-12) throw Error("evaluation_budget_insufficient");
+  const providers = input.models.map(row => createProvider(row.id, input.per_call_budget_usd));
+  const results: PairedRoutingResult[] = []; const errors: string[] = []; let stop = false;
+  let reserved = 0; const overallStarted = performance.now();
+  for (const [caseIndex, item] of input.cases.entries()) {
+    for (let repetition = 0; repetition < input.repetitions && !stop; repetition++) {
+      const order = (parseInt(hash(input.seed).slice(0, 2), 16) + caseIndex * input.repetitions + repetition) % 2 === 0 ? [0, 1] : [1, 0];
+      for (const index of order) {
+        if (performance.now() - overallStarted > 600000) { stop = true; errors.push("aggregate_deadline_exceeded"); break; }
+        if (externalSignal?.aborted) { stop = true; errors.push("cancelled"); break; }
+        const chosen = input.models[index]!; const provider = providers[index]!;
+        // Neither case id nor answer is sent; pair order is stable, counterbalanced and recorded.
+        const prompt = JSON.stringify({ excerpt: item.excerpt }); const controller = new AbortController();
+        const cancel = () => controller.abort(); externalSignal?.addEventListener("abort", cancel, { once: true });
+        const started = performance.now(); let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
+        let timedOut = false; let abortListener: (() => void) | undefined;
+        const result: PairedRoutingResult = { case_id: item.id, repetition, model_id: chosen.id, prompt_sha256: hash(prompt),
+          status: "incomplete", correct: null, category: null, requested_model: chosen.id, reported_model: null,
+          input_tokens: null, output_tokens: null, api_reported_model: null, policy_fingerprint: null, process_exit_confirmed: false, cache_creation_input_tokens: null, cache_read_input_tokens: null, estimated_cost_usd: null, duration_ms: 0, error: null };
+        reserved += input.per_call_budget_usd;
+        let invocationSettled = false;
+        const invocation = Promise.resolve().then(() => provider.invoke(prompt, controller.signal));
+        invocation.then(() => { invocationSettled = true; }, () => { invocationSettled = true; });
+        try {
+          const value = await Promise.race([invocation,
+            new Promise<never>((_resolve, reject) => {
+              abortListener = () => reject(Error(timedOut ? "deadline_exceeded" : "cancelled"));
+              controller.signal.addEventListener("abort", abortListener, { once: true });
+              timer = setTimeout(() => { timedOut = true; controller.abort(); }, input.timeout_ms);
+              if (controller.signal.aborted) abortListener();
+            })]);
+          if (value === null || typeof value !== "object" || !("process_exit_confirmed" in value) || value.process_exit_confirmed !== true)
+            throw Error("provider_stop_unconfirmed");
+          const observed = receipt.parse(value);
+          result.reported_model = observed.reported_model;
+          result.api_reported_model = observed.api_reported_model; result.policy_fingerprint = observed.policy_fingerprint; result.process_exit_confirmed = observed.process_exit_confirmed;
+          result.cache_creation_input_tokens = observed.usage.cache_creation_input_tokens; result.cache_read_input_tokens = observed.usage.cache_read_input_tokens;
+          result.input_tokens = observed.usage.input_tokens; result.output_tokens = observed.usage.output_tokens;
+          result.estimated_cost_usd = (observed.usage.input_tokens * chosen.input_usd_per_million + observed.usage.output_tokens * chosen.output_usd_per_million + observed.usage.cache_creation_input_tokens * chosen.cache_creation_usd_per_million + observed.usage.cache_read_input_tokens * chosen.cache_read_usd_per_million) / 1000000;
+          if (observed.requested_model !== chosen.id || observed.reported_model !== chosen.id || observed.api_reported_model !== chosen.id) throw Error("model_identity_mismatch");
+          if (result.estimated_cost_usd > input.per_call_budget_usd + 1e-12) { stop = true; throw Error("estimated_call_budget_exceeded"); }
+          const parsed = answer.parse(JSON.parse(observed.text));
+          result.category = parsed.category; result.correct = parsed.category === item.expected; result.status = "completed";
+        } catch (error) {
+          const known = ["model_identity_mismatch", "estimated_call_budget_exceeded", "deadline_exceeded", "cancelled", "not_authenticated", "unsupported_auth_routing", "provider_stop_unconfirmed", "runtime_spawn_unverified", "runtime_sdk_version_mismatch", "runtime_policy_mismatch", "runtime_policy_changed", "runtime_init_unverified", "runtime_spawn_mismatch", "unexpected_runtime_activity", "non_text_activity"];
+          result.error = error instanceof Error && known.includes(error.message) ? error.message : "provider_or_output_invalid";
+          // Any provider failure ends this qualification attempt. Never spend more
+          // against an unverified runtime or compare an easier surviving subset.
+          stop = true; errors.push(timedOut ? "evaluation_stopped_after_timeout" : result.error);
+        } finally {
+          if (timer) clearTimeout(timer); if (abortListener) controller.signal.removeEventListener("abort", abortListener);
+          externalSignal?.removeEventListener("abort", cancel); controller.abort();
+          // cli-entry exits explicitly after main returns. Give the controlled
+          // runtime's 250ms SIGKILL escalation and 1s exit observation time to run.
+          if (!invocationSettled) {
+            let drainTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
+            await Promise.race([invocation.catch(() => undefined), new Promise<void>(resolve => { drainTimer = setTimeout(resolve, 1500); })]);
+            if (drainTimer) clearTimeout(drainTimer);
+          }
+          result.duration_ms = performance.now() - started;
+        }
+        results.push(result); if (stop) break;
+      }
+    }
+    if (stop) break;
+  }
+  const differences: number[] = [];
+  for (const item of input.cases) {
+    const rows = results.filter(row => row.case_id === item.id);
+    if (rows.length !== input.repetitions * 2 || rows.some(row => row.status !== "completed")) continue;
+    const scores = input.models.map(m => rows.filter(r => r.model_id === m.id && r.correct).length / input.repetitions);
+    differences.push(scores[0]! - scores[1]!);
+  }
+  const allCompleted = results.length === maxCalls && results.every(row => row.status === "completed");
+  const average = allCompleted && differences.length ? differences.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / differences.length : null;
+  const radius = allCompleted && differences.length ? Math.sqrt(2 * Math.log(40) / differences.length) : null;
+  return {
+    schema_version: 1 as const, mode: "paired_holdout" as const, dataset_id: input.dataset_id, dataset_sha256: hash(JSON.stringify(input)), seed: input.seed,
+    status: (allCompleted ? "completed" : "incomplete") as "completed" | "incomplete",
+    qualification: "observed_no_tools_classification_only" as const, default_route_changed: false as const,
+    model_identity_evidence: "requested_and_runtime_reported_not_weights_attestation" as const,
+    holdout_evidence: "operator_declared_training_hashes_checked_no_independent_label_review" as const,
+    planned_calls: maxCalls, attempted_calls: results.length, reserved_budget_usd: reserved, pricing_source: input.pricing_source,
+    cost_basis: "usage_times_operator_supplied_prices_not_invoice" as const,
+    paired_cases: differences.length, comparison_available: allCompleted, paired_accuracy_difference: average,
+    paired_difference_interval_95: average === null || radius === null ? null : [Math.max(-1, average - radius), Math.min(1, average + radius)],
+    interval_method: "Hoeffding_bounded_paired_case_means_assumes_independent_cases_repeats_grouped" as const,
+    models: input.models.map(m => {
+      const rows = results.filter(row => row.model_id === m.id); const complete = rows.filter(row => row.status === "completed");
+      return { model_id: m.id, reserved_cost_usd: rows.length * input.per_call_budget_usd, unknown_usage_calls: rows.filter(row => row.input_tokens === null).length, completed: complete.length, incomplete: rows.length - complete.length,
+        accuracy: complete.length === input.cases.length * input.repetitions ? complete.filter(row => row.correct).length / complete.length : null,
+        estimated_cost_usd: rows.some(row => row.estimated_cost_usd === null) ? null : rows.reduce((sum, row) => sum + row.estimated_cost_usd!, 0),
+        observed_input_tokens: rows.reduce((sum, row) => sum + (row.input_tokens ?? 0), 0), observed_output_tokens: rows.reduce((sum, row) => sum + (row.output_tokens ?? 0), 0) };
+    }), results, errors,
+  };
+}
+
+/** Production entrypoint: existing controlled runtime enforces no tools, one turn,
+ * exact requested/runtime model, isolated settings, a spend cap and physical stop. */
+export async function runControlledPairedRouting(raw: unknown) {
+  const { createScratchDir } = await import("@osstack/core");
+  const { createControlledTriageProvider } = await import("@osstack/agents");
+  const { join } = await import("node:path");
+  const { rm } = await import("node:fs/promises");
+  let directory: string | undefined;
+  const runtimeRoot = () => directory === undefined
+    ? createScratchDir("runtime", "paired-routing").then(path => { directory = path; return path; })
+    : Promise.resolve(directory);
+  const controller = new AbortController(); const cancel = () => controller.abort();
+  // Repeated termination requests must not remove the handler during physical drain.
+  process.on("SIGINT", cancel); process.on("SIGTERM", cancel);
+  let report: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof evaluatePairedRouting>>;
+  try { report = await evaluatePairedRouting(raw, (modelId, budget) => {
+    let provider: ReturnType<typeof createControlledTriageProvider> | undefined;
+    return { async invoke(prompt, signal) {
+      const root = await runtimeRoot();
+      provider ??= createControlledTriageProvider({ state_directory: join(root, hash(modelId)), model_id: modelId, max_cost_usd: budget, detailed_receipt: true });
+      return provider.invoke(prompt, signal);
+    } };
+  }, controller.signal); }
+  finally { process.removeListener("SIGINT", cancel); process.removeListener("SIGTERM", cancel); }
+  // Deadline is logical, not proof that the child exited; retain only those scratch
+  // runtimes. Never remove a potentially live process's private directory.
+  if (directory !== undefined && report.results.every(row => row.process_exit_confirmed))
+    await rm(directory, { recursive: true, force: true });
+  return report;
+}

+import { randomBytes } from "node:crypto";
+import { spawn, type ChildProcessWithoutNullStreams } from "node:child_process";
+import { rm, writeFile } from "node:fs/promises";
+import { join } from "node:path";
+import { deliveryCanonical } from "@osstack/core";
+import {
+  RemoteChannel,
+  type RemoteHomeEntry,
+  RemoteClientRegistry,
+  assertPrivateRemoteFile,
+  readPinnedRemoteRegistry,
+} from "./remote-home-protocol.js";
+import { buildRemoteSshArgs } from "./remote-home-reader.js";

+  SignedRemoteCancelGrant,
+  RemoteCancelHandshake,
+  RemoteCancelReceipt,
+  remoteCancelDigest,
+  initializeRemoteJournal,
+  writeRemoteJournal,
+  readRemoteJournal,
+} from "./remote-cancel-protocol.js";
+export function buildRemoteControlSshArgs(entry: RemoteHomeEntry, known: string): string[] {
+  const args = buildRemoteSshArgs(entry, known);
+  args[args.length - 1] = "osstack remote-control --stdio";
+  return args;
+}
+export interface RemoteCancelClientDependencies {
+  home: string;
+  spawn?: typeof spawn;
+  timeoutMs?: number;
+  stdout?: (text: string) => void;
+  stderr?: (text: string) => void;
+}
+export const REMOTE_CANCEL_HELP = `remote-home cancel|query --registry FILE --sha256 HASH --alias NAME --grant FILE\nSigned exact Graph cancellation only. Local durable attempt precedes transport; repeat cancel becomes query, never resend.\nRemote stop_requested is not physical stopped proof. Missing/offline receipts remain unknown.\n`;
+export async function handleRemoteCancelCommand(
+  args: string[],
+  deps: RemoteCancelClientDependencies,
+): Promise<number> {
+  const out =
+      deps.stdout ??
+      ((text) => {
+        process.stdout.write(text);
+      }),
+    err =
+      deps.stderr ??
+      ((text) => {
+        process.stderr.write(text);
+      });
+  if (
+    (args.length === 1 && args[0] === "--help") ||
+    (args.length === 2 && ["cancel", "query"].includes(args[0]!) && args[1] === "--help")
+  ) {
+    out(REMOTE_CANCEL_HELP);
+    return 0;
+  }
+  let child: ChildProcessWithoutNullStreams | undefined,
+    channel: RemoteChannel | undefined,
+    dir: string | undefined,
+    timer: NodeJS.Timeout | undefined;
+  let unknown: Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
+  try {
+    if (
+      !["cancel", "query"].includes(args[0]!) ||
+      args.length !== 9 ||
+      args.some((a) => Buffer.byteLength(a) > 4096)
+    )
+      throw Error();
+    const flags = new Map<string, string>();
+    for (let i = 1; i < args.length; i += 2) {
+      const key = args[i]!,
+        value = args[i + 1];
+      if (!["--registry", "--sha256", "--alias", "--grant"].includes(key) || flags.has(key) || !value)
+        throw Error();
+      flags.set(key, value);
+    }
+    const registry = RemoteClientRegistry.parse(
+      await readPinnedRemoteRegistry(flags.get("--registry") ?? "", flags.get("--sha256") ?? ""),
+    );
+    const entry = registry.homes.find((e) => e.alias === flags.get("--alias"));
+    if (!entry) throw Error();
+    const grant = SignedRemoteCancelGrant.parse(
+        JSON.parse((await readPrivateDeliveryFile(flags.get("--grant") ?? "", 32768)).toString("utf8")),
+      ),
+      b = grant.body,
+      digest = remoteCancelDigest(grant);
+    if (
+      b.home_id !== entry.home_id ||
+      b.server_registry_sha256 !== entry.registry_sha256 ||
+      b.scope_alias !== entry.scope
+    )
+      throw Error();
+    const journal = await initializeRemoteJournal(deps.home),
+      recordPath = join(journal, `${b.request_id}.local.json`);
+    const record = {
+      alias: entry.alias,
+      endpoint_sha256: remoteCancelDigest(entry),
+      request_sha256: digest,
+      grant,
+    };
+    const previous = await readRemoteJournal(recordPath);
+    if (previous && deliveryCanonical(previous) !== deliveryCanonical(record)) throw Error();
+    let action = args[0] as "cancel" | "query";
+    if (action === "cancel") {
+      if (previous) action = "query";
+      else if (!(await writeRemoteJournal(recordPath, record))) {
+        if (deliveryCanonical(await readRemoteJournal(recordPath)) !== deliveryCanonical(record))
+          throw Error();
+        action = "query";
+      }
+    }
+    unknown = {
+      schema_version: 1,
+      mode: "remote_graph_cancel",
+      alias: entry.alias,
+      request_id: b.request_id,
+      request_sha256: digest,
+      outcome: "delivery_unknown",
+      physical_stop: "not_attested",
+      qualification: "remote_host_pending",
+    };
+    await assertPrivateRemoteFile(entry.identity_file);
+    const { createScratchDir } = await import("@osstack/core");
+    dir = await createScratchDir("runtime", "remote-cancel");
+    const known = join(dir, "known_hosts");
+    await writeFile(
+      known,
+      `${entry.port === 22 ? entry.host : `[${entry.host}]:${entry.port}`} ${entry.host_key}\n`,
+      { mode: 0o600, flag: "wx" },
+    );
+    child = (deps.spawn ?? spawn)("/usr/bin/ssh", buildRemoteControlSshArgs(entry, known), {
+      stdio: ["pipe", "pipe", "pipe"],
+      shell: false,
+      env: { PATH: "/usr/bin:/bin", LANG: "C", LC_ALL: "C" },
+    }) as ChildProcessWithoutNullStreams;
+    channel = new RemoteChannel(child.stdout, child.stdin);
+    let stderrBytes = 0;
+    child.stderr.on("error", () => {
+      child?.stdin.destroy();
+      child?.stdout.destroy();
+    });
+    child.stderr.on("data", (chunk: Buffer) => {
+      stderrBytes += chunk.length;
+      if (stderrBytes > 8192) child?.kill("SIGKILL");
+    });
+    child.on("error", () => {
+      child?.stdin.destroy();
+      child?.stdout.destroy();
+    });
+    const active = channel,
+      nonce = randomBytes(32).toString("hex"),
+      timeout = Math.max(1, Math.min(10000, deps.timeoutMs ?? 10000));
+    const exchange = async () => {
+      await active.send({
+        type: "control_hello",
+        protocol: 1,
+        nonce,
+        home_id: entry.home_id,
+        registry_sha256: entry.registry_sha256,
+      });
+      const handshake = RemoteCancelHandshake.parse(await active.receive(timeout));
+      if (
+        handshake.nonce !== nonce ||
+        handshake.home_id !== entry.home_id ||
+        handshake.registry_sha256 !== entry.registry_sha256
+      )
+        throw Error();
+      await active.send({ type: action, token: handshake.token, grant });
+      const receipt = RemoteCancelReceipt.parse(await active.receive(timeout));
+      if (
+        receipt.nonce !== nonce ||
+        receipt.home_id !== b.home_id ||
+        receipt.request_id !== b.request_id ||
+        receipt.request_sha256 !== digest ||
+        receipt.daemon_generation !== b.daemon_generation ||
+        receipt.graph_generation !== b.graph_generation ||
+        receipt.graph_run_id !== b.graph_run_id ||
+        (receipt.outcome === "stop_requested" &&
+          (receipt.observed_revision === null || receipt.observed_revision <= b.expected_revision))
+      )
+        throw Error();
+      await writeRemoteJournal(
+        join(journal, `${b.request_id}.observed-${remoteCancelDigest({ ...receipt, nonce: "" })}.json`),
+        receipt,
+      );
+      return receipt;
+    };
+    const receipt = await Promise.race([
+      exchange(),
+      new Promise<never>((_, reject) => {
+        timer = setTimeout(() => reject(Error("remote_cancel_timeout")), timeout);
+      }),
+    ]);
+    out(`${JSON.stringify({ ...unknown, outcome: receipt.outcome, receipt })}\n`);
+    return receipt.outcome === "stop_requested" ? 0 : 1;
+  } catch {
+    if (unknown) out(`${JSON.stringify(unknown)}\n`);
+    else err("invalid_remote_cancel_request\n");
+    return 1;
+  } finally {
+    if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
+    channel?.close();
+    if (child) {
+      child.stdin.destroy();
+      child.stdout.destroy();
+      child.stderr.destroy();
+      if (child.exitCode === null) child.kill("SIGKILL");
+    }
+    if (dir) await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  }
+}

+import { randomBytes } from "node:crypto";

+  RemoteCancelServerRegistry,
+  RemoteCancelHello,
+  RemoteCancelRequest,
