+async function registry(path: string, digest: string): Promise<RemoteCancelServerRegistry> {
+  if (!/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(digest)) throw Error("remote_cancel_registry_invalid");
+  const raw = await readPrivateDeliveryFile(path, 32768);
+  if (remoteHash(raw) !== digest) throw Error("remote_cancel_registry_changed");
+  const value = RemoteCancelServerRegistry.parse(JSON.parse(raw.toString("utf8")));
+  await assertRemoteJournal(value.journal_dir);
+  for (const scope of value.scopes) {
+    for (const protectedPath of [path, value.journal_dir, value.socket]) {
+      const rel = relative(scope.repo_path, protectedPath);
+      if (rel === "" || (!isAbsolute(rel) && rel !== ".." && !rel.startsWith(`..${sep}`)))
+        throw Error("remote_cancel_registry_in_repository");
+    }
+    if (
+      (await realpath(scope.repo_path)) !== scope.repo_path ||
+      (await lstat(scope.repo_path)).isDirectory() !== true
+    )
+      throw Error("remote_cancel_scope_unavailable");
+  }
+  return value;
+}
+/** One connected existing daemon: all identity/status/cancel frames stay bound to that same process. */
+async function graphConnection(socketPath: string) {
+  await assertRemoteJournal(dirname(socketPath));
+  if ((await realpath(socketPath)) !== socketPath) throw Error("remote_cancel_endpoint_invalid");
+  const before = await lstat(socketPath);
+  if (!before.isSocket() || before.uid !== process.getuid?.() || (before.mode & 0o077) !== 0)
+    throw Error("remote_cancel_endpoint_invalid");
+  const socket = connect(socketPath),
+    channel = new RemoteChannel(socket, socket, 65536 + 1024, 8);
+  let id = 0;
+  return {
+    async call(
+      method: "deployment.identity" | "graph.status" | "graph.cancel",
+      params?: unknown,
+    ): Promise<unknown> {
+      const requestId = ++id;
+      await channel.send({ id: requestId, method, ...(params === undefined ? {} : { params }) });
+      const raw = await channel.receive();
+      await assertRemoteJournal(dirname(socketPath));
+      const current = await lstat(socketPath);
+      if (current.ino !== before.ino || current.dev !== before.dev)
+        throw Error("remote_cancel_endpoint_changed");
+      return z
+        .object({ id: z.literal(requestId), ok: z.literal(true), result: z.unknown() })
+        .strict()
+        .parse(raw).result;
+    },
+    close() {
+      channel.close();
+      socket.destroy();
+    },
+  };
+}
+export async function handleRemoteControlExportCommand(
+  args: string[],
+  deps: { input?: Readable; output?: Writable; env?: NodeJS.ProcessEnv } = {},
+): Promise<number> {
+  if (args.length === 1 && args[0] === "--help") {
+    (deps.output ?? process.stdout).write(
+      "remote-control --stdio\nDedicated signed Graph cancellation/receipt exporter. Requires host-private OSSTACK_REMOTE_CONTROL_REGISTRY and SHA256 configuration. No generic RPC or automatic daemon startup.\n",
+    );
+    return 0;
+  }
+  if (args.length !== 1 || args[0] !== "--stdio") return 1;
+  const env = deps.env ?? process.env,
+    input = deps.input ?? process.stdin,
+    output = deps.output ?? process.stdout;
+  const path = env["OSSTACK_REMOTE_CONTROL_REGISTRY"] ?? "",
+    pin = env["OSSTACK_REMOTE_CONTROL_SHA256"] ?? "";
+  let rpc: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof graphConnection>> | undefined;
+  let channel: RemoteChannel | undefined;
+  let expired = false;
+  const timer = setTimeout(() => {
+    expired = true;
+    rpc?.close();
+    channel?.close();
+    input.pause();
+  }, 10000);
+  const active = () => {
+    if (expired) throw Error("remote_cancel_timeout");
+  };
+  try {
+    const config = await registry(path, pin);
+    channel = new RemoteChannel(input, output);
+    const hello = RemoteCancelHello.parse(await channel.receive());
+    if (hello.home_id !== config.home_id || hello.registry_sha256 !== pin)
+      throw Error("remote_cancel_identity_mismatch");
+    const token = randomBytes(32).toString("hex");
+    await channel.send({
+      type: "control_grant",
+      protocol: 1,
+      nonce: hello.nonce,
+      home_id: config.home_id,
+      daemon_generation: "unobserved",
+      registry_sha256: pin,
+      token,
+      methods: ["cancel", "query"],
+    });
+    const request = RemoteCancelRequest.parse(await channel.receive());
+    if (request.token !== token) throw Error("remote_cancel_token_invalid");
+    const grant = request.grant,
+      b = grant.body,
+      hash = remoteCancelDigest(grant);
+    verifyRemoteCancelGrant(grant, config, pin, false);
+    const startedPath = join(config.journal_dir, `${b.request_id}.started.json`),
+      resultPath = join(config.journal_dir, `${b.request_id}.result.json`);
+    const base = {
+      type: "control_receipt" as const,
+      protocol: 1 as const,
+      nonce: hello.nonce,
+      home_id: config.home_id,
+      request_id: b.request_id,
+      request_sha256: hash,
+      daemon_generation: b.daemon_generation,
+      graph_generation: b.graph_generation,
+      graph_run_id: b.graph_run_id,
+      physical_stop: "not_attested" as const,
+      observed_revision: null,
+    };
+    const existing = await readRemoteJournal(startedPath);
+    if (existing && deliveryCanonical(existing) !== deliveryCanonical({ request_sha256: hash, grant }))
+      throw Error("remote_cancel_idempotency_conflict");
+    const prior = await readRemoteJournal(resultPath);
+    if (prior) {
+      const receipt = RemoteCancelReceipt.parse(prior);
+      if (
+        !existing ||
+        receipt.request_sha256 !== hash ||
+        receipt.home_id !== b.home_id ||
+        receipt.request_id !== b.request_id ||
+        receipt.daemon_generation !== b.daemon_generation ||
+        receipt.graph_generation !== b.graph_generation ||
+        receipt.graph_run_id !== b.graph_run_id ||
+        (receipt.outcome === "stop_requested" &&
+          (receipt.observed_revision === null || receipt.observed_revision <= b.expected_revision))
+      )
+        throw Error("remote_cancel_receipt_invalid");
+      await channel.send({ ...receipt, nonce: hello.nonce });
+      return 0;
+    }
+    if (request.type === "query" || existing) {
+      await channel.send({ ...base, outcome: existing ? "delivery_unknown" : "not_found" });
+      return 0;
+    }
+    verifyRemoteCancelGrant(grant, config, pin, true);
+    active();
+    rpc = await graphConnection(config.socket);
+    active();
+    const identity = DeploymentIdentitySchema.parse(await rpc.call("deployment.identity"));
+    if (
+      identity.home_id !== b.home_id ||
+      identity.generation !== b.daemon_generation ||
+      identity.protocol_version !== config.daemon_protocol
+    )
+      throw Error("remote_cancel_identity_mismatch");
+    const status = GraphDelegationStatus.parse(
+      await rpc.call("graph.status", { schemaVersion: 1, scope: b.graph_scope, graphRunId: b.graph_run_id }),
+    );
+    if (
+      status.graphRunId !== b.graph_run_id ||
+      deliveryCanonical(status.scope) !== deliveryCanonical(b.graph_scope) ||
+      status.generation !== b.graph_generation ||
+      status.revision !== b.expected_revision
+    )
+      throw Error("remote_cancel_generation_mismatch");
+    // Registry revocation and grant time are rechecked immediately before durable reserve.
+    await registry(path, pin);
+    verifyRemoteCancelGrant(grant, config, pin, true);
+    active();
+    if (!(await writeRemoteJournal(startedPath, { request_sha256: hash, grant }))) {
+      const other = await readRemoteJournal(startedPath);
+      if (deliveryCanonical(other) !== deliveryCanonical({ request_sha256: hash, grant }))
+        throw Error("remote_cancel_idempotency_conflict");
+      await channel.send({ ...base, outcome: "delivery_unknown" });
+      return 0;
+    }
+    let receipt: RemoteCancelReceipt = { ...base, outcome: "delivery_unknown" };
+    try {
+      await registry(path, pin);
+      verifyRemoteCancelGrant(grant, config, pin, true);
+      active();
+      const result = GraphDelegationStatus.parse(
+        await rpc.call("graph.cancel", {
+          schemaVersion: 1,
+          kind: "cancel",
+          scope: b.graph_scope,
+          graphRunId: b.graph_run_id,
+          expectedRevision: b.expected_revision,
+          idempotencyKey: `remote-cancel:${b.request_id}`,
+        }),
+      );
+      if (
+        result.graphRunId !== b.graph_run_id ||
+        deliveryCanonical(result.scope) !== deliveryCanonical(b.graph_scope) ||
+        result.generation !== b.graph_generation ||
+        result.revision <= b.expected_revision ||
+        !["cancel_pending", "cancelled"].includes(result.status)
+      )
+        throw Error("remote_cancel_receipt_invalid");
+      receipt = { ...base, outcome: "stop_requested", observed_revision: result.revision };
+    } catch {
+      /* The durable reserve never re-arms, including a crash or lost daemon response. */
+    }
+    await writeRemoteJournal(resultPath, receipt);
+    await channel.send(receipt);
+    return 0;
+  } catch {
+    return 1;
+  } finally {
+    clearTimeout(timer);
+    rpc?.close();
+    channel?.close();
+    input.pause();
+  }
+}

+import { createPrivateKey, createPublicKey, randomBytes, randomUUID, sign } from "node:crypto";
+import { join, dirname, isAbsolute, relative, sep } from "node:path";
+import { createInterface } from "node:readline/promises";
+import { z } from "zod";
+import { GraphScope } from "@osstack/protocol";
+import { deliveryCanonical } from "@osstack/core";
+import { readPrivateDeliveryFile } from "./follow-up-delivery-host.js";
+import {
+  RemoteCancelGrantBody,
+  SignedRemoteCancelGrant,
+  remoteCancelDigest,
+  writeRemoteJournal,
+  assertRemoteJournal,
+} from "./remote-cancel-protocol.js";
+const ref = z.string().regex(/^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_.-]{0,127}$/);
+export const RemoteCancelIssuerConfig = z
+  .object({
+    version: z.literal(1),
+    enabled: z.literal(true),
+    actor_id: ref,
+    key_id: ref,
+    public_key_pem: z.string().max(4096),
+    max_validity_ms: z.number().int().min(1000).max(300000),
+    targets: z
+      .array(
+        z
+          .object({
+            name: ref,
+            home_id: z.string().regex(/^home_[a-f0-9]{32}$/),
+            server_registry_sha256: z.string().regex(/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/),
+            scope_alias: ref,
+            repo_path: z.string().min(1).max(4096),
+            graph_scope: GraphScope,
+          })
+          .strict(),
+      )
+      .min(1)
+      .max(16),
+  })
+  .strict()
+  .refine((v) => new Set(v.targets.map((t) => t.name)).size === v.targets.length);
+export interface RemoteCancelIssuerDependencies {
+  home: string;
+  confirm?: (preview: string, phrase: string) => Promise<boolean>;
+  stdout?: (text: string) => void;
+  stderr?: (text: string) => void;
+}
+async function terminalConfirm(preview: string, phrase: string): Promise<boolean> {
+  if (!process.stdin.isTTY || !process.stderr.isTTY) throw Error("remote_cancel_terminal_required");
+  process.stderr.write(preview);
+  const rl = createInterface({ input: process.stdin, output: process.stderr, terminal: true });
+  try {
+    return (
+      (await rl.question(`Type exactly ${phrase}\n> `, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(60000) })) === phrase
+    );
+  } finally {
+    rl.close();
+  }
+}
+export const REMOTE_CANCEL_ISSUER_HELP = `remote-home issue-cancel-grant --target NAME --daemon-generation INSTANCE --graph-run ID --graph-generation N --revision N --output FILE
+Requires host-private remote-cancel-issuer.json, issuer-KEY.pem, local interactive TTY and exact preview digest approval.
+Creates only one bounded generation/revision-specific cancellation grant. Does not connect or cancel. No --yes or RPC signer.
+`;
+export async function handleRemoteCancelIssuerCommand(
+  args: string[],
+  deps: RemoteCancelIssuerDependencies,
+): Promise<number> {
+  const out =
+      deps.stdout ??
+      ((s) => {
+        process.stdout.write(s);
+      }),
+    err =
+      deps.stderr ??
+      ((s) => {
+        process.stderr.write(s);
+      });
+  if (
+    (args.length === 1 && args[0] === "--help") ||
+    (args.length === 2 && args[0] === "issue-cancel-grant" && args[1] === "--help")
+  ) {
+    out(REMOTE_CANCEL_ISSUER_HELP);
+    return 0;
+  }
+  try {
+    if (
+      args[0] !== "issue-cancel-grant" ||
+      args.length !== 13 ||
+      args.some((a) => Buffer.byteLength(a) > 4096)
+    )
+      throw Error();
+    const flags = new Map<string, string>();
+    for (let i = 1; i < args.length; i += 2) {
+      const k = args[i]!,
+        v = args[i + 1];
+      if (
+        ![
+          "--target",
+          "--daemon-generation",
+          "--graph-run",
+          "--graph-generation",
+          "--revision",
+          "--output",
+        ].includes(k) ||
+        !v ||
+        flags.has(k)
+      )
+        throw Error();
+      flags.set(k, v);
+    }
+    const configPath = join(deps.home, "remote-cancel-issuer.json"),
+      raw = await readPrivateDeliveryFile(configPath, 32768),
+      config = RemoteCancelIssuerConfig.parse(JSON.parse(raw.toString("utf8")));
+    const target = config.targets.find((t) => t.name === flags.get("--target")),
+      output = flags.get("--output");
+    if (!target || !output || !isAbsolute(output)) throw Error();
+    const rel = relative(target.repo_path, configPath);
+    if (rel === "" || (!isAbsolute(rel) && rel !== ".." && !rel.startsWith(`..${sep}`)))
+      throw Error("remote_cancel_issuer_in_repository");
+    await assertRemoteJournal(dirname(output));
+    const numeric = (key: string) => {
+      const v = flags.get(key) ?? "";
+      if (!/^(0|[1-9][0-9]*)$/.test(v)) throw Error();
+      return Number(v);
+    };
+    const issued = Date.now();
+    const body = RemoteCancelGrantBody.parse({
+      domain: "osstack.remote-graph-cancel.v1",
+      grant_id: randomUUID(),
+      request_id: randomUUID(),
+      actor_id: config.actor_id,
+      home_id: target.home_id,
+      daemon_generation: flags.get("--daemon-generation"),
+      server_registry_sha256: target.server_registry_sha256,
+      scope_alias: target.scope_alias,
+      repo_path: target.repo_path,
+      graph_scope: target.graph_scope,
+      action: "graph.cancel",
+      graph_run_id: flags.get("--graph-run"),
+      graph_generation: numeric("--graph-generation"),
+      expected_revision: numeric("--revision"),
+      nonce: randomBytes(32).toString("hex"),
+      issued_at_ms: issued,
+      expires_at_ms: issued + config.max_validity_ms,
+      max_calls: 1,
+    });
+    const preview = {
+        action: "remote Graph cancellation",
+        target: target.name,
+        key_id: config.key_id,
+        grant: body,
+        physical_stop: "not_attested",
+        retry: "never; query same request after uncertainty",
+      },
+      digest = remoteCancelDigest(preview);
+    if (
+      !(await (deps.confirm ?? terminalConfirm)(`${JSON.stringify(preview, null, 2)}\n`, `APPROVE ${digest}`))
+    )
+      throw Error();
+    if (
+      !raw.equals(await readPrivateDeliveryFile(configPath, 32768)) ||
+      Date.now() >= body.expires_at_ms ||
+      Date.now() < body.issued_at_ms
+    )
+      throw Error();
+    const key = createPrivateKey(
+      await readPrivateDeliveryFile(join(deps.home, `issuer-${config.key_id}.pem`), 4096),
+    );
+    if (!config.public_key_pem.startsWith("-----BEGIN PUBLIC KEY-----")) throw Error();
+    const expected = createPublicKey(config.public_key_pem);
+    if (
+      key.asymmetricKeyType !== "ed25519" ||
+      expected.asymmetricKeyType !== "ed25519" ||
+      !createPublicKey(key)
+        .export({ format: "der", type: "spki" })
+        .equals(expected.export({ format: "der", type: "spki" }))
+    )
+      throw Error();
+    if (!raw.equals(await readPrivateDeliveryFile(configPath, 32768))) throw Error();
+    const grant = SignedRemoteCancelGrant.parse({
+      body,
+      key_id: config.key_id,
+      signature: sign(null, Buffer.from(deliveryCanonical(body)), key).toString("base64"),
+    });
+    if (!(await writeRemoteJournal(output, grant))) throw Error();
+    out(
+      `${JSON.stringify({ schema_version: 1, status: "grant_created", request_id: body.request_id, preview_sha256: digest, output })}\n`,
+    );
+    return 0;
+  } catch {
+    err("remote_cancel_issuer_refused\n");
+    return 1;
+  }
+}

+import { createHash, createPublicKey, verify } from "node:crypto";
+import { constants } from "node:fs";
+import { lstat, mkdir, open, opendir, realpath } from "node:fs/promises";
+import { dirname, join, isAbsolute, resolve } from "node:path";
+import { z } from "zod";
+import { GraphScope } from "@osstack/protocol";
+import { deliveryCanonical } from "@osstack/core";
+import { readPrivateDeliveryFile } from "./follow-up-delivery-host.js";
+const digest = z.string().regex(/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/),
+  id = z.string().uuid(),
+  ref = z.string().regex(/^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_.-]{0,127}$/);
+export const RemoteCancelGrantBody = z
+  .object({
+    domain: z.literal("osstack.remote-graph-cancel.v1"),
+    grant_id: id,
+    request_id: id,
+    actor_id: ref,
+    home_id: z.string().regex(/^home_[a-f0-9]{32}$/),
+    daemon_generation: z.string().regex(/^instance_[a-f0-9]{32}$/),
+    server_registry_sha256: digest,
+    scope_alias: ref,
+    repo_path: z.string().min(1).max(4096),
+    graph_scope: GraphScope,
+    action: z.literal("graph.cancel"),
+    graph_run_id: ref,
+    graph_generation: z.number().int().safe().nonnegative(),
+    expected_revision: z.number().int().safe().nonnegative(),
+    nonce: digest,
+    issued_at_ms: z.number().int().safe().nonnegative(),
+    expires_at_ms: z.number().int().safe().nonnegative(),
+    max_calls: z.literal(1),
+  })
+  .strict()
+  .refine((v) => v.expires_at_ms > v.issued_at_ms && v.expires_at_ms - v.issued_at_ms <= 300000);
+export const SignedRemoteCancelGrant = z
+  .object({
+    body: RemoteCancelGrantBody,
+    key_id: ref,
+    signature: z.string().regex(/^[A-Za-z0-9+/]{85}[AQgw]==$/),
+  })
+  .strict();
+export type SignedRemoteCancelGrant = z.infer<typeof SignedRemoteCancelGrant>;
+export const RemoteCancelServerRegistry = z
+  .object({
+    version: z.literal(1),
+    home_id: z.string().regex(/^home_[a-f0-9]{32}$/),
+    daemon_protocol: z.number().int().positive(),
+    socket: z.string().max(4096),
+    journal_dir: z.string().max(4096),
+    scopes: z
+      .array(z.object({ alias: ref, repo_path: z.string().max(4096), graph_scope: GraphScope }).strict())
+      .min(1)
+      .max(16),
+    keys: z
+      .array(
+        z
+          .object({
+            key_id: ref,
+            public_key_pem: z.string().max(4096),
+            actor_id: ref,
+            scope_aliases: z.array(ref).min(1).max(16),
+            max_validity_ms: z.number().int().min(1000).max(300000),
+          })
+          .strict(),
+      )
+      .min(1)
+      .max(16),
+  })
+  .strict()
+  .refine(
+    (v) =>
+      new Set(v.scopes.map((s) => s.alias)).size === v.scopes.length &&
+      new Set(v.keys.map((k) => k.key_id)).size === v.keys.length,
+  );
+export type RemoteCancelServerRegistry = z.infer<typeof RemoteCancelServerRegistry>;
+export const RemoteCancelHello = z
+  .object({
+    type: z.literal("control_hello"),
+    protocol: z.literal(1),
+    nonce: digest,
+    home_id: z.string(),
+    registry_sha256: digest,
+  })
+  .strict();
+export const RemoteCancelHandshake = z
+  .object({
+    type: z.literal("control_grant"),
+    protocol: z.literal(1),
+    nonce: digest,
+    home_id: z.string(),
+    daemon_generation: z.string(),
+    registry_sha256: digest,
+    token: digest,
+    methods: z.tuple([z.literal("cancel"), z.literal("query")]),
+  })
+  .strict();
+export const RemoteCancelRequest = z
+  .object({ type: z.enum(["cancel", "query"]), token: digest, grant: SignedRemoteCancelGrant })
+  .strict();
+export const RemoteCancelReceipt = z
+  .object({
+    type: z.literal("control_receipt"),
+    protocol: z.literal(1),
+    nonce: digest,
+    home_id: z.string(),
+    request_id: id,
+    request_sha256: digest,
+    daemon_generation: z.string(),
+    graph_generation: z.number().int().safe().nonnegative(),
+    graph_run_id: ref,
+    outcome: z.enum(["not_found", "delivery_unknown", "stop_requested"]),
+    physical_stop: z.literal("not_attested"),
+    observed_revision: z.number().int().safe().nonnegative().nullable(),
+  })
+  .strict()
+  .refine((v) => (v.outcome === "stop_requested") === (v.observed_revision !== null));
+export type RemoteCancelReceipt = z.infer<typeof RemoteCancelReceipt>;
+export const remoteCancelDigest = (value: unknown) =>
+  createHash("sha256").update(deliveryCanonical(value)).digest("hex");
+export function verifyRemoteCancelGrant(
+  grant: SignedRemoteCancelGrant,
+  registry: RemoteCancelServerRegistry,
+  registryDigest: string,
+  forWrite: boolean,
+  now = Date.now(),
+): void {
+  const b = grant.body,
+    key = registry.keys.find((k) => k.key_id === grant.key_id),
+    scope = registry.scopes.find((s) => s.alias === b.scope_alias);
+  if (
+    !key ||
+    !scope ||
+    b.home_id !== registry.home_id ||
+    b.server_registry_sha256 !== registryDigest ||
+    b.actor_id !== key.actor_id ||
+    !key.scope_aliases.includes(b.scope_alias) ||
+    scope.repo_path !== b.repo_path ||
+    deliveryCanonical(scope.graph_scope) !== deliveryCanonical(b.graph_scope) ||
+    b.expires_at_ms - b.issued_at_ms > key.max_validity_ms ||
+    !key.public_key_pem.startsWith("-----BEGIN PUBLIC KEY-----")
+  )
+    throw Error("remote_cancel_authorization_refused");
+  const publicKey = createPublicKey(key.public_key_pem);
+  if (
+    publicKey.asymmetricKeyType !== "ed25519" ||
+    !verify(null, Buffer.from(deliveryCanonical(b)), publicKey, Buffer.from(grant.signature, "base64"))
+  )
+    throw Error("remote_cancel_authorization_refused");
+  // Exact historical receipt query remains possible after expiry; it never replays an effect.
+  if (forWrite && (!Number.isSafeInteger(now) || now < b.issued_at_ms || now >= b.expires_at_ms))
+    throw Error("remote_cancel_grant_expired");
+}
+export async function assertRemoteJournal(path: string): Promise<void> {
+  if (!isAbsolute(path) || resolve(path) !== path || (await realpath(path)) !== path)
+    throw Error("remote_cancel_journal_unavailable");
+  const info = await lstat(path);
+  if (!info.isDirectory() || info.uid !== process.getuid?.() || (info.mode & 0o077) !== 0)
+    throw Error("remote_cancel_journal_unavailable");
+  for (let p = dirname(path); ; p = dirname(p)) {
+    const st = await lstat(p);
+    if (
+      !st.isDirectory() ||
+      st.isSymbolicLink() ||
+      (st.uid !== 0 && st.uid !== process.getuid?.()) ||
+      ((st.mode & 0o022) !== 0 && !(st.uid === 0 && (st.mode & 0o1000) !== 0))
+    )
+      throw Error("remote_cancel_journal_unavailable");
+    if (dirname(p) === p) break;
+  }
+}
+export async function initializeRemoteJournal(home: string): Promise<string> {
+  const dir = join(home, "remote-actions");
+  await mkdir(dir, { mode: 0o700 }).catch((e) => {
+    if ((e as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code !== "EEXIST") throw e;
+  });
+  await assertRemoteJournal(dir);
+  return dir;
+}
+/** Create-only, file + directory fsync; a partial/crashed record is never overwritten or rearmed. */
+export async function writeRemoteJournal(path: string, value: unknown): Promise<boolean> {
+  await assertRemoteJournal(dirname(path));
+  const entries = await opendir(dirname(path));
+  let count = 0;
+  try {
+    for await (const _ of entries) {
+      if (++count > 2048) throw Error("remote_cancel_journal_full");
+    }
+  } finally {
+    await entries.close().catch(() => {});
+  }
+  const bytes = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(value));
+  if (bytes.length > 32768) throw Error("remote_cancel_journal_too_large");
+  let file: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof open>>;
+  try {
+    file = await open(
+      path,
+      constants.O_WRONLY | constants.O_CREAT | constants.O_EXCL | constants.O_NOFOLLOW,
+      0o600,
+    );
+  } catch (e) {
+    if ((e as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code === "EEXIST") return false;
+    throw e;
+  }
+  try {
+    await file.writeFile(bytes);
+    await file.sync();
+  } finally {
+    await file.close();
+  }
+  const directory = await open(dirname(path), constants.O_RDONLY);
+  try {
+    await directory.sync();
+  } finally {
+    await directory.close();
+  }
+  return true;
+}
+export async function readRemoteJournal(path: string): Promise<unknown | null> {
+  try {
+    return JSON.parse(
+      new TextDecoder("utf-8", { fatal: true }).decode(await readPrivateDeliveryFile(path, 32768)),
+    );
+  } catch (e) {
+    if ((e as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code === "ENOENT") return null;
+    throw Error("remote_cancel_journal_unavailable");
+  }
+}

+import { execFile } from "node:child_process";
+import { constants } from "node:fs";
+import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
+import { lstat, mkdir, open, realpath, link, unlink } from "node:fs/promises";
+import {
+  dirname,
+  isAbsolute,
+  join,
+  relative,
+  basename,
+  resolve,
+} from "node:path";
+import { promisify } from "node:util";
+import {
+  assertFrozenDeliveryContract,
+  deliveryCheckReference,
+  deliveryContractDigest,
+  freezeDeliveryContract,
+} from "@osstack/core";
+import { z } from "zod";
+import type { DeliverCommandDeps } from "./deliver-command.js";
+import {
+  deliveryBytesDigest,
+  readDeliveryHostRecord,
+} from "./delivery-contract-host.js";
+
+const exec = promisify(execFile);
+const safeId = z.string().regex(/^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_.-]{0,79}$/);
+const digestSchema = z.string().regex(/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/);
+const pathSchema = z
+  .string()
+  .min(1)
+  .max(1024)
+  .refine(
+    (p) =>
+      !p.startsWith("-") &&
+      !isAbsolute(p) &&
+      !p.includes("\\") &&
+      [...p].every((c) => c.charCodeAt(0) >= 32 && c.charCodeAt(0) !== 127) &&
+      p.split("/").every((c) => c !== "." && c !== ".." && c !== ""),
+  );
+const inspectSchema = z
+  .object({
+    repo: z.string().min(1).max(4096),
+    goal: z.string().min(1).max(16000),
+    paths: z.array(pathSchema).min(1).max(16),
+    profile: safeId,
+    requestId: safeId,
+  })
+  .strict();
+type InspectInput = z.infer<typeof inspectSchema>;
+type Policy = NonNullable<DeliverCommandDeps["deliveryContractPolicy"]>;
+interface ScoutHostOptions {
+  home: string;
+  policy: Policy;
+  deliver?: (
+    args: string[],
+    emitJson: (value: unknown) => void,
+  ) => Promise<number>;
+}
+interface ScoutRecord {
+  schemaVersion: 1;
+  kind: "scout";
+  tools: ["git_committed_blob_read"];
+  requestId: string;
+  profile: string;
+  contract: ReturnType<typeof freezeDeliveryContract>;
+  facts: Array<{
+    path: string;
+    blob: string;
+    bytes: number;
+    sha256: string;
+    text: string;
+  }>;
+  qualification: "committed_source_investigation_not_code_delivery";
+}
+/** Fixed read operations only. No model, shell, mutable file read, checkout, filter or check command is exposed. */
+async function gitRead(
+  repo: string,
+  args: string[],
+  maxBuffer = 262144,
+): Promise<Buffer> {
+  const environment: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = {};
+  for (const key of ["PATH", "SYSTEMROOT", "TMPDIR"])
+    if (process.env[key]) environment[key] = process.env[key];
+  const result = await exec(
+    "git",
+    [
+      "--no-replace-objects",
+      "-c",
+      "core.hooksPath=/dev/null",
+      "-c",
+      "core.fsmonitor=false",
+      ...args,
+    ],
+    {
+      cwd: repo,
+      timeout: 5000,
+      maxBuffer,
+      encoding: "buffer",
+      env: {
+        ...environment,
+        GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM: "1",
+        GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL: "/dev/null",
+        GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT: "0",
+        GIT_OPTIONAL_LOCKS: "0",
+      },
+    },
+  );
+  return result.stdout;
+}
+async function headOf(repo: string): Promise<string> {
+  const head = (await gitRead(repo, ["rev-parse", "--verify", "HEAD^{commit}"]))
+    .toString()
+    .trim();
+  if (!/^[a-f0-9]{40,64}$/.test(head)) throw Error("scout_invalid_head");
+  return head;
+}
+async function privateDirectory(path: string): Promise<string> {
+  await mkdir(path, { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
+  const canonical = await realpath(path);
+  const info = await lstat(path);
+  if (
+    !info.isDirectory() ||
+    info.isSymbolicLink() ||
+    (info.mode & 0o077) !== 0 ||
+    (process.getuid && info.uid !== process.getuid())
+  )
+    throw Error("scout_private_state_required");
+  return canonical;
+}
+async function createOnly(path: string, body: string): Promise<void> {
+  const temporary = join(dirname(path), `.scout-${randomUUID()}.tmp`);
+  const file = await open(
+    temporary,
+    constants.O_CREAT |
+      constants.O_EXCL |
+      constants.O_WRONLY |
+      constants.O_NOFOLLOW,
+    0o600,
+  );
+  try {
+    await file.writeFile(body);
+    await file.sync();
+    await link(temporary, path);
+    const directory = await open(dirname(path), constants.O_RDONLY);
+    try {
+      await directory.sync();
+    } finally {
+      await directory.close();
+    }
+  } finally {
+    await file.close();
+    await unlink(temporary);
+  }
+}
+async function prospectivePath(path: string): Promise<string> {
+  try {
+    return await realpath(path);
+  } catch (error) {
+    if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code !== "ENOENT") throw error;
+    const parent = dirname(path);
+    if (parent === path) throw error;
+    return join(await prospectivePath(parent), basename(path));
+  }
+}
+function outsideRepository(repo: string, target: string): void {
+  const rel = relative(repo, target);
+  if (
+    rel === "" ||
+    (!rel.startsWith("../") && rel !== ".." && !isAbsolute(rel))
+  )
+    throw Error("scout_state_must_be_outside_repository");
+}
+export function createScoutHost(options: ScoutHostOptions) {
+  const stateRoot = async () => {
+    if (!isAbsolute(options.home)) throw Error("scout_absolute_home_required");
+    const home = await privateDirectory(options.home);
+    return privateDirectory(join(home, "scout"));
+  };
+  const store = async (value: unknown) => {
+    const root = await stateRoot();
+    const body = JSON.stringify(value) + "\n";
+    const digest = deliveryBytesDigest(body);
+    const path = join(root, `${digest}.json`);
+    try {
+      await createOnly(path, body);
+    } catch (error) {
+      if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code !== "EEXIST") throw error;
+      await readDeliveryHostRecord(root, { path, digest });
+    }
+    return { path, digest };
+  };
+  const read = async (digest: string): Promise<ScoutRecord> => {
+    digestSchema.parse(digest);
+    const root = await stateRoot();
+    const raw = (await readDeliveryHostRecord(root, {
+      path: join(root, `${digest}.json`),
+      digest,
+    })) as ScoutRecord;
+    if (
+      raw.schemaVersion !== 1 ||
+      raw.kind !== "scout" ||
+      raw.qualification !==
+        "committed_source_investigation_not_code_delivery" ||
+      raw.tools?.length !== 1 ||
+      raw.tools[0] !== "git_committed_blob_read"
+    )
+      throw Error("scout_record_invalid");
+    const frozen = assertFrozenDeliveryContract(raw.contract);
+    if (frozen.contract.mode !== "scout") throw Error("scout_record_invalid");
+    safeId.parse(raw.profile);
+    return raw;
+  };
+  return {
+    async inspect(value: InspectInput) {
+      const input = inspectSchema.parse(value);
+      const repo = await realpath(input.repo);
+      if (
+        (await realpath(
+          (
+            await gitRead(repo, ["rev-parse", "--show-toplevel"])
+          )
+            .toString()
+            .trim(),
+        )) !== repo
+      )
+        throw Error("scout_repository_root_required");
+      outsideRepository(repo, await prospectivePath(resolve(options.home)));
+      const root = await stateRoot();
+      outsideRepository(repo, root);
+      const head = await headOf(repo);
+      const policy = await options.policy(repo, head);
+      if (policy.budgetRef !== `policy:${policy.policyDigest}`)
+        throw Error("scout_policy_invalid");
+      const paths = [...new Set(input.paths)].sort();
+      const facts: ScoutRecord["facts"] = [];
+      let bytes = 0;
+      for (const path of paths) {
+        const entry = (
+          await gitRead(repo, ["ls-tree", "-z", head, "--", path])
+        ).toString();
+        const match =
+          /^(100644|100755) blob ([a-f0-9]{40,64})\t([^\0]+)\0$/.exec(entry);
+        if (!match || match[3] !== path)
+          throw Error("scout_regular_committed_file_required");
+        const blob = match[2]!;
+        const size = Number(
+          (await gitRead(repo, ["cat-file", "-s", blob])).toString().trim(),
+        );
+        bytes += size;
+        if (
+          !Number.isSafeInteger(size) ||
+          size < 0 ||
+          size > 65536 ||
+          bytes > 262144
+        )
+          throw Error("scout_source_budget_exceeded");
+        const content = await gitRead(repo, ["cat-file", "blob", blob], 65537);
+        if (content.length !== size || content.includes(0))
+          throw Error("scout_source_invalid");
+        const text = content.toString("utf8");
+        if (!Buffer.from(text).equals(content))
+          throw Error("scout_utf8_required");
+        facts.push({
+          path,
+          blob,
+          bytes: size,
+          sha256: deliveryBytesDigest(content),
+          text,
+        });
+      }
+      if ((await headOf(repo)) !== head) throw Error("scout_head_changed");
+      const currentPolicy = await options.policy(repo, head);
+      if (
+        deliveryContractDigest(currentPolicy) !== deliveryContractDigest(policy)
+      )
+        throw Error("scout_policy_changed");
+      const contract = freezeDeliveryContract({
+        schema_version: 2,
+        contract_id: `scout-${input.requestId}`,
+        task_id: `scout-${input.requestId}`,
+        canonical_repo: repo,
+        mode: "scout",
+        base_head: head,
+        goal: input.goal,
+        required_checks: policy.verifyCommands.map((command, index) => ({
+          id: `host-${index}`,
+          registry_ref: deliveryCheckReference(command),
+        })),
+        review_policy_ref: `profile:${input.profile}`,
+        requested_completion: "local_verified",
+        allowed_paths: paths,
+        requested_actions: ["verify"],
+        budget_ref: policy.budgetRef,
+        policy_digest: policy.policyDigest,
+        publication_scope: { mode: "none" },
+        merge_authority: { mode: "none" },
+      });
+      const record: ScoutRecord = {
+        schemaVersion: 1,
+        kind: "scout",
+        tools: ["git_committed_blob_read"],
+        requestId: input.requestId,
+        profile: input.profile,
+        contract,
+        facts,
+        qualification: "committed_source_investigation_not_code_delivery",
+      };
+      const saved = await store(record);
+      const requestPath = join(
+        root,
+        `inspect-${deliveryContractDigest([repo, input.requestId])}.json`,
+      );
+      try {
+        await createOnly(
+          requestPath,
+          JSON.stringify({ digest: saved.digest }) + "\n",
+        );
+      } catch (error) {
+        if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code !== "EEXIST") throw error;
+        const file = await open(
+          requestPath,
+          constants.O_RDONLY | constants.O_NOFOLLOW,
+        );
+        try {
+          const stat = await file.stat();
+          if (
+            !stat.isFile() ||
+            stat.size > 128 ||
+            JSON.parse(
+              (
+                await file.read({
+                  buffer: Buffer.alloc(129),
+                  length: 129,
+                  position: 0,
+                })
+              ).buffer
+                .toString("utf8")
+                .replace(/\0+$/, ""),
+            ).digest !== saved.digest
+          )
+            throw Error("scout_request_conflict");
+        } finally {
+          await file.close();
+        }
+      }
+      return { ...record, ...saved };
+    },
+    async promote(value: { digest: string; requestId: string }) {
+      const input = z
+        .object({ digest: digestSchema, requestId: safeId })
+        .strict()
+        .parse(value);
+      if (!options.deliver) throw Error("scout_delivery_host_required");
+      const parent = await read(input.digest);
+      const repo = await realpath(parent.contract.contract.canonical_repo);
+      const head = await headOf(repo);
+      const policy = await options.policy(repo, head);
+      const frozen = freezeDeliveryContract({
+        ...parent.contract.contract,
+        contract_id: `implement-${deliveryContractDigest([input.digest, input.requestId]).slice(0, 48)}`,
+        task_id: `implement-${deliveryContractDigest([input.digest, input.requestId]).slice(0, 48)}`,
+        parent_contract_id: parent.contract.contract.contract_id,
+        mode: "implement",
+        base_head: head,
+        policy_digest: policy.policyDigest,
+        budget_ref: policy.budgetRef,
+        required_checks: policy.verifyCommands.map((command, index) => ({
+          id: `host-${index}`,
+          registry_ref: deliveryCheckReference(command),
+        })),
+        requested_actions: ["build", "verify"],
+      });
+      const contractFile = await store(frozen.contract);
+      const root = await stateRoot();
+      const claim = join(
+        root,
+        `promotion-${deliveryContractDigest([repo, input.digest])}.json`,
+      );
+      try {
+        await createOnly(
+          claim,
+          JSON.stringify({
+            state: "started",
+            requestId: input.requestId,
+            parent: input.digest,
+            contractDigest: frozen.digest,
+            baseHead: head,
+          }) + "\n",
+        );
+      } catch (error) {
+        if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code === "EEXIST")
+          throw Error("scout_promotion_already_claimed_reconcile");
+        throw error;
+      }
+      // This performs all normal fresh profile/risk/policy/worktree/check/review admission. Old scout facts grant nothing.
+      let deliveryResult: unknown;
+      const exitCode = await options.deliver(
+        [
+          "--workspace",
+          repo,
+          "--autonomy",
+          "guarded-local",
+          "--profile",
+          parent.profile,
+          "--contract",
+          contractFile.path,
+          "--max-iter",
+          String(
+            Math.min(
+              policy.budget.max_builder_iterations,
+              policy.budget.max_review_rounds,
+            ),
+          ),
+          "--json",
+        ],
+        (value) => {
+          deliveryResult = value;
+        },
+      );
+      if (exitCode === 0) {
+        const summary = z
+          .object({
+            delivery_contract: z
+              .object({
+                digest: digestSchema,
+                achieved_completion: z.literal("local_verified"),
+              })
+              .passthrough(),
+          })
+          .passthrough()
+          .parse(deliveryResult);
+        if (summary.delivery_contract.digest !== frozen.digest)
+          throw Error("scout_delivery_result_mismatch");
+      }
+      const result = {
+        ...(deliveryResult === undefined
+          ? {}
+          : { deliveryResultDigest: deliveryContractDigest(deliveryResult) }),
+        state: exitCode === 0 ? ("completed" as const) : ("failed" as const),
+        exitCode,
+        parentDigest: input.digest,
+        contractDigest: frozen.digest,
+        claim,
+      };
+      const receipt = await store(result);
+      await createOnly(
+        join(root, `promotion-result-${input.digest}.json`),
+        JSON.stringify(receipt) + "\n",
+      );
+      return {
+        ...result,
+        ...receipt,
+        ...(deliveryResult === undefined ? {} : { deliveryResult }),
+      };
+    },
+  };
+}
+function terminalJson(value: unknown): string {
+  return [...JSON.stringify(value, null, 2)]
+    .map((character) => {
+      const code = character.charCodeAt(0);
+      return (code >= 128 && code <= 159) || code === 0x2028 || code === 0x2029
+        ? `\\u${code.toString(16).padStart(4, "0")}`
+        : character;
+    })
+    .join("");
+}
+export async function handleScoutCommand(
+  args: string[],
+  options: ScoutHostOptions,
+  stdout: (line: string) => void = console.log,
+): Promise<number> {
+  if (args.includes("--help") || args[0] === "help") {
+    stdout(
+      "osstack scout inspect --repo PATH --goal TEXT --path FILE [--path FILE] --profile NAME --request-id ID\nosstack scout promote --scout DIGEST --request-id ID\nInspect reads bounded committed files only. Promote explicitly starts fresh guarded-local delivery; it never inherits approval or publishes.",
+    );
+    return 0;
+  }
+  const flags = new Map<string, string>();
+  const paths: string[] = [];
+  for (let i = 1; i < args.length; i++) {
+    const key = args[i]!;
+    if (key === "--json") continue;
+    const value = args[++i];
+    if (!value || value.startsWith("--"))
+      throw Error("scout_invalid_arguments");
+    if (key === "--path") {
+      paths.push(value);
+      continue;
+    }
+    if (
+      !["--repo", "--goal", "--profile", "--request-id", "--scout"].includes(
+        key,
+      ) ||
+      flags.has(key)
+    )
+      throw Error("scout_invalid_arguments");
+    flags.set(key, value);
+  }
+  const host = createScoutHost(options);
+  if (args[0] === "inspect") {
+    if (flags.has("--scout")) throw Error("scout_invalid_arguments");
+    const result = await host.inspect({
+      repo: flags.get("--repo")!,
+      goal: flags.get("--goal")!,
+      paths,
+      profile: flags.get("--profile")!,
+      requestId: flags.get("--request-id")!,
+    });
+    stdout(terminalJson(result));
+    return 0;
+  }
+  if (args[0] === "promote") {
+    if (
+      paths.length ||
+      ["--repo", "--goal", "--profile"].some((key) => flags.has(key))
+    )
+      throw Error("scout_invalid_arguments");
+    const result = await host.promote({
+      digest: flags.get("--scout")!,
+      requestId: flags.get("--request-id")!,
+    });
+    stdout(terminalJson(result));
+    return result.exitCode;
+  }
+  throw Error("scout_subcommand_required");
+}

+import { constants, openSync, closeSync, readSync, fstatSync, lstatSync, realpathSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { dirname, isAbsolute, join, resolve, relative, sep } from 'node:path';
+import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
+import { LocalSourceManifestSchema, localSourceManifestDigest } from './local-data-source-registry.js';
+import type { HostSourceObservationService } from './source-observation-host.js';
+import type { ExecutionContextSource } from '@osstack/knowledge';
+const digest=(bytes:Buffer)=>createHash('sha256').update(bytes).digest('hex');
+export function readSourceContextFile(path:string,maximum:number,privateFile:boolean):Buffer{
+ if(!isAbsolute(path)||resolve(path)!==path||realpathSync(path)!==path)throw Error('source_dispatch_path_changed');
+ if(privateFile&&(lstatSync(dirname(path)).mode&0o077)!==0)throw Error('source_dispatch_directory_not_private');
+ if(privateFile)for(let parent=dirname(path);;parent=dirname(parent)){const s=lstatSync(parent),sticky=s.uid===0&&(s.mode&0o1000)!==0;if(!s.isDirectory()||s.isSymbolicLink()||(s.uid!==0&&s.uid!==process.getuid?.())||((s.mode&0o022)!==0&&!sticky))throw Error('source_dispatch_unsafe_ancestor');if(dirname(parent)===parent)break;}
+ const fd=openSync(path,constants.O_RDONLY|constants.O_NOFOLLOW|constants.O_NONBLOCK);try{const before=fstatSync(fd);if(!before.isFile()||before.size>maximum||(privateFile&&(before.nlink!==1||before.uid!==process.getuid?.()||(before.mode&0o077)!==0)))throw Error('source_dispatch_file_invalid');const bytes=Buffer.alloc(maximum+1);let length=0;while(length<bytes.length){const n=readSync(fd,bytes,length,bytes.length-length,null);if(!n)break;length+=n;}const after=fstatSync(fd),current=lstatSync(path);if(length!==before.size||before.mtimeMs!==after.mtimeMs||before.ctimeMs!==after.ctimeMs||current.ino!==before.ino||current.dev!==before.dev||current.isSymbolicLink()||current.mtimeMs!==before.mtimeMs||current.ctimeMs!==before.ctimeMs)throw Error('source_dispatch_file_changed');return bytes.subarray(0,length);}finally{closeSync(fd);}
+}
+export interface SourceContextBinding {policy_digest:string;registry_digest:string;source_id:string;generation:string;revision:number;record_revision:number;manifest_digest:string;content_digest:string}
+/** No await: the final adapter dispatch follows this bounded host-authority check in the same JS turn. */
+export function assertSourceContextDispatch(home:string,entries:readonly ExecutionContextSource[],service:HostSourceObservationService,signal:AbortSignal):void{
+ const sources=entries.filter(e=>e.source_kind==='data_source');if(!sources.length)return;if(sources.length>4||signal.aborted)throw Error('source_context_dispatch_invalid');
+ const policyPath=join(home,'source-context.json'),registryPath=join(home,'source-registry.json');const policyBytes=readSourceContextFile(policyPath,32768,true),registryBytes=readSourceContextFile(registryPath,131072,true);const config=JSON.parse(registryBytes.toString()) as {schema_version?:unknown;registrations?:unknown};if(config.schema_version!==1||!Array.isArray(config.registrations)||config.registrations.length>32)throw Error('source_dispatch_registry_invalid');
+ const now=Date.now();let expires=Infinity;for(const entry of sources){const binding=JSON.parse(entry.source_ref) as SourceContextBinding;if(binding.policy_digest!==digest(policyBytes)||binding.registry_digest!==digest(registryBytes))throw Error('source_context_authority_changed');const rows=config.registrations.filter((r:unknown)=>r!==null&&typeof r==='object'&&'manifest' in r&&(r as {manifest?:{id?:unknown}}).manifest?.id===binding.source_id);if(rows.length!==1)throw Error('source_context_registration_changed');const row=rows[0] as {manifest:unknown;source_root:unknown;manifest_digest:unknown;content_digest:unknown};const manifest=LocalSourceManifestSchema.parse(row.manifest);if(manifest.repo_path!==entry.scope.repo_path||manifest.version!==binding.generation||row.manifest_digest!==binding.manifest_digest||row.content_digest!==binding.content_digest||localSourceManifestDigest(manifest)!==binding.manifest_digest||manifest.verified_at_ms>now||manifest.expires_at_ms<=now||manifest.verified_at_ms+manifest.fresh_for_ms<now||typeof row.source_root!=='string')throw Error('source_context_registration_changed');
+  expires=Math.min(expires,manifest.expires_at_ms,manifest.verified_at_ms+manifest.fresh_for_ms);
+  const root=realpathSync(row.source_root),path=resolve(root,manifest.file),rel=relative(root,path);if(isAbsolute(rel)||rel==='..'||rel.startsWith(`..${sep}`))throw Error('source_dispatch_path_changed');if(digest(readSourceContextFile(path,manifest.max_bytes,false))!==binding.content_digest)throw Error('source_context_content_changed');
+  const current=service.status({repo_path:entry.scope.repo_path,source_id:binding.source_id,default_tenant:true});if(current?.state!=='ready'||current.record_revision!==binding.record_revision||current.accepted.generation!==binding.generation||current.accepted.revision!==binding.revision||current.accepted.content_digest!==binding.content_digest||current.accepted.manifest_digest!==binding.manifest_digest)throw Error('source_context_ledger_changed');
+ }
+ // Later source reads cannot hide revocation of an earlier source in the shared registry.
+ const dispatchedAt=Date.now();if(dispatchedAt<now||dispatchedAt>=expires||signal.aborted||digest(readSourceContextFile(policyPath,32768,true))!==digest(policyBytes)||digest(readSourceContextFile(registryPath,131072,true))!==digest(registryBytes))throw Error('source_context_authority_changed');
+}

+import { join } from 'node:path';
+import { assertSourceContextDispatch, type SourceContextBinding } from './source-context-fence.js';
+import { z } from 'zod';
+import { executionContextDigest, type ExecutionContextEntry, type ExecutionContextSource } from '@osstack/knowledge';
+import type { ExecutionContextLoader } from './execution-context.js';
+import { createHostSourceObservationService, readPrivateSourcePolicy, type HostSourceObservationService } from './source-observation-host.js';
+const SourceContextPolicy=z.object({schema_version:z.literal(1),repo_path:z.string().max(4096),default_tenant:z.literal(true),source_ids:z.array(z.string().regex(/^[a-z][a-z0-9_-]{0,63}$/)).min(1).max(4),max_bytes:z.number().int().min(1).max(8192)}).strict().refine(v=>new Set(v.source_ids).size===v.source_ids.length);
+type Binding=SourceContextBinding;
+/** Explicit host opt-in, never inferred from a repository pack or source registration. */
+export function createHostSourceContext(home:string,service:HostSourceObservationService=createHostSourceObservationService(home)){
+ const policyPath=join(home,'source-context.json'),registryPath=join(home,'source-registry.json');
+ const load:ExecutionContextLoader=async(_task,{workspacePath,signal})=>{
+  const file=await readPrivateSourcePolicy(policyPath);if(!file)return [];const policy=SourceContextPolicy.parse(file.value);if(policy.repo_path!==workspacePath)throw Error('source_context_scope_mismatch');const registry=await readPrivateSourcePolicy(registryPath,131072);if(!registry)throw Error('source_context_registry_missing');
+  const entries:ExecutionContextEntry[]=[];let bytes=0;
+  for(const id of policy.source_ids){if(signal.aborted)throw Error('source_cancelled');const {snapshot,observation}=await service.observe({repo_path:workspacePath,source_id:id,default_tenant:true},signal);if(snapshot.state!=='ready'||!observation||observation.state!=='ready'||!snapshot.provenance)throw Error('source_context_not_ready');const p=snapshot.provenance;
+   const binding:Binding={policy_digest:file.digest,registry_digest:registry.digest,source_id:id,generation:p.version,revision:p.revision,record_revision:observation.record_revision,manifest_digest:p.manifest_digest,content_digest:p.content_digest};
+   // One bounded JSON entry per source preserves its complete snapshot; the normal prompt assembler may omit it and records that decision.
+   const text=JSON.stringify({source_id:id,generation:p.version,revision:p.revision,entries:snapshot.entries});bytes+=Buffer.byteLength(text);if(bytes>policy.max_bytes)throw Error('source_context_budget_exceeded');
+   entries.push({id:`source:${id}`,source_kind:'data_source',source_ref:JSON.stringify(binding),source_digest:p.content_digest,text_sha256:executionContextDigest(text),scope:{repo_path:workspacePath,tenant:'default'},tier:'optional',text,verified_at_ms:p.verified_at_ms,expires_at_ms:p.expires_at_ms,evidence_kind:'pinned_local_snapshot'});
+  }
+  if((await readPrivateSourcePolicy(policyPath))?.digest!==file.digest||(await readPrivateSourcePolicy(registryPath,131072))?.digest!==registry.digest)throw Error('source_policy_changed');return entries;
+ };
+ async function validate(entries:readonly ExecutionContextSource[],signal:AbortSignal){
+  for(const entry of entries.filter(e=>e.source_kind==='data_source')){const binding=JSON.parse(entry.source_ref) as Binding;const file=await readPrivateSourcePolicy(policyPath);if(!file||file.digest!==binding.policy_digest)throw Error('source_policy_changed');const policy=SourceContextPolicy.parse(file.value);if(policy.repo_path!==entry.scope.repo_path||!policy.source_ids.includes(binding.source_id))throw Error('source_context_scope_mismatch');
+   const scope={repo_path:entry.scope.repo_path,source_id:binding.source_id,default_tenant:true as const};const {snapshot,observation:current}=await service.observe(scope,signal);const p=snapshot.provenance;
+   if(signal.aborted||snapshot.state!=='ready'||!p||p.version!==binding.generation||p.revision!==binding.revision||p.manifest_digest!==binding.manifest_digest||p.content_digest!==binding.content_digest||current?.state!=='ready'||current.record_revision!==binding.record_revision)throw Error('source_context_changed_before_invoke');
+   if((await readPrivateSourcePolicy(policyPath))?.digest!==binding.policy_digest)throw Error('source_policy_changed');
+  }
+ }
+ return {load,validate,validateSync:(entries:readonly ExecutionContextSource[],signal:AbortSignal)=>assertSourceContextDispatch(home,entries,service,signal)};
+}

+export const SOURCE_OBSERVATION_HELP='sources observe|events|acknowledge SOURCE_ID --repo ABSOLUTE_PATH --default-tenant [--after SEQUENCE] [--grant GRANT_ID] [--json]\nObserve persists sequence evidence; events reads its bounded journal. Acknowledge requires an exact private host grant.\n';

   async claim(digest: string, groupId: string): Promise<UpgradeJournalWriter> {

+import { existsSync, readdirSync } from 'node:fs';

+/** Creates a NEW private managed installation; interrupted identical provisioning is explicitly resumable. */
+export async function provisionManagedInstallation(input: { request: string; approvedRegistryDigest: string; artifact: string; artifactDigest: string }, signal: AbortSignal) {
+  const bytes = readPrivate(input.request); if (sha256(bytes) !== input.approvedRegistryDigest) throw Error('upgrade_registry_approval');
+  const config = ManagedInstallationSchema.parse(JSON.parse(bytes.toString())); privateDirectory(config.root);
+  const claim={registry_digest:input.approvedRegistryDigest,artifact_digest:input.artifactDigest};
+  const names=readdirSync(config.root);const resumed=existsSync(join(config.root,'provisioning.json'));
+  if(names.length&&(!resumed||upgradeCanonical(JSON.parse(readPrivate(join(config.root,'provisioning.json')).toString()))!==upgradeCanonical(claim)))throw Error('upgrade_installation_not_empty');
+  const allowed=new Set(['provisioning.json','provision-owner.pid','provision-owner.pid.lock.db','consumers','services','releases','current','installation.json']);
+  if(resumed&&names.some(name=>!allowed.has(name)&&!/^\.publish-[a-f0-9]{32}$/.test(name)))throw Error('upgrade_provision_unknown_file');
+  const homeLocks:Array<()=>void>=[];
+  const publishSame=async(path:string,value:unknown)=>{try{await publishPrivate(path,value);}catch(error){if((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code!=='EEXIST'||upgradeCanonical(JSON.parse(readPrivate(path).toString()))!==upgradeCanonical(value))throw error;}await finishPrivatePublication(path);};
+  await publishSame(join(config.root,'provisioning.json'),claim);
+  const releaseOwner=acquireDaemonLock(join(config.root,'provision-owner.pid'));
+  try{
+    const bundle = loadBundle(input.artifact,input.artifactDigest);if(!bundle.files.some(f=>f.path===config.entry))throw Error('upgrade_missing_entry');
+    for(const c of config.consumers){privateDirectory(c.home);const identity=JSON.parse(readPrivate(join(c.home,'deployment-identity.json'),512).toString());if(identity.home_id!==c.home_id||identity.schema_version!==1)throw Error('upgrade_home_identity_changed');homeLocks.push(acquireDaemonLock(join(c.home,'daemon.pid')));await removeAbandonedUpgradeSocket(c.socket,signal);}

+import { execFile } from 'node:child_process';
+import { existsSync, lstatSync, readFileSync, readdirSync, realpathSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { unlink } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { connect } from 'node:net';

+export interface UpgradeBackend {
+  assertConsumers(config: ManagedInstallation, allowedPids: readonly number[], signal: AbortSignal): Promise<void>;
+  service(label: string, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<'running' | 'absent'>;
+  stop(label: string, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<void>;
+  start(plist: string, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<void>;

+/** Fixed executable and fixed subcommands only. Test harnesses inject a process-backed backend, never CLI inputs. */
+export function launchdUpgradeBackend(): UpgradeBackend {
+  if (process.platform !== 'darwin' || !process.getuid) throw Error('upgrade_backend_unsupported');
+  const domain = `gui/${process.getuid()}`;
+  const run = (args: string[], signal: AbortSignal): Promise<{ code: number; text: string }> => new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
+    execFile('/bin/launchctl', args, { signal, timeout: 15000, maxBuffer: 131072, env: { PATH: '/usr/bin:/bin', LANG: 'C' } }, (error, stdout, stderr) => {
+      if (signal.aborted || error && typeof error.code !== 'number') { reject(Error('upgrade_backend_unknown')); return; }
+      resolve({ code: error?.code as number ?? 0, text: stdout + stderr });
+    });
+  });
+  return {
+    async assertConsumers(config,allowedPids,signal) {
+      const listed=await run(['list'],signal);if(listed.code!==0)throw Error('upgrade_consumer_inventory_unknown');
+      const labels=listed.text.split('\n').map(line=>line.trim().split(/\s+/).at(-1)!).filter(label=>label?.startsWith('ai.osstack.managed.'));
+      if(labels.length>256)throw Error('upgrade_consumer_inventory_limit');
+      for(const label of labels){const printed=await run(['print',`${domain}/${label}`],signal);if(printed.code!==0)throw Error('upgrade_consumer_inventory_unknown');
+        const expected=config.consumers.find(c=>c.label===label);
+        if(!expected&&printed.text.includes(config.root+'/'))throw Error('upgrade_unregistered_consumer');
+        if(expected&&(!printed.text.includes(join(config.root,'current',config.entry))||!printed.text.includes(expected.home)||!printed.text.includes(config.node)))throw Error('upgrade_service_identity');
+      }
+      const processTable=await new Promise<string>((resolve,reject)=>execFile('/bin/ps',['-axo','pid=,command='],{signal,timeout:5000,maxBuffer:2097152,env:{PATH:'/usr/bin:/bin',LANG:'C'}},(error,stdout)=>error?reject(Error('upgrade_process_inventory_unknown')):resolve(stdout)));
+      for(const line of processTable.split('\n')){const match=line.trim().match(/^(\d+)\s+(.*)$/);if(!match)continue;const pid=Number(match[1]);const command=match[2]!;
+        if(pid!==process.pid&&(command.includes(config.root+'/current/')||command.includes(config.root+'/releases/'))&&!allowedPids.includes(pid))throw Error('upgrade_unregistered_process');
+      }
+    },
+    async service(label, signal) { const r = await run(['print', `${domain}/${label}`], signal);
+      if (r.code === 0) return 'running';
+      // launchctl's fixed absence exit code; other failures are unknown, not permission to bootstrap.
+      if (r.code === 113 && /Could not find service/.test(r.text)) return 'absent';
+      throw Error('upgrade_backend_unknown');
+    },
+    async stop(label, signal) { if ((await run(['bootout', `${domain}/${label}`], signal)).code !== 0) throw Error('upgrade_stop_unknown'); },
+    async start(plist, signal) { if ((await run(['bootstrap', domain, plist], signal)).code !== 0) throw Error('upgrade_restart_unknown'); },
+  };
+}
+export function upgradeRpc(socketPath: string, method: 'deployment.identity' | 'daemon.drain' | 'health', signal: AbortSignal): Promise<unknown> {
+  if (signal.aborted) return Promise.reject(Error('upgrade_cancelled'));
+  const info = lstatSync(socketPath); if (!info.isSocket() || realpathSync(socketPath) !== socketPath || process.getuid && info.uid !== process.getuid()) return Promise.reject(Error('upgrade_unsafe_socket'));
+  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
+    const socket = connect(socketPath); let buffer = Buffer.alloc(0); let settled = false;
+    const finish = (error?: Error, value?: unknown) => { if (settled) return; settled = true; clearTimeout(timer); signal.removeEventListener('abort', abort); socket.destroy(); if (error) reject(error); else resolve(value); };
+    const abort = () => finish(Error('upgrade_cancelled')); const timer = setTimeout(() => finish(Error('upgrade_rpc_unknown')), 15000);
+    signal.addEventListener('abort', abort, { once: true }); if (signal.aborted) abort();
+    socket.on('error', () => finish(Error('upgrade_rpc_unknown'))); socket.on('close', () => finish(Error('upgrade_rpc_unknown')));
+    socket.on('connect', () => { if (settled) return; const body = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ id: 1, method, ...(method === 'daemon.drain' ? { params: { timeout_ms: 10000 } } : {}) })); const header = Buffer.alloc(4); header.writeUInt32BE(body.length); socket.write(Buffer.concat([header, body])); });
+    socket.on('data', chunk => { if (buffer.length + chunk.length > 65536) { finish(Error('upgrade_rpc_invalid')); return; }
+      buffer = Buffer.concat([buffer, chunk]); if (buffer.length < 4) return; const size = buffer.readUInt32BE();
+      if (size > 65532 || buffer.length > size + 4) { finish(Error('upgrade_rpc_invalid')); return; } if (buffer.length < size + 4) return;
+      try { const result = z.object({ id: z.literal(1), ok: z.literal(true), result: z.unknown() }).strict().parse(JSON.parse(buffer.subarray(4).toString())); finish(undefined, result.result); } catch { finish(Error('upgrade_rpc_invalid')); }
+    });
+  });

+const ReleaseManifest = z.object({ schema_version: z.literal(1), target: UpgradeTargetSchema,
+  files: z.array(z.object({ path: z.string(), mode: z.number(), sha256: z.string().regex(/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/) }).strict()).min(1).max(20000) }).strict();
+export function verifyRelease(root: string, digest: string): z.infer<typeof ReleaseManifest> {
+  const release = join(root, 'releases', digest); privateDirectory(release);
+  const manifest = ReleaseManifest.parse(JSON.parse(readPrivate(join(release, '.osstack-release.json'), 8 * 1024 * 1024).toString()));
+  if (manifest.target.build_digest !== digest) throw Error('upgrade_release_mismatch');
+  const inventory: string[] = [];
+  const walk = (directory: string, prefix = ''): void => { for (const name of readdirSync(directory)) { const path = join(directory,name); const relative = prefix + name; const info = lstatSync(path); if (info.isDirectory() && !info.isSymbolicLink()) walk(path,relative+'/'); else inventory.push(relative); if (inventory.length > 20001) throw Error('upgrade_release_limit'); } };
+  walk(release);
+  if (upgradeCanonical(inventory.sort()) !== upgradeCanonical([...manifest.files.map(f=>f.path),'.osstack-release.json'].sort())
+    || sha256(upgradeCanonical([...manifest.files].sort((a,b)=>a.path < b.path ? -1 : a.path > b.path ? 1 : 0))) !== digest) throw Error('upgrade_release_inventory');
+  for (const file of manifest.files) {
+    if (!file.path || file.path.split('/').some(p => !p || p === '.' || p === '..') || file.path.startsWith('/')) throw Error('upgrade_release_path');
+    const path = join(release, file.path); const info = lstatSync(path);
+    if (realpathSync(path) !== path || !info.isFile() || info.nlink !== 1 || (info.mode & 0o777) !== file.mode || process.getuid && info.uid !== process.getuid()
+      || sha256(readFileSync(path)) !== file.sha256) throw Error('upgrade_release_changed');
+  }
+  return manifest;

+export function managedPlist(config: ManagedInstallation, consumer: ManagedInstallation['consumers'][number]): string {
+  const xml = (s: string) => s.replaceAll('&','&amp;').replaceAll('<','&lt;').replaceAll('>','&gt;').replaceAll('"','&quot;').replaceAll("'",'&apos;');
+  return `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?><!DOCTYPE plist PUBLIC "-//Apple//DTD PLIST 1.0//EN" "http://www.apple.com/DTDs/PropertyList-1.0.dtd"><plist version="1.0"><dict><key>Label</key><string>${xml(consumer.label)}</string><key>ProgramArguments</key><array><string>${xml(config.node)}</string><string>${xml(join(config.root,'current',config.entry))}</string><string>serve</string></array><key>EnvironmentVariables</key><dict><key>OSSTACK_HOME</key><string>${xml(consumer.home)}</string><key>OSSTACK_SOCKET</key><string>${xml(consumer.socket)}</string><key>OSSTACK_MANAGED_INSTALLATION</key><string>${xml(config.root)}</string></dict><key>RunAtLoad</key><true/><key>KeepAlive</key><false/></dict></plist>`;

+/** Called before opening a managed daemon's DB. Managed releases cannot serve unregistered homes. */
+export function managedStartupIdentity(home: string, entry: string | undefined): { deployment_kind: 'npm'; build_digest: string; build_evidence: 'host_manifest'; version: string; protocol_version: number; database_schema: number } | null {
+  const markerPath=join(home,'managed-consumer.json');

+  for (let depth = 0; depth < 16; depth++) { if (existsSync(join(probe, '.osstack-release.json'))) { release = probe; break; } const parent = dirname(probe); if (parent === probe) break; probe = parent; }

+  // Dynamic import is not available before startup; caller only uses validated factory below.
+  return managedIdentityForConfig(config, root, home, entry);

+function managedIdentityForConfig(raw: unknown, root: string, home: string, entry: string) {
+  const config = managedInstallationParser(raw);
+  if (realpathSync(process.execPath) !== config.node || sha256(readFileSync(config.node)) !== config.node_sha256) throw Error('upgrade_node_changed'); if (config.root !== root || realpathSync(join(root,'current',config.entry)) !== realpathSync(entry)) throw Error('upgrade_wrong_active_release');

+  if (upgradeCanonical(registry) !== upgradeCanonical(consumer)) throw Error('upgrade_consumer_changed');

+    if (permit.plan_digest !== started.plan_digest || permit.build_digest !== digest) throw Error('upgrade_start_blocked');
+  }

+/** Metadata is checked against the running code and opened DB, never used to override their version/schema. */
+export function verifyManagedRuntime(expected: NonNullable<ReturnType<typeof managedStartupIdentity>>, actual: {version:string;protocol_version:number;database_schema:number}) {

+/** Only call while holding the same home daemon mutex; a live or changed socket is never removed. */
+export async function removeAbandonedUpgradeSocket(socketPath:string,signal:AbortSignal):Promise<void>{

+import { UpgradeJournal } from './upgrade-journal.js';
+import { coordinateUpgrade } from './upgrade-coordinator.js';
+import { ManagedNativeUpgradePort } from './upgrade-native-port.js';
+import { provisionManagedInstallation } from './upgrade-managed-provision.js';
+import { upgradeDigest } from './upgrade-plan.js';
+export const NATIVE_UPGRADE_HELP = `homes prepare-managed-installation --request FILE --approve-registry SHA256 --artifact FILE --artifact-sha256 SHA256
+homes apply-upgrade --journal DIR --digest SHA256 --group ID --registry FILE --registry-sha256 SHA256 --artifact FILE --artifact-sha256 SHA256 --approve-plan SHA256
+homes reconcile-upgrade (same flags) --approve-history SHA256
+Managed launchd installations only. Explicit plan approval authorizes forward installation; planning does not.
+Reconciliation requires current upgrade-status records digest, never restores a database, and stops on unknown observations.`;
+export async function handleNativeUpgradeCommand(args: string[], io: {stdout?:(s:string)=>void;stderr?:(s:string)=>void} = {}): Promise<number> {
+  const out = io.stdout ?? console.log; const err = io.stderr ?? console.error;
+  if (args.at(-1) === '--help') { out(NATIVE_UPGRADE_HELP); return 0; }
+  let port: ManagedNativeUpgradePort | undefined;
+  try {
+    const command = args[0]; if (!['apply-upgrade','reconcile-upgrade','prepare-managed-installation'].includes(command!)) throw Error('upgrade_invalid_command');
+    const required = command === 'prepare-managed-installation' ? ['--request','--approve-registry','--artifact','--artifact-sha256'] : ['--journal','--digest','--group','--registry','--registry-sha256','--artifact','--artifact-sha256','--approve-plan',...(command === 'reconcile-upgrade' ? ['--approve-history'] : [])];
+    const flags = new Map<string,string>();
+    for (let i=1;i<args.length;i++) { const key=args[i]!; const value=args[++i]; if (!required.includes(key) || flags.has(key) || !value || value.startsWith('--') || value.length>4096) throw Error('upgrade_invalid_arguments'); flags.set(key,value); }
+    if (flags.size !== required.length) throw Error('upgrade_invalid_arguments');
+    if (command === 'prepare-managed-installation') { out(JSON.stringify(await provisionManagedInstallation({request:flags.get('--request')!,approvedRegistryDigest:flags.get('--approve-registry')!,artifact:flags.get('--artifact')!,artifactDigest:flags.get('--artifact-sha256')!},AbortSignal.timeout(300000)))); return 0; }
+    if (flags.get('--digest') !== flags.get('--approve-plan')) throw Error('upgrade_explicit_approval_required');
+    const journal = new UpgradeJournal(flags.get('--journal')!); const plan = await journal.plan(flags.get('--digest')!); const group=plan.plan.groups.find(g=>g.group_id===flags.get('--group'));
+    if (!group) throw Error('upgrade_group_missing');
+    port = new ManagedNativeUpgradePort({registry:flags.get('--registry')!,registryDigest:flags.get('--registry-sha256')!,artifact:flags.get('--artifact')!,artifactDigest:flags.get('--artifact-sha256')!,approvedPlanDigest:flags.get('--approve-plan')!});
+    const initialStatus=await journal.status(plan.digest,group.group_id);
+    if(command==='apply-upgrade'&&initialStatus.completed){out(JSON.stringify({state:'verified',history_digest:upgradeDigest(initialStatus.records),reason:'inspect_native_fence_before_new_plan'}));return 0;}
+    const signal=AbortSignal.timeout(Math.min(plan.plan.budget.max_total_ms,300000));
+    if (command==='reconcile-upgrade') { out(JSON.stringify(await port.reconcileForward(plan,group,journal,flags.get('--approve-history')!,signal))); return 0; }
+    const result=await coordinateUpgrade({journal,plan,groupId:group.group_id,port,signal});
+    if(result.state==='verified'&&result.reason===null) await port.complete();
+    const status=await journal.status(plan.digest,group.group_id); out(JSON.stringify({...result,history_digest:upgradeDigest(status.records)})); return result.state==='verified'?0:2;
+  } catch(error) { err(error instanceof Error && /^upgrade_[a-z_]+$/.test(error.message)?error.message:'upgrade_unavailable'); return 1; }
+  finally { await port?.close(); }
+}

+import { createHash, randomBytes } from 'node:crypto';
+import { constants, closeSync, fstatSync, lstatSync, openSync, readFileSync, readSync, readdirSync, realpathSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { link, mkdir, open, rename, symlink, unlink } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { dirname, isAbsolute, join, resolve } from 'node:path';
+import { z } from 'zod';
+import { UpgradeDigest, UpgradeId, UpgradeTargetSchema, upgradeCanonical } from './upgrade-plan.js';
+export const absolute = z.string().min(1).max(4096).refine(isAbsolute);
+export const ManagedInstallationSchema = z.object({ schema_version: z.literal(1), backend: z.literal('launchd'),
+  installation_id: UpgradeId, root: absolute, node: absolute, node_sha256: UpgradeDigest,
+  entry: z.string().regex(/^[A-Za-z0-9_./-]+$/).refine(safeRelative),
+  consumers: z.array(z.object({ home_id: z.string().regex(/^home_[a-f0-9]{32}$/), home: absolute, socket: absolute,
+    label: z.string().regex(/^ai\.osstack\.managed\.[A-Za-z0-9._-]{1,100}$/) }).strict()).min(1).max(32),
+}).strict().superRefine((v, ctx) => {
+  for (const values of [v.consumers.map(c => c.home_id), v.consumers.map(c => c.home), v.consumers.map(c => c.socket), v.consumers.map(c => c.label)])
+    if (new Set(values).size !== values.length) ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', message: 'duplicate_consumer' });
+});
+export type ManagedInstallation = z.infer<typeof ManagedInstallationSchema>;
+export const ReleaseBundleSchema = z.object({ schema_version: z.literal(1), target: UpgradeTargetSchema,
+  files: z.array(z.object({ path: z.string().max(1024).refine(safeRelative), mode: z.union([z.literal(0o600), z.literal(0o700)]),
+    content_base64: z.string().max(24 * 1024 * 1024).regex(/^(?:[A-Za-z0-9+/]{4})*(?:[A-Za-z0-9+/]{2}==|[A-Za-z0-9+/]{3}=)?$/) }).strict()).min(1).max(20000),
+}).strict().superRefine((v, ctx) => {
+  if (new Set(v.files.map(f => f.path.toLowerCase())).size !== v.files.length) ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', message: 'duplicate_bundle_path' });
+  if (v.files.some(f => f.path === '.osstack-release.json')) ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', message: 'reserved_bundle_path' });
+});
+export type ReleaseBundle = z.infer<typeof ReleaseBundleSchema>;
+export function safeRelative(path: string): boolean {
+  return path.length > 0 && /^[A-Za-z0-9_./@+-]+$/.test(path) && !isAbsolute(path) && path.split('/').every(p => p !== '' && p !== '.' && p !== '..');
+}
+export function sha256(bytes: string | Buffer): string { return createHash('sha256').update(bytes).digest('hex'); }
+/** Canonical same-UID private roots and regular files; no ancestor or final symlinks. */
+export function privateDirectory(path: string): void {
+  const info = lstatSync(path);
+  if (realpathSync(path) !== resolve(path) || !info.isDirectory() || (info.mode & 0o077) || process.getuid && info.uid !== process.getuid()) throw Error('upgrade_unsafe_directory');
+}
+export function readPrivate(path: string, limit = 262144): Buffer {
+  privateDirectory(dirname(path)); const fd = openSync(path, constants.O_RDONLY | constants.O_NOFOLLOW | constants.O_NONBLOCK);
+  try {
+    const before = fstatSync(fd);
+    if (!before.isFile() || (before.nlink !== 1 && before.nlink !== 2) || before.size > limit || before.mode & 0o077 || process.getuid && before.uid !== process.getuid()) throw Error('upgrade_unsafe_file');
+    if(before.nlink===2){const names=readdirSync(dirname(path));if(names.length>22000)throw Error('upgrade_file_links');let matches=0;for(const name of names.filter(name=>/^\.publish-[a-f0-9]{32}$/.test(name))){const other=lstatSync(join(dirname(path),name));if(other.isFile()&&other.ino===before.ino&&other.dev===before.dev)matches++;}if(matches!==1)throw Error('upgrade_file_links');}
+    const bytes = Buffer.alloc(Math.min(limit + 1, before.size + 1)); let count = 0;
+    while (count < bytes.length) { const size = readSync(fd, bytes, count, bytes.length - count, count); if (!size) break; count += size; }
+    const after = fstatSync(fd);
+    if (count > limit || before.size !== after.size || before.mtimeMs !== after.mtimeMs || before.ctimeMs !== after.ctimeMs) throw Error('upgrade_file_changed');
+    return bytes.subarray(0, count);
+  } finally { closeSync(fd); }
+}
+export async function syncDirectory(path: string): Promise<void> {
+  const fd = await open(path, constants.O_RDONLY | constants.O_NOFOLLOW); try { await fd.sync(); } finally { await fd.close(); }
+}
+export async function publishPrivate(path: string, value: unknown): Promise<void> {return publishPrivateBytes(path,Buffer.from(upgradeCanonical(value)));}
+export async function publishPrivateBytes(path:string,bytes:Buffer):Promise<void>{
+  privateDirectory(dirname(path)); const temp=join(dirname(path),`.publish-${randomBytes(16).toString('hex')}`);
+  const fd = await open(temp, constants.O_CREAT | constants.O_EXCL | constants.O_WRONLY | constants.O_NOFOLLOW, 0o600);
+  try { await fd.writeFile(bytes); await fd.sync(); } finally { await fd.close(); }
+  try { await link(temp,path); } finally { await unlink(temp); }
+  await syncDirectory(dirname(path));
+}
+export function loadInstallation(path: string, digest: string): ManagedInstallation {
+  UpgradeDigest.parse(digest); const bytes = readPrivate(path);
+  if (sha256(bytes) !== digest) throw Error('upgrade_registry_changed');
+  const result = ManagedInstallationSchema.parse(JSON.parse(bytes.toString('utf8')));
+  if (path !== join(result.root, 'installation.json')) throw Error('upgrade_registry_location');
+  privateDirectory(result.root);
+  if (realpathSync(result.node) !== result.node || !lstatSync(result.node).isFile() || sha256(readFileSync(result.node)) !== result.node_sha256) throw Error('upgrade_node_changed');
+  for (const consumer of result.consumers) { privateDirectory(consumer.home); if (dirname(consumer.socket) !== consumer.home) throw Error('upgrade_socket_location');
+    const identity = z.object({schema_version:z.literal(1),home_id:z.literal(consumer.home_id)}).strict().parse(JSON.parse(readPrivate(join(consumer.home,'deployment-identity.json'),512).toString()));
+    if (identity.home_id !== consumer.home_id) throw Error('upgrade_home_identity_changed');
+  }
+  return result;
+}
+export function bundleBuildDigest(files: ReleaseBundle['files']): string {
+  return sha256(upgradeCanonical([...files].map(file => ({ path: file.path, mode: file.mode, sha256: sha256(Buffer.from(file.content_base64, 'base64')) })).sort((a,b) => a.path < b.path ? -1 : a.path > b.path ? 1 : 0)));
+}
+export function loadBundle(path: string, digest: string): ReleaseBundle {
+  UpgradeDigest.parse(digest); const bytes = readPrivate(path, 128 * 1024 * 1024);
+  if (sha256(bytes) !== digest) throw Error('upgrade_artifact_changed');
+  const bundle = ReleaseBundleSchema.parse(JSON.parse(bytes.toString('utf8')));
+  if (bundleBuildDigest(bundle.files) !== bundle.target.build_digest) throw Error('upgrade_build_digest_mismatch');
+  return bundle;
+}
+export async function stageRelease(root: string, bundle: ReleaseBundle, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<string> {
+  privateDirectory(root); const releases = join(root, 'releases'); await mkdir(releases, { mode: 0o700 }).catch(e => { if (e.code !== 'EEXIST') throw e; }); privateDirectory(releases);
+  const target = join(releases, bundle.target.build_digest); const temporary = join(releases, `.stage-${randomBytes(16).toString('hex')}`);
+  await mkdir(temporary, { mode: 0o700 });
+  for (const file of bundle.files) {
+    signal.throwIfAborted(); const path = join(temporary, file.path); await mkdir(dirname(path), { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
+    const fd = await open(path, constants.O_CREAT | constants.O_EXCL | constants.O_WRONLY | constants.O_NOFOLLOW, file.mode);
+    try { await fd.writeFile(Buffer.from(file.content_base64, 'base64')); await fd.sync(); } finally { await fd.close(); }
+    await syncDirectory(dirname(path));
+  }
+  await publishPrivate(join(temporary, '.osstack-release.json'), { schema_version: 1, target: bundle.target, files: bundle.files.map(f => ({ path: f.path, mode: f.mode, sha256: sha256(Buffer.from(f.content_base64, 'base64')) })) });
+  signal.throwIfAborted(); await rename(temporary, target); await syncDirectory(releases); return target;
+}
+export async function switchRelease(root: string, release: string, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<void> {
+  privateDirectory(root); if (dirname(release) !== join(root, 'releases')) throw Error('upgrade_release_location');
+  const temp = join(root, `.current-${randomBytes(16).toString('hex')}`); await symlink(release, temp);
+  try { signal.throwIfAborted(); await rename(temp, join(root, 'current')); await syncDirectory(root); } finally { await unlink(temp).catch(() => {}); }
+}
+
+/** On explicit provision resume, finish only this exact published inode's temporary link. */
+export async function finishPrivatePublication(path:string):Promise<void>{const target=lstatSync(path);for(const name of readdirSync(dirname(path)).filter(name=>/^\.publish-[a-f0-9]{32}$/.test(name))){const other=lstatSync(join(dirname(path),name));if(other.isFile()&&other.ino===target.ino&&other.dev===target.dev)await unlink(join(dirname(path),name));}await syncDirectory(dirname(path));}
