import { join } from 'node:path';
import { assertSourceContextDispatch, type SourceContextBinding } from './source-context-fence.js';
import { z } from 'zod';
import { executionContextDigest, type ExecutionContextEntry, type ExecutionContextSource } from '@osstack/knowledge';
import type { ExecutionContextLoader } from './execution-context.js';
import { createHostSourceObservationService, readPrivateSourcePolicy, type HostSourceObservationService } from './source-observation-host.js';
const SourceContextPolicy=z.object({schema_version:z.literal(1),repo_path:z.string().max(4096),default_tenant:z.literal(true),source_ids:z.array(z.string().regex(/^[a-z][a-z0-9_-]{0,63}$/)).min(1).max(4),max_bytes:z.number().int().min(1).max(8192)}).strict().refine(v=>new Set(v.source_ids).size===v.source_ids.length);
type Binding=SourceContextBinding;
/** Explicit host opt-in, never inferred from a repository pack or source registration. */
export function createHostSourceContext(home:string,service:HostSourceObservationService=createHostSourceObservationService(home)){
 const policyPath=join(home,'source-context.json'),registryPath=join(home,'source-registry.json');
 const load:ExecutionContextLoader=async(_task,{workspacePath,signal})=>{
  const file=await readPrivateSourcePolicy(policyPath);if(!file)return [];const policy=SourceContextPolicy.parse(file.value);if(policy.repo_path!==workspacePath)throw Error('source_context_scope_mismatch');const registry=await readPrivateSourcePolicy(registryPath,131072);if(!registry)throw Error('source_context_registry_missing');
  const entries:ExecutionContextEntry[]=[];let bytes=0;
  for(const id of policy.source_ids){if(signal.aborted)throw Error('source_cancelled');const {snapshot,observation}=await service.observe({repo_path:workspacePath,source_id:id,default_tenant:true},signal);if(snapshot.state!=='ready'||!observation||observation.state!=='ready'||!snapshot.provenance)throw Error('source_context_not_ready');const p=snapshot.provenance;
   const binding:Binding={policy_digest:file.digest,registry_digest:registry.digest,source_id:id,generation:p.version,revision:p.revision,record_revision:observation.record_revision,manifest_digest:p.manifest_digest,content_digest:p.content_digest};
   // One bounded JSON entry per source preserves its complete snapshot; the normal prompt assembler may omit it and records that decision.
   const text=JSON.stringify({source_id:id,generation:p.version,revision:p.revision,entries:snapshot.entries});bytes+=Buffer.byteLength(text);if(bytes>policy.max_bytes)throw Error('source_context_budget_exceeded');
   entries.push({id:`source:${id}`,source_kind:'data_source',source_ref:JSON.stringify(binding),source_digest:p.content_digest,text_sha256:executionContextDigest(text),scope:{repo_path:workspacePath,tenant:'default'},tier:'optional',text,verified_at_ms:p.verified_at_ms,expires_at_ms:p.expires_at_ms,evidence_kind:'pinned_local_snapshot'});
  }
  if((await readPrivateSourcePolicy(policyPath))?.digest!==file.digest||(await readPrivateSourcePolicy(registryPath,131072))?.digest!==registry.digest)throw Error('source_policy_changed');return entries;
 };
 async function validate(entries:readonly ExecutionContextSource[],signal:AbortSignal){
  for(const entry of entries.filter(e=>e.source_kind==='data_source')){const binding=JSON.parse(entry.source_ref) as Binding;const file=await readPrivateSourcePolicy(policyPath);if(!file||file.digest!==binding.policy_digest)throw Error('source_policy_changed');const policy=SourceContextPolicy.parse(file.value);if(policy.repo_path!==entry.scope.repo_path||!policy.source_ids.includes(binding.source_id))throw Error('source_context_scope_mismatch');
   const scope={repo_path:entry.scope.repo_path,source_id:binding.source_id,default_tenant:true as const};const {snapshot,observation:current}=await service.observe(scope,signal);const p=snapshot.provenance;
   if(signal.aborted||snapshot.state!=='ready'||!p||p.version!==binding.generation||p.revision!==binding.revision||p.manifest_digest!==binding.manifest_digest||p.content_digest!==binding.content_digest||current?.state!=='ready'||current.record_revision!==binding.record_revision)throw Error('source_context_changed_before_invoke');
   if((await readPrivateSourcePolicy(policyPath))?.digest!==binding.policy_digest)throw Error('source_policy_changed');
  }
 }
 return {load,validate,validateSync:(entries:readonly ExecutionContextSource[],signal:AbortSignal)=>assertSourceContextDispatch(home,entries,service,signal)};
}

FULL CONTEXT FILE packages/cli/src/source-context-fence.ts
import { constants, openSync, closeSync, readSync, fstatSync, lstatSync, realpathSync } from 'node:fs';
import { dirname, isAbsolute, join, resolve, relative, sep } from 'node:path';
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import { LocalSourceManifestSchema, localSourceManifestDigest } from './local-data-source-registry.js';
import type { HostSourceObservationService } from './source-observation-host.js';
import type { ExecutionContextSource } from '@osstack/knowledge';
const digest=(bytes:Buffer)=>createHash('sha256').update(bytes).digest('hex');
export function readSourceContextFile(path:string,maximum:number,privateFile:boolean):Buffer{
 if(!isAbsolute(path)||resolve(path)!==path||realpathSync(path)!==path)throw Error('source_dispatch_path_changed');
 if(privateFile&&(lstatSync(dirname(path)).mode&0o077)!==0)throw Error('source_dispatch_directory_not_private');
 if(privateFile)for(let parent=dirname(path);;parent=dirname(parent)){const s=lstatSync(parent),sticky=s.uid===0&&(s.mode&0o1000)!==0;if(!s.isDirectory()||s.isSymbolicLink()||(s.uid!==0&&s.uid!==process.getuid?.())||((s.mode&0o022)!==0&&!sticky))throw Error('source_dispatch_unsafe_ancestor');if(dirname(parent)===parent)break;}
 const fd=openSync(path,constants.O_RDONLY|constants.O_NOFOLLOW|constants.O_NONBLOCK);try{const before=fstatSync(fd);if(!before.isFile()||before.size>maximum||(privateFile&&(before.nlink!==1||before.uid!==process.getuid?.()||(before.mode&0o077)!==0)))throw Error('source_dispatch_file_invalid');const bytes=Buffer.alloc(maximum+1);let length=0;while(length<bytes.length){const n=readSync(fd,bytes,length,bytes.length-length,null);if(!n)break;length+=n;}const after=fstatSync(fd),current=lstatSync(path);if(length!==before.size||before.mtimeMs!==after.mtimeMs||before.ctimeMs!==after.ctimeMs||current.ino!==before.ino||current.dev!==before.dev||current.isSymbolicLink()||current.mtimeMs!==before.mtimeMs||current.ctimeMs!==before.ctimeMs)throw Error('source_dispatch_file_changed');return bytes.subarray(0,length);}finally{closeSync(fd);}
}
export interface SourceContextBinding {policy_digest:string;registry_digest:string;source_id:string;generation:string;revision:number;record_revision:number;manifest_digest:string;content_digest:string}
/** No await: the final adapter dispatch follows this bounded host-authority check in the same JS turn. */
export function assertSourceContextDispatch(home:string,entries:readonly ExecutionContextSource[],service:HostSourceObservationService,signal:AbortSignal):void{
 const sources=entries.filter(e=>e.source_kind==='data_source');if(!sources.length)return;if(sources.length>4||signal.aborted)throw Error('source_context_dispatch_invalid');
 const policyPath=join(home,'source-context.json'),registryPath=join(home,'source-registry.json');const policyBytes=readSourceContextFile(policyPath,32768,true),registryBytes=readSourceContextFile(registryPath,131072,true);const config=JSON.parse(registryBytes.toString()) as {schema_version?:unknown;registrations?:unknown};if(config.schema_version!==1||!Array.isArray(config.registrations)||config.registrations.length>32)throw Error('source_dispatch_registry_invalid');
 const now=Date.now();let expires=Infinity;for(const entry of sources){const binding=JSON.parse(entry.source_ref) as SourceContextBinding;if(binding.policy_digest!==digest(policyBytes)||binding.registry_digest!==digest(registryBytes))throw Error('source_context_authority_changed');const rows=config.registrations.filter((r:unknown)=>r!==null&&typeof r==='object'&&'manifest' in r&&(r as {manifest?:{id?:unknown}}).manifest?.id===binding.source_id);if(rows.length!==1)throw Error('source_context_registration_changed');const row=rows[0] as {manifest:unknown;source_root:unknown;manifest_digest:unknown;content_digest:unknown};const manifest=LocalSourceManifestSchema.parse(row.manifest);if(manifest.repo_path!==entry.scope.repo_path||manifest.version!==binding.generation||row.manifest_digest!==binding.manifest_digest||row.content_digest!==binding.content_digest||localSourceManifestDigest(manifest)!==binding.manifest_digest||manifest.verified_at_ms>now||manifest.expires_at_ms<=now||manifest.verified_at_ms+manifest.fresh_for_ms<now||typeof row.source_root!=='string')throw Error('source_context_registration_changed');
  expires=Math.min(expires,manifest.expires_at_ms,manifest.verified_at_ms+manifest.fresh_for_ms);
  const root=realpathSync(row.source_root),path=resolve(root,manifest.file),rel=relative(root,path);if(isAbsolute(rel)||rel==='..'||rel.startsWith(`..${sep}`))throw Error('source_dispatch_path_changed');if(digest(readSourceContextFile(path,manifest.max_bytes,false))!==binding.content_digest)throw Error('source_context_content_changed');
  const current=service.status({repo_path:entry.scope.repo_path,source_id:binding.source_id,default_tenant:true});if(current?.state!=='ready'||current.record_revision!==binding.record_revision||current.accepted.generation!==binding.generation||current.accepted.revision!==binding.revision||current.accepted.content_digest!==binding.content_digest||current.accepted.manifest_digest!==binding.manifest_digest)throw Error('source_context_ledger_changed');
 }
 // Later source reads cannot hide revocation of an earlier source in the shared registry.
 const dispatchedAt=Date.now();if(dispatchedAt<now||dispatchedAt>=expires||signal.aborted||digest(readSourceContextFile(policyPath,32768,true))!==digest(policyBytes)||digest(readSourceContextFile(registryPath,131072,true))!==digest(registryBytes))throw Error('source_context_authority_changed');
}

FULL CONTEXT FILE packages/core/src/state/attention-observer.ts
import { classifyAttentionWait, type WaitInput } from "../attention.js";
import { realpathSync } from "node:fs";
import { projectAttentionRun } from "./attention-run-projection.js";
import {
  attentionDigest,
  type AttentionStore,
  type AttentionOwner,
  type AttentionScope,
} from "./attention-store.js";
interface Subject {
  scope_id: string;
  kind: string;
  subject_id: string;
  binding_hash: string;
  source_version: number;
  ordinal_cursor: number;
  admitted: number;
  discovered_at: number;
  sampled_at: number;
  generation: number;
  payload_json: string;
}
interface Source {
  session_id: string;
  run_id: string;
  request_sha256: string;
  agent_id: string;
  native_session_id: string | null;
  repo_path: string;
  version: number;
  state: string;
}
interface Turn {
  turn_id: string;
  ordinal: number;
  decision_json: string | null;
  state: string;
}
/** Bounded synchronous transactions; no adapter, network, or model port. */
export interface AttentionObserverStepOptions { maxSubjects?: number; maxEvents?: number; maxDurationMs?: number }
export interface AttentionObserverStepResult { passComplete: boolean; scopesVisited: number; subjectsSampled: number; eventQuotaUsed: number; durationMs: number }
export class AttentionObserver {
  private pass: { scopes: AttentionScope[]; current?: { scope: AttentionScope; subjects: Array<{ kind: string; subject_id: string }>; next: number } } | undefined;
  /** Each admitted source gets one share per pass; backlog never jumps the queue.
   * Persisted discovery/run/ordinal cursors remain the recovery authority. */
  step(options: AttentionObserverStepOptions = {}, now = Date.now()): AttentionObserverStepResult {
    const maxSubjects = options.maxSubjects ?? 25; const maxEvents = options.maxEvents ?? 200; const maxDuration = options.maxDurationMs ?? 5;
    if (!Number.isFinite(now) || !Number.isSafeInteger(maxSubjects) || maxSubjects < 1 || maxSubjects > 100
      || !Number.isSafeInteger(maxEvents) || maxEvents < 1 || maxEvents > 200 || !Number.isFinite(maxDuration) || maxDuration <= 0 || maxDuration > 1000) throw Error("invalid_step_budget");
    this.store.assertOwner(this.owner);
    const started = performance.now(); let scopesVisited = 0; let subjectsSampled = 0; let eventQuotaUsed = 0;
    this.pass ??= { scopes: this.store.listScopes().sort((a, b) => a.sampled_at - b.sampled_at || a.scope_id.localeCompare(b.scope_id)) };
    const pass = this.pass;
    if (!pass.current) {
      const queuedScope = pass.scopes.shift();
      const scope = queuedScope ? this.store.getScope(queuedScope.scope_id) : undefined;
      if (scope) {
        scopesVisited++;
        let discovered = false;
        try { discovered = this.store.write(this.owner, () => this.discover(scope, now)); }
        catch (error) {
          if (error instanceof Error && error.message === "stale_owner") throw error;
          this.store.write(this.owner, () => this.store.db.prepare("UPDATE attention_scopes SET error='source_error',scan_complete=0 WHERE scope_id=?").run(scope.scope_id));
        }
        pass.current = { scope, subjects: discovered ? this.store.db.prepare("SELECT kind,subject_id FROM attention_subjects WHERE scope_id=? AND admitted=1 AND kind IN ('session','run') ORDER BY sampled_at,kind,subject_id").all(scope.scope_id) as Array<{ kind: string; subject_id: string }> : [], next: 0 };
      }
    }
    const current = pass.current;
    if (current) {
      const slots = Math.min(maxSubjects, maxEvents, current.subjects.length - current.next);
      const eventLimit = slots > 0 ? Math.floor(maxEvents / slots) : 0;
      const sliceStart = current.next;
      try {
        // One disk-space preflight/commit per bounded slice. Nested per-subject
        // savepoints still isolate malformed source facts; owner/scope/release
        // checks remain inside the transaction and the clock is checked before
        // every subject, never only at batch entry.
        if (slots > 0) this.store.write(this.owner, () => {
          while (current.next < current.subjects.length && subjectsSampled < slots && performance.now() - started < maxDuration) {
            const key = current.subjects[current.next++]!;
            this.store.write(this.owner, () => {
              const persisted = this.store.getScope(current.scope.scope_id);
              const task = this.store.db.prepare("SELECT created_at FROM tasks WHERE task_id=? AND tenant_id IS NULL").get(current.scope.task_id) as { created_at: number } | undefined;
              if (!persisted || persisted.task_id !== current.scope.task_id || persisted.repo_path !== current.scope.repo_path || !task
                || attentionDigest([this.owner.home_id, persisted.task_id, persisted.repo_path, task.created_at]) !== persisted.scope_id) {
                if (persisted) this.store.db.prepare("UPDATE attention_scopes SET error='source_binding_conflict',scan_complete=0 WHERE scope_id=?").run(persisted.scope_id);
                return;
              }
              const subject = this.store.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM attention_subjects WHERE scope_id=? AND kind=? AND subject_id=? AND admitted=1").get(current.scope.scope_id, key.kind, key.subject_id) as Subject | undefined;
              if (!subject) return; // An explicit release during the pass is never reversed.
              this.sample(current.scope, subject, now, eventLimit);
              subjectsSampled++; eventQuotaUsed += subject.kind === "session" ? Math.min(50, eventLimit) : eventLimit;
            });
          }
        });
      } catch (error) {
        // A transaction rollback also rolls back its scheduling cursor. Otherwise
        // a retry could skip facts that were sampled but never committed.
        current.next = sliceStart;
        throw error;
      }
      if (current.next >= current.subjects.length) delete pass.current;
    }
    const passComplete = pass.scopes.length === 0 && !pass.current;
    if (passComplete) this.pass = undefined;
    return { passComplete, scopesVisited, subjectsSampled, eventQuotaUsed, durationMs: performance.now() - started };
  }
  constructor(readonly store: AttentionStore, readonly owner: AttentionOwner) {}
  tick(now = Date.now()): void {
    if (!Number.isFinite(now)) throw new Error("invalid_clock");
    this.store.assertOwner(this.owner);
    for (const scope of this.store.listScopes()) {
      try {
        this.store.write(this.owner, () => this.scan(scope, now));
      } catch (error) {
        if (error instanceof Error && error.message === "stale_owner")
          throw error;
        this.store.write(this.owner, () =>
          this.store.db
            .prepare(
              "UPDATE attention_scopes SET error=?,scan_complete=0 WHERE scope_id=?"
            )
            .run(
              error instanceof Error
                ? error.message.slice(0, 300)
                : "source_error",
              scope.scope_id
            )
        );
      }
    }
  }
  private scan(scope: AttentionScope, now: number): void {
    if (!this.discover(scope, now)) return;
    const subjects = this.store.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM attention_subjects WHERE scope_id=? AND admitted=1 ORDER BY kind,subject_id").all(scope.scope_id) as Subject[];
    for (const subject of subjects) this.sample(scope, subject, now, 200);
  }
  private discover(scope: AttentionScope, now: number): boolean {
    const db = this.store.db;
    const task = db
      .prepare(
        "SELECT created_at FROM tasks WHERE task_id=? AND tenant_id IS NULL"
      )
      .get(scope.task_id) as { created_at: number } | undefined;
    if (
      !task ||
      attentionDigest([
        this.owner.home_id,
        scope.task_id,
        scope.repo_path,
        task.created_at,
      ]) !== scope.scope_id
    ) {
      db.prepare(
        "UPDATE attention_items SET state='source_missing',revision=revision+1 WHERE scope_id=? AND state='open'"
      ).run(scope.scope_id);
      db.prepare(
        "UPDATE attention_scopes SET error='source_missing',scan_complete=0 WHERE scope_id=?"
      ).run(scope.scope_id);
      db.prepare("UPDATE attention_run_cursors SET gap=COALESCE(gap,'source_missing'),generation=?,updated_at=? WHERE scope_id=?")
        .run(this.owner.generation, now, scope.scope_id);
      db.prepare("UPDATE attention_subjects SET sampled_at=?,generation=?,payload_json=? WHERE scope_id=? AND admitted=1")
        .run(now, this.owner.generation, JSON.stringify({ state: "source_missing", reason: "source_missing",
          process_liveness: "unknown", gap: "history_gap", event_replay: { complete: false, caught_up: false, gap: "source_missing", completeness_scope: "unverified_retention" } }), scope.scope_id);
      return false;
    }
    // A scan advances a durable keyset, then restarts from the beginning. New IDs
    // inserted behind the cursor are picked up on the next pass, never evicted.
    const rows = db
      .prepare(
        `SELECT '0:'||s.session_id AS key,s.session_id AS id,'session' AS kind FROM agent_sessions s JOIN runs r ON r.run_id=s.run_id AND r.task_id=s.task_id
      WHERE s.task_id=? AND r.tenant_id IS NULL AND '0:'||s.session_id>?
      UNION ALL SELECT '1:'||r.run_id,r.run_id,'run' FROM runs r WHERE r.task_id=? AND r.tenant_id IS NULL AND '1:'||r.run_id>?
      ORDER BY key LIMIT 250`
      )
      .all(
        scope.task_id,
        scope.discovery_after,
        scope.task_id,
        scope.discovery_after
      ) as { key: string; id: string; kind: string }[];
    for (const row of rows) {
      if (
        db
          .prepare(
            "SELECT 1 FROM attention_subjects WHERE scope_id=? AND kind=? AND subject_id=? AND admitted=1"
          )
          .get(scope.scope_id, row.kind, row.id)
      )
        continue;
      const count = (
        db
          .prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM attention_subjects WHERE admitted=1")
          .get() as { n: number }
      ).n;
      const prior = db
        .prepare(
          "SELECT payload_json FROM attention_subjects WHERE scope_id=? AND kind=? AND subject_id=?"
        )
        .get(scope.scope_id, row.kind, row.id) as
        | { payload_json: string }
        | undefined;
      if (prior) {
        try {
          // Only explicit retryable discovery states can gain admission later.
          // Missing/corrupt metadata is not evidence that a prior release ended.
          if (!["capacity_exceeded", "binding_unknown", "source_error"].includes(JSON.parse(prior.payload_json).reason)) continue;
        } catch {
          continue;
        }
      }
      let source: Source | undefined;
      let sourceError = false;
      try {
        source =
          row.kind === "session" ? this.source(scope, row.id) : undefined;
      } catch {
        sourceError = true;
      }
      const bound = row.kind === "run" || source !== undefined;
      const admitted = bound && count < 100 ? 1 : 0;
      db.prepare(
        `INSERT INTO attention_subjects(scope_id,kind,subject_id,binding_hash,source_version,admitted,discovered_at,sampled_at,generation,payload_json) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?) ON CONFLICT(scope_id,kind,subject_id) DO UPDATE SET
        admitted=excluded.admitted,binding_hash=excluded.binding_hash,source_version=excluded.source_version,sampled_at=excluded.sampled_at,generation=excluded.generation,payload_json=excluded.payload_json`
      ).run(
        scope.scope_id,
        row.kind,
        row.id,
        source
          ? this.binding(source)
          : attentionDigest([scope.scope_id, row.kind, row.id]),
        source?.version ?? 0,
        admitted,
        now,
        now,
        this.owner.generation,
        JSON.stringify({
          ...(row.kind === "run" ? { event_replay: { complete: false, caught_up: false,
            gap: admitted ? "not_sampled" : "capacity_exceeded", completeness_scope: "unverified_retention" } } : {}),
          state: source?.state ?? "unknown",
          awaiting_decision: source?.state === "awaiting_decision",
          reason: sourceError
            ? "source_error"
            : !bound
            ? "binding_unknown"
            : !admitted
            ? "capacity_exceeded"
            : "not_sampled",
          unresolved_receipts: source ? this.receipts(source.session_id) : [],
        })
      );
    }
    const complete = rows.length < 250;
    const undiscovered = db
      .prepare(
        `SELECT 1 FROM agent_sessions s JOIN runs r ON r.run_id=s.run_id AND r.task_id=s.task_id
      WHERE s.task_id=? AND r.tenant_id IS NULL AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM attention_subjects a WHERE a.scope_id=? AND a.kind='session' AND a.subject_id=s.session_id)
      UNION ALL SELECT 1 FROM runs r WHERE r.task_id=? AND r.tenant_id IS NULL AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM attention_subjects a WHERE a.scope_id=? AND a.kind='run' AND a.subject_id=r.run_id) LIMIT 1`
      )
      .get(scope.task_id, scope.scope_id, scope.task_id, scope.scope_id);

    db.prepare(
      "UPDATE attention_scopes SET discovery_after=?,scan_complete=?,sampled_at=?,deadline=?,generation=?,error=NULL WHERE scope_id=?"
    ).run(
      complete ? "" : rows.at(-1)!.key,
      complete && !undiscovered ? 1 : 0,
      now,
      now + 65000,
      this.owner.generation,
      scope.scope_id
    );
    return true;
  }
  private sample(scope: AttentionScope, subject: Subject, now: number, eventLimit: number): void {
    const db = this.store.db;
    try {
      db.transaction(() => {
        if (subject.kind === "run") {
          projectAttentionRun(this.store, this.owner, scope, subject.subject_id, now, Math.min(200, eventLimit));
          return;
        }
        if (subject.kind !== "session") return;
        const source = this.source(scope, subject.subject_id);
        if (!source) {
          this.store.markMissing(
            this.owner,
            scope.scope_id,
            subject.subject_id
          );
          this.update(subject, now, {
            state: "source_missing",
            reason: "source_missing",
          });
          return;
        }
        if (
          this.binding(source) !== subject.binding_hash ||
          source.version < subject.source_version
        ) {
          this.update(subject, now, {
            state: source.state,
            reason: "source_binding_conflict",
          });
          return;
        }
        const turns = db
          .prepare(
            "SELECT turn_id,ordinal,state,decision_json FROM session_turns WHERE session_id=? AND ordinal>? ORDER BY ordinal LIMIT ?"
          )
          .all(source.session_id, subject.ordinal_cursor, Math.min(50, eventLimit)) as Turn[];
        const latest = db
          .prepare(
            "SELECT turn_id,ordinal,state,decision_json FROM session_turns WHERE session_id=? ORDER BY ordinal DESC LIMIT 1"
          )
          .get(source.session_id) as Turn | undefined;
        let cursor = subject.ordinal_cursor;
        let gap =
          JSON.parse(subject.payload_json).gap === "history_gap" ||
          (latest?.ordinal ?? 0) < cursor;
        for (const turn of turns) {
          if (turn.ordinal !== cursor + 1) gap = true;
          try {
            this.project(scope, source, turn, now);
          } catch {
            gap = true;
          }
          cursor = turn.ordinal;
        }
        if (latest && latest.ordinal <= cursor) {
          try {
            this.project(scope, source, latest, now);
          } catch {
            gap = true;
          }
        }
        const backlogged = latest !== undefined && latest.ordinal > cursor;
        db.prepare(
          "UPDATE attention_subjects SET source_version=?,ordinal_cursor=? WHERE scope_id=? AND kind=? AND subject_id=?"
        ).run(
          source.version,
          cursor,
          scope.scope_id,
          subject.kind,
          subject.subject_id
        );
        const unresolved = this.receipts(source.session_id);
        if (["completed", "failed", "cancelled"].includes(source.state))
          db.prepare(
            "UPDATE attention_items SET state='source_closed',revision=revision+1 WHERE scope_id=? AND session_id=? AND state='open'"
          ).run(scope.scope_id, source.session_id);
        // Only an applied receipt referencing this exact origin turn establishes
        // external continuation. Authorization/risk obligations remain unresolved.
        const items = (
          db
            .prepare(
              "SELECT item_id FROM attention_items WHERE scope_id=? AND session_id=? AND state='open'"
            )
            .all(scope.scope_id, source.session_id) as { item_id: string }[]
        )
          .map((r) => this.store.getItem(scope.scope_id, r.item_id)!)
          .filter(
            (i) =>
              i.session_id === source.session_id &&
              i.state === "open" &&
              !["risk_approval", "task_authorization"].includes(
                i.decision.continuation?.gate_kind ?? ""
              )
          );
        for (const item of items) {
          const applied = db
            .prepare(
              "SELECT message_id FROM session_messages WHERE session_id=? AND turn_id=? AND direction='inbound' AND state='applied' ORDER BY created_at,message_id LIMIT 1"
            )
            .get(source.session_id, item.turn_id) as
            | { message_id: string }
            | undefined;
          if (applied)
            db.prepare(
              "UPDATE attention_items SET state='continued_externally',external_message_id=?,revision=revision+1 WHERE item_id=?"
            ).run(applied.message_id, item.item_id);
        }
        this.update(subject, now, {
          state: source.state,
          awaiting_decision: source.state === "awaiting_decision",
          version: source.version,
          turn_id: latest?.turn_id ?? null,
          process_liveness: "unknown",
          gap: gap ? "history_gap" : "mutable_snapshot_no_complete_event_log",
          reason: backlogged ? "history_backlog" : null,
          unresolved_receipts: unresolved,
          wait: this.wait(source, latest?.turn_id ?? null, now),
        });
      })();
    } catch (error) {
      if (error instanceof Error && error.message === "stale_owner") throw error;
      this.store.assertOwner(this.owner);
      this.update(subject, now, {
        state: "unknown",
        reason: "invalid_source",
        detail:
          error instanceof Error
            ? error.message.slice(0, 120)
            : "invalid_source",
      });
    }
  }
  private source(scope: AttentionScope, id: string): Source | undefined {
    const row = this.store.db
      .prepare(
        `SELECT s.* FROM agent_sessions s JOIN runs r ON r.run_id=s.run_id AND r.task_id=s.task_id JOIN tasks t ON t.task_id=s.task_id
      WHERE s.session_id=? AND s.task_id=? AND r.tenant_id IS NULL AND t.tenant_id IS NULL`
      )
      .get(id, scope.task_id) as Source | undefined;
    try {
      return row && realpathSync(row.repo_path) === scope.repo_path
        ? row
        : undefined;
    } catch (error) {
      if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code === "ENOENT") return undefined;
      throw error;
    }
  }
  private binding(source: Source): string {
    return attentionDigest([
      source.session_id,
      source.run_id,
      source.request_sha256,
      source.agent_id,
      source.repo_path,
    ]);
  }
  private project(
    scope: AttentionScope,
    source: Source,
    turn: Turn,
    now: number
  ): void {
    if (turn.decision_json === null) return;
    if (turn.decision_json.length > 16384)
      throw new Error("oversized_decision");
    this.store.projectDecision(this.owner, {
      scopeId: scope.scope_id,
      sessionId: source.session_id,
      turnId: turn.turn_id,
      sourceVersion: source.version,
      decision: JSON.parse(turn.decision_json),
      now,
    });
  }
  private wait(source: Source, turnId: string | null, now: number) {
    const db = this.store.db;
    const input: WaitInput = {
      state: source.state,
      turnId,
      version: source.version,
      now,
    };
    const g = db
      .prepare(
        "SELECT grant_id,status,expected_turn_id,expected_version,body_json FROM session_continuation_grants WHERE session_id=? AND status='active'"
      )
      .get(source.session_id) as
      | {
          grant_id: string;
          status: string;
          expected_turn_id: string;
          expected_version: number;
          body_json: string;
        }
      | undefined;
    try {
      if (g)
        input.grant = {
          id: g.grant_id,
          status: g.status,
          turnId: g.expected_turn_id,
          version: g.expected_version,
          expires: Date.parse(JSON.parse(g.body_json).expires_at),
        };
    } catch {
      return { reason: "grant_unreadable", owner: "unknown", blocked: true };
    }
    const attempt = db
      .prepare(
        "SELECT a.* FROM session_continuation_attempts a JOIN session_continuation_grants g ON a.grant_id=g.grant_id WHERE g.session_id=? AND a.status IN ('checking','reserved') ORDER BY a.created_at LIMIT 1"
      )
      .get(source.session_id) as
      | {
          status: string;
          source_turn_id: string;
          source_version: number;
          reservation_version: number | null;
          message_id: string | null;
        }
      | undefined;
    if (attempt)
      input.attempt = {
        state: attempt.status,
        sourceTurn: attempt.source_turn_id,
        sourceVersion: attempt.source_version,
        ...(attempt.reservation_version === null
          ? {}
          : { reservationVersion: attempt.reservation_version }),
        ...(attempt.message_id === null
          ? {}
          : { messageId: attempt.message_id }),
      };
    const message = db
      .prepare(
        "SELECT message_id,state FROM session_messages WHERE session_id=? AND direction='inbound' AND state IN ('accepted','failed') AND message_id NOT IN (SELECT message_id FROM attention_receipt_resolutions) ORDER BY CASE state WHEN 'accepted' THEN 0 ELSE 1 END,created_at LIMIT 1"
      )
      .get(source.session_id) as
      | { message_id: string; state: string }
      | undefined;
    if (message)
      input.message = { id: message.message_id, state: message.state };
    return {
      ...classifyAttentionWait(input),
      grant_id: g?.grant_id ?? null,
      message_id: message?.message_id ?? null,
    };
  }
  private receipts(id: string): unknown[] {
    return this.store.db
      .prepare(
        "SELECT message_id,state FROM session_messages WHERE session_id=? AND direction='inbound' AND state IN ('accepted','failed') AND message_id NOT IN (SELECT message_id FROM attention_receipt_resolutions) ORDER BY created_at LIMIT 10"
      )
      .all(id);
  }
  private update(s: Subject, now: number, payload: unknown): void {
    this.store.assertOwner(this.owner);
    if (s.kind === "run" && payload && typeof payload === "object")
      payload = { event_replay: { complete: false, caught_up: false, gap: "invalid_source", completeness_scope: "unverified_retention" }, ...payload };
    let old: { gap?: string } = {};
    try {
      old = JSON.parse(s.payload_json) as { gap?: string };
    } catch {
      old = { gap: "history_gap" };
    }
    if (old.gap === "history_gap" && payload && typeof payload === "object")
      payload = { ...payload, gap: "history_gap" };
    this.store.db
      .prepare(
        "UPDATE attention_subjects SET sampled_at=?,generation=?,payload_json=? WHERE scope_id=? AND kind=? AND subject_id=?"
      )
      .run(
        now,
        this.owner.generation,
        JSON.stringify(payload),
        s.scope_id,
        s.kind,
        s.subject_id
      );
  }
  inventory(scopeId: string, now = Date.now(), after = "", limit = 100) {
    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(limit) || limit < 1 || limit > 100)
      throw new Error("invalid_limit");
    const scope = this.store.getScope(scopeId);
    if (!scope) throw new Error("not_found");
    const db = this.store.db;
    const counts = db
      .prepare(
        "SELECT COUNT(*) total,COALESCE(SUM(admitted),0) admitted FROM attention_subjects WHERE scope_id=?"
      )
      .get(scopeId) as { total: number; admitted: number };
    const rows = db
      .prepare(
        "SELECT * FROM attention_subjects WHERE scope_id=? AND kind||':'||subject_id>? ORDER BY kind,subject_id LIMIT ?"
      )
      .all(scopeId, after, limit + 1) as Subject[];
    const current = db
      .prepare("SELECT generation FROM daemon_owner WHERE singleton=1")
      .get() as { generation: number };
    const faulty = db
      .prepare(
        "SELECT 1 FROM attention_subjects WHERE scope_id=? AND (sampled_at<? OR generation<>? OR CASE WHEN json_valid(payload_json) THEN (json_extract(payload_json,'$.state')='unknown' OR json_extract(payload_json,'$.reason') IS NOT NULL AND json_extract(payload_json,'$.reason')<>'snapshot_only' OR json_extract(payload_json,'$.gap')='history_gap') ELSE 1 END) LIMIT 1"
      )
      .get(scopeId, now - 65000, current.generation);
    const coverage =
      now < scope.sampled_at
        ? "unknown"
        : scope.generation !== current.generation || now > scope.deadline
        ? "uncovered"
        : !scope.scan_complete ||
          scope.error ||
          counts.total > counts.admitted ||
          faulty
        ? "degraded"
        : "covered";
    return {
      scope,
      coverage,
      subject_total: counts.total,
      admitted: counts.admitted,
      returned: Math.min(limit, rows.length),
      truncated: rows.length > limit,
      next_after:
        rows.length > limit
          ? `${rows[limit - 1]!.kind}:${rows[limit - 1]!.subject_id}`
          : null,
      subjects: rows.slice(0, limit).map(({ payload_json, ...r }) => ({
        ...r,
        facts: (() => {
          try { return JSON.parse(payload_json) as unknown; }
          catch { return { state: "unknown", reason: "invalid_source", gap: "history_gap",
            ...(r.kind === "run" ? { event_replay: { complete: false, caught_up: false, gap: "invalid_source", completeness_scope: "unverified_retention" } } : {}) }; }
        })(),
      })),
      limitations: [
        "No complete mutable-source history",
        "Process liveness unknown",
        "Coverage is observation, not execution ownership",
      ],
    };
  }
}

FULL CONTEXT FILE packages/core/src/state/attention-store.ts
import { createHash, randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { realpathSync, statSync } from "node:fs";
import type Database from "better-sqlite3";
import { SessionDecision } from "@osstack/protocol";
import { withWrite } from "./db.js";

export function attentionDigest(value: unknown): string {
  const canonical = (v: unknown): string => {
    if (Array.isArray(v)) return `[${v.map(canonical).join(",")}]`;
    if (v !== null && typeof v === "object")
      return `{${Object.entries(v)
        .sort(([a], [b]) => (a < b ? -1 : a > b ? 1 : 0))
        .map(([k, x]) => `${JSON.stringify(k)}:${canonical(x)}`)
        .join(",")}}`;
    return JSON.stringify(v);
  };
  return createHash("sha256").update(canonical(value)).digest("hex");
}
export interface AttentionOwner {
  instance_id: string;
  generation: number;
  home_id: string;
}
export interface AttentionScope {
  scope_id: string;
  task_id: string;
  repo_path: string;
  discovery_after: string;
  scan_complete: number;
  sampled_at: number;
  deadline: number;
  generation: number;
  error: string | null;
}
export interface AttentionItem {
  item_id: string;
  scope_id: string;
  session_id: string;
  turn_id: string;
  decision_id: string;
  content_hash: string;
  decision: SessionDecision;
  source_version: number;
  revision: number;
  state: string;
  created_at: number;
  updated_at: number;
  external_message_id: string | null;
}
type ItemRow = Omit<AttentionItem, "decision"> & { decision_json: string };
function parseItem(row: ItemRow): AttentionItem {
  const { decision_json, ...rest } = row;
  return {
    ...rest,
    decision: SessionDecision.parse(JSON.parse(decision_json)),
  };
}
export class AttentionStore {
  constructor(readonly db: Database.Database, readonly path: string) {}
  /** Only called AFTER the daemon's process lock is acquired. Not an executor lease. */
  claimOwner(instanceId: string): AttentionOwner {
    return withWrite(this.db, this.path, () => {
      this.db
        .prepare(
          `INSERT INTO daemon_owner(singleton,home_id,instance_id,generation) VALUES(1,?,?,1)
        ON CONFLICT(singleton) DO UPDATE SET instance_id=excluded.instance_id,generation=generation+1`
        )
        .run(randomUUID(), instanceId);
      return this.db
        .prepare(
          "SELECT home_id,instance_id,generation FROM daemon_owner WHERE singleton=1"
        )
        .get() as AttentionOwner;
    });
  }
  assertOwner(owner: AttentionOwner): void {
    if (
      !this.db
        .prepare(
          "SELECT 1 FROM daemon_owner WHERE singleton=1 AND home_id=? AND instance_id=? AND generation=?"
        )
        .get(owner.home_id, owner.instance_id, owner.generation)
    )
      throw new Error("stale_owner");
  }
  write<T>(owner: AttentionOwner, fn: () => T): T {
    if (this.db.inTransaction) {
      this.assertOwner(owner);
      return fn();
    }
    return withWrite(this.db, this.path, () => {
      this.assertOwner(owner);
      return fn();
    });
  }
  registerScope(
    owner: AttentionOwner,
    taskId: string,
    repoPath: string
  ): AttentionScope {
    const canonicalRepo = realpathSync(repoPath);
    if (!statSync(canonicalRepo).isDirectory())
      throw new Error("repository_directory_required");
    return this.write(owner, () => {
      const task = this.db
        .prepare(
          "SELECT created_at FROM tasks WHERE task_id=? AND tenant_id IS NULL"
        )
        .get(taskId) as { created_at: number } | undefined;
      if (!task) throw new Error("not_found");
      if (
        (
          this.db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM attention_scopes").get() as {
            n: number;
          }
        ).n >= 100 &&
        !this.db
          .prepare(
            "SELECT 1 FROM attention_scopes WHERE task_id=? AND repo_path=?"
          )
          .get(taskId, canonicalRepo)
      )
        throw new Error("scope_capacity");
      const scopeId = attentionDigest([
        owner.home_id,
        taskId,
        canonicalRepo,
        task.created_at,
      ]);
      this.db
        .prepare(
          `INSERT INTO attention_scopes(scope_id,task_id,repo_path,generation) VALUES(?,?,?,?)
        ON CONFLICT(scope_id) DO NOTHING`
        )
        .run(scopeId, taskId, canonicalRepo, owner.generation);
      return this.getScope(scopeId)!;
    });
  }
  getScope(scopeId: string): AttentionScope | undefined {
    return this.db
      .prepare("SELECT * FROM attention_scopes WHERE scope_id=?")
      .get(scopeId) as AttentionScope | undefined;
  }
  listScopes(): AttentionScope[] {
    return this.db
      .prepare("SELECT * FROM attention_scopes ORDER BY scope_id")
      .all() as AttentionScope[];
  }
  getItem(scopeId: string, itemId: string): AttentionItem | undefined {
    const row = this.db
      .prepare("SELECT * FROM attention_items WHERE scope_id=? AND item_id=?")
      .get(scopeId, itemId) as ItemRow | undefined;
    return row ? parseItem(row) : undefined;
  }
  listItems(scopeId: string, after = "", limit = 100): AttentionItem[] {
    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(limit) || limit < 1 || limit > 100)
      throw new Error("invalid_limit");
    return (
      this.db
        .prepare(
          "SELECT * FROM attention_items WHERE scope_id=? AND item_id>? ORDER BY item_id LIMIT ?"
        )
        .all(scopeId, after, limit) as ItemRow[]
    ).map(parseItem);
  }
  projectDecision(
    owner: AttentionOwner,
    input: {
      scopeId: string;
      sessionId: string;
      turnId: string;
      sourceVersion: number;
      decision: unknown;
      now: number;
    }
  ): AttentionItem {
    const decision = SessionDecision.parse(input.decision);
    const contentHash = attentionDigest(decision);
    const itemId = attentionDigest([
      input.scopeId,
      input.sessionId,
      input.turnId,
      decision.decision_id,
    ]);
    if (
      !Number.isSafeInteger(input.sourceVersion) ||
      input.sourceVersion < 1 ||
      !Number.isFinite(input.now)
    )
      throw new Error("invalid_source_version");
    return this.write(owner, () => {
      if (!this.getScope(input.scopeId)) throw new Error("not_found");
      const existing = this.getItem(input.scopeId, itemId);
      if (existing && existing.content_hash !== contentHash)
        throw new Error("decision_content_conflict");
      if (existing && existing.source_version > input.sourceVersion)
        throw new Error("stale_source");
      this.db
        .prepare(
          `INSERT INTO attention_items(item_id,scope_id,session_id,turn_id,decision_id,content_hash,decision_json,source_version,created_at,updated_at)
        VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?) ON CONFLICT(item_id) DO UPDATE SET
        revision=revision+CASE WHEN source_version<>excluded.source_version OR state='source_missing' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END,
        state=CASE WHEN state='source_missing' THEN 'open' ELSE state END,
        source_version=excluded.source_version,updated_at=excluded.updated_at`
        )
        .run(
          itemId,
          input.scopeId,
          input.sessionId,
          input.turnId,
          decision.decision_id,
          contentHash,
          JSON.stringify(decision),
          input.sourceVersion,
          input.now,
          input.now
        );
      return this.getItem(input.scopeId, itemId)!;
    });
  }
  release(
    owner: AttentionOwner,
    scopeId: string,
    kind: string,
    subjectId: string,
    expectedVersion: number
  ): void {
    if (!["session", "run", "loop"].includes(kind))
      throw new Error("invalid_subject_kind");
    this.write(owner, () => {
      const row = this.db
        .prepare(
          "SELECT payload_json,source_version FROM attention_subjects WHERE scope_id=? AND kind=? AND subject_id=?"
        )
        .get(scopeId, kind, subjectId) as
        | { payload_json: string; source_version: number }
        | undefined;
      if (!row || row.source_version !== expectedVersion)
        throw new Error("subject_version_conflict");
      this.db
        .prepare(
          "UPDATE attention_subjects SET admitted=0,payload_json=? WHERE scope_id=? AND kind=? AND subject_id=?"
        )
        .run(
          JSON.stringify({
            ...JSON.parse(row.payload_json),
            reason: "owner_released",
          }),
          scopeId,
          kind,
          subjectId
        );
    });
  }
  markMissing(owner: AttentionOwner, scopeId: string, sessionId: string): void {
    this.write(owner, () =>
      this.db
        .prepare(
          `UPDATE attention_items SET state='source_missing',revision=revision+1
      WHERE scope_id=? AND session_id=? AND state='open'`
        )
        .run(scopeId, sessionId)
    );
  }
}

FULL CONTEXT FILE packages/cli/src/supervision-service.ts
import { AttentionInbox } from "@osstack/protocol";
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import type Database from "better-sqlite3";
import {
  AttentionStore,
  AttentionObserver,
  AttentionExperience,
  AttentionActions,
  attentionDigest,
  readAttentionRecovery,
  type AttentionSender,
} from "@osstack/core";
type LoopReadInput = { repo: string; path: string; loop_id: string; signal: AbortSignal };
export interface SupervisionOptions {
  loopReader?: (input: LoopReadInput) => Promise<unknown>;
  readTimeoutMs?: number;
}
type ReadFailure = "timeout" | "aborted" | "invalid_payload" | "read_failed" | "read_capacity_exceeded";
type LoopFacts = Record<string, unknown> & { state: string; reason?: string };
interface ReadSlot {
  controller: AbortController;
  logical: Promise<LoopFacts>;
}
interface LoopBinding {
  binding_hash: string;
  admitted: number;
  generation: number;
  payload_json: string;
}
function loopPayload(json: string): Record<string, unknown> | null {
  try {
    const value: unknown = JSON.parse(json);
    return value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value)
      ? value as Record<string, unknown> : null;
  } catch { return null; }
}
function validLoopFacts(value: unknown, id: string): value is LoopFacts {
  if (!value || typeof value !== "object") return false;
  const v = value as Record<string, unknown>;
  if (v.loop_id !== id || typeof v.path !== "string" || v.process_liveness !== "unknown") return false;
  if (v.state === "source_missing") return v.coverage === "uncovered" && v.gap === "source_missing";
  return v.state === "snapshot_available" && v.coverage === "snapshot_only"
    && v.gap === "best_effort_snapshot_no_event_replay" && typeof v.updated_at === "string"
    && Array.isArray(v.in_progress) && Array.isArray(v.pending)
    && Number.isSafeInteger(v.completed_count) && (v.completed_count as number) >= 0
    && typeof v.recovery_required === "boolean";
}
/** The daemon has already acquired its kernel process lock before construction. */
export class SupervisionService {
  readonly store: AttentionStore;
  readonly observer: AttentionObserver;
  readonly experience: AttentionExperience;
  private delivering = false;
  private deliveryRecoveryNeeded = false;
  private readonly deliveryAbort = new AbortController();
  private deliveryDone: Promise<void> | undefined;
  private ciReader: import("@osstack/github").AttentionCiReader | undefined;
  private timer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | undefined;
  private started = false;
  private stopped = false;
  private dirty = false;
  private scanDone: Promise<void> | undefined;
  private stopDone: Promise<void> | undefined;
  private epoch = 0;
  // A slot outlives its logical timeout until the underlying promise settles.
  // It holds no continuation that can access SQLite after stop.
  private readonly readers = new Map<string, ReadSlot>();
  private readonly logicalReads = new Set<Promise<LoopFacts>>();
  private readonly readTimeoutMs: number;
  constructor(
    db: Database.Database,
    path: string,
    readonly sender?: AttentionSender,
    private readonly options: SupervisionOptions = {}
  ) {
    this.readTimeoutMs = options.readTimeoutMs ?? 1000;
    if (!Number.isFinite(this.readTimeoutMs) || this.readTimeoutMs < 1 || this.readTimeoutMs > 1000)
      throw new Error("invalid_read_timeout");
    this.store = new AttentionStore(db, path);
    this.observer = new AttentionObserver(
      this.store,
      this.store.claimOwner(randomUUID())
    );
    this.experience = new AttentionExperience(this.store, this.observer.owner);
    this.experience.recoverDeliveries();
    new AttentionActions(this.store, this.observer.owner).recover();
  }
  private assertRunning(): void {
    if (this.stopped) throw new Error("supervision_stopped");
  }
  start(): void {
    this.assertRunning();
    if (this.started) return;
    this.started = true;
    void this.requestScan("start").catch(() => {});
    this.timer = setInterval(() => { void this.requestScan("timer").catch(() => {}); }, 30000);
    this.timer.unref();
  }
  /** Dirty signals are hints; each pass still reads the persisted source facts. */
  requestScan(_reason?: string): Promise<void> {
    if (this.stopped) return Promise.reject(new Error("supervision_stopped"));
    this.dirty = true;
    this.scanDone ??= Promise.resolve().then(async () => {
      while (this.dirty && !this.stopped) {
        this.dirty = false;
        try { await this.scan(); } catch { /* prior coverage expires */ }
      }
    }).finally(() => {
      this.scanDone = undefined;
      // A dirty signal can arrive after the loop exits but before this cleanup.
      if (this.dirty && !this.stopped) return this.requestScan("coalesced");
      return undefined;
    });
    return this.scanDone;
  }
  private async scan(): Promise<void> {
    this.assertRunning();
    while (!this.stopped) {
      // A socket request spans multiple event-loop turns (accept, frame, reply).
      // Leave room within its 10ms p95 budget instead of consuming the observer's
      // entire 5ms ceiling on every turn. The observer still checks each subject.
      const step = this.observer.step({maxSubjects:25,maxEvents:200,maxDurationMs:2});
      if (step.passComplete) break;
      await new Promise<void>(resolve => setImmediate(resolve));
    }
    if (this.stopped) return;
    const loops = this.store.db.prepare(
      "SELECT scope_id,subject_id,payload_json FROM attention_subjects WHERE kind='loop' AND admitted=1"
    ).all() as { scope_id: string; subject_id: string; payload_json: string }[];
    await Promise.allSettled(loops.map(async (row) => {
      let path: unknown;
      try { path = (JSON.parse(row.payload_json) as Record<string, unknown>).state_path; } catch { /* below */ }
      if (typeof path !== "string" || !path || path.length > 4096) {
        if (!this.stopped) this.store.write(this.observer.owner, () => {
          // Do not replace newer metadata or an explicit owner release.
          this.store.db.prepare("UPDATE attention_subjects SET sampled_at=?,payload_json=? WHERE scope_id=? AND kind='loop' AND subject_id=? AND payload_json=?")
            .run(Date.now(), JSON.stringify({ state: "unknown", reason: "invalid_payload", process_liveness: "unknown" }), row.scope_id, row.subject_id, row.payload_json);
        });
        return;
      }
      try {
        await this.handle("attention.loop", {
          scope_id: row.scope_id, loop_id: row.subject_id,
          state_path: path,
        });
      } catch { /* source errors never suppress other readers */ }
    }));
    if (this.stopped) return;
    for (const scope of this.store.listScopes()) {
      try { this.experience.enqueue(scope.scope_id); }
      catch { /* Keep observed items; one unavailable scope must not block other notices. */ }
    }
    if (this.sender && !this.delivering) {
      // Only this service owns the local idempotent sender. A failed receipt
      // write leaves `sending`; reconcile it before the next bounded batch.
      if (this.deliveryRecoveryNeeded) {
        this.experience.recoverDeliveries();
        this.deliveryRecoveryNeeded = false;
      }
      this.delivering = true;
      this.deliveryDone = Promise.resolve().then(() => this.experience.drainBatch(this.sender!, { signal: this.deliveryAbort.signal }))
        .then(() => {})
        .catch(() => { this.deliveryRecoveryNeeded = true; })
        .finally(() => { this.delivering = false; });
    }
  }
  stop(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.stopDone) return this.stopDone;
    this.stopped = true;
    this.deliveryAbort.abort();
    this.epoch++;
    this.dirty = false;
    if (this.timer) clearInterval(this.timer);
    this.timer = undefined;
    for (const slot of this.readers.values()) slot.controller.abort();
    this.stopDone = Promise.allSettled([this.scanDone, this.deliveryDone, ...this.logicalReads]).then(() => {});
    return this.stopDone;
  }
  private loopBinding(scope: string, id: string): LoopBinding | undefined {
    return this.store.db.prepare(
      "SELECT binding_hash,admitted,generation,payload_json FROM attention_subjects WHERE scope_id=? AND kind='loop' AND subject_id=?"
    ).get(scope, id) as LoopBinding | undefined;
  }
  private readLoop(scopeId: string, id: string, path: string): Promise<LoopFacts> {
    this.assertRunning();
    const epoch = this.epoch;
    const binding = attentionDigest([path, id]);
    const key = JSON.stringify([scopeId, "loop", id, binding]);
    const scope = this.store.getScope(scopeId)!;
    // Register the binding before IO, so release and competing RPCs see it.
    this.store.write(this.observer.owner, () => {
      this.assertRunning();
      const old = this.loopBinding(scopeId, id);
      if (old && old.binding_hash !== binding) throw new Error("loop_binding_conflict");
      if (old) {
        const payload = loopPayload(old.payload_json);
        if (!payload || payload.reason === "owner_released") return;
        const count = (this.store.db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM attention_subjects WHERE admitted=1").get() as { n: number }).n;
        if (old.generation !== this.observer.owner.generation || (!old.admitted && count < 100))
          this.store.db.prepare("UPDATE attention_subjects SET admitted=?,generation=?,sampled_at=0,payload_json=? WHERE scope_id=? AND kind='loop' AND subject_id=?")
            .run(old.admitted || count < 100 ? 1 : 0, this.observer.owner.generation,
              JSON.stringify({ ...payload, state: "unknown", reason: "read_pending" }), scopeId, id);
        return;
      }
      const count = (this.store.db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM attention_subjects WHERE admitted=1").get() as { n: number }).n;
      this.store.db.prepare(
        "INSERT INTO attention_subjects(scope_id,kind,subject_id,binding_hash,source_version,admitted,discovered_at,sampled_at,generation,payload_json) VALUES(?,'loop',?,?,0,?,?,?,?,?)"
      ).run(scopeId, id, binding, count < 100 ? 1 : 0, Date.now(), 0, this.observer.owner.generation,
        JSON.stringify({ state_path: path, state: "unknown", reason: count < 100 ? "read_pending" : "capacity_exceeded" }));
    });
    const old = this.loopBinding(scopeId, id)!;
    const failure = (reason: ReadFailure): LoopFacts => ({ loop_id: id, path, state: "unknown",
      process_liveness: "unknown", coverage: "uncovered", gap: "snapshot_unavailable", reason });
    const payload = loopPayload(old.payload_json);
    if (!payload) return Promise.resolve(failure("invalid_payload"));
    if (payload.reason === "owner_released")
      return Promise.resolve({ state: "unknown", reason: "owner_released" });
    const existing = this.readers.get(key);
    if (existing) return existing.logical;
    const commit = (facts: LoopFacts): LoopFacts => {
      // No await from the lifecycle check through the owner-fenced commit.
      if (this.stopped || epoch !== this.epoch) return facts;
      this.store.write(this.observer.owner, () => {
        const current = this.loopBinding(scopeId, id);
        const currentPayload = current ? loopPayload(current.payload_json) : null;
        if (!current || current.binding_hash !== binding || current.generation !== this.observer.owner.generation
          || current.admitted !== old.admitted || !currentPayload || currentPayload.reason === "owner_released") return;
        this.store.db.prepare(
          "UPDATE attention_subjects SET sampled_at=?,payload_json=? WHERE scope_id=? AND kind='loop' AND subject_id=?"
        ).run(Date.now(), JSON.stringify({ ...facts, state_path: path,
          reason: facts.reason ?? (!current.admitted ? "capacity_exceeded" : facts.state === "source_missing"
            ? "source_missing" : facts.recovery_required ? "recovery_required" : "snapshot_only") }), scopeId, id);
      });
      return facts;
    };
    if (!old.admitted || this.readers.size >= 100)
      return Promise.resolve(commit(failure("read_capacity_exceeded")));
    const controller = new AbortController();
    let reason: ReadFailure = "aborted";
    let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
    let abortListener: (() => void) | undefined;
    const aborted = new Promise<LoopFacts>((resolve) => {
      abortListener = () => resolve(failure(reason));
      controller.signal.addEventListener("abort", abortListener, { once: true });
      timer = setTimeout(() => { reason = "timeout"; controller.abort(); }, this.readTimeoutMs);
    });
    const physical = Promise.resolve().then(async () => {
      controller.signal.throwIfAborted();
      const reader = this.options.loopReader ?? (await import("./attention-loop-source.js")).readAttentionLoop;
      controller.signal.throwIfAborted();
      return await reader({ repo: scope.repo_path, path, loop_id: id, signal: controller.signal });
    }).then((value) => validLoopFacts(value, id) ? value : failure("invalid_payload"), (error: unknown) =>
      failure(controller.signal.aborted ? reason : error instanceof SyntaxError
        || (error instanceof Error && error.name === "ZodError") ? "invalid_payload" : "read_failed"));
    // Physical settlement has no DB access, even if the logical deadline passed.
    const settled = physical.finally(() => { this.readers.delete(key); });
    const logical = Promise.race([settled, aborted]).then(commit).finally(() => {
      if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
      if (abortListener) controller.signal.removeEventListener("abort", abortListener);
      this.logicalReads.delete(logical);
    });
    this.readers.set(key, { controller, logical });
    this.logicalReads.add(logical);
    return logical;
  }
  async handle(method: string, params: unknown): Promise<unknown> {
    this.assertRunning();
    if (!params || typeof params !== "object")
      throw new Error("explicit_scope_required");
    const p = params as Record<string, unknown>;
    const str = (key: string) => {
      const value = p[key];
      if (typeof value !== "string" || !value || value.length > 4096)
        throw new Error(`invalid_${key}`);
      return value;
    };
    if (method === "attention.watch") {
      if (p["default_tenant"] !== true)
        throw new Error("explicit_default_tenant_required");
      const scope = this.store.registerScope(
        this.observer.owner,
        str("task_id"),
        str("repo_path")
      );
      await this.requestScan("watch");
      this.assertRunning();
      return this.observer.inventory(scope.scope_id);
    }
    const scopeId = str("scope_id");
    if (!this.store.getScope(scopeId)) throw new Error("not_found");
    if (method === "attention.recovery") {
      if (Array.isArray(p) || Object.keys(p).some(key => !["scope_id","after","limit"].includes(key)) || (p["after"] !== undefined && typeof p["after"] !== "string") || (p["limit"] !== undefined && typeof p["limit"] !== "number")) throw new Error("invalid_recovery_request");
      return readAttentionRecovery(this.store,this.observer.owner,{scope_id:scopeId,...(p["after"] === undefined ? {} : {after:p["after"] as string}),...(p["limit"] === undefined ? {} : {limit:p["limit"] as number})});
    }
    if (method === "attention.release") {
      this.store.release(
        this.observer.owner,
        scopeId,
        str("kind"),
        str("subject_id"),
        Number(p["expected_version"])
      );
      return {
        released: true,
        execution_affected: false,
        coverage: "uncovered",
      };
    }
    if (method === "attention.loop")
      return await this.readLoop(scopeId, str("loop_id"), str("state_path"));
    if (method === "attention.ci") {
      const { AttentionCiReader } = await import("@osstack/github");
      this.assertRunning();
      this.ciReader ??= new AttentionCiReader();
      return await this.ciReader.read({
        repo: str("github_repo"),
        pr: p["pr"],
        head: str("head"),
        required: p["required"],
      });
    }
    if (method === "attention.brief")
      return this.experience.brief(scopeId, str("actor"));
    if (method === "attention.posture") {
      const posture = str("posture");
      if (!["quiet", "away", "attended"].includes(posture))
        throw new Error("invalid_posture");
      this.experience.posture(
        scopeId,
        str("actor"),
        posture as "quiet" | "away" | "attended"
      );
      return { posture };
    }
    if (method === "attention.seen") {
      if (!Array.isArray(p["revisions"])) throw new Error("invalid_revisions");
      this.experience.seen(scopeId, str("actor"), p["revisions"]);
      return { marked: true };
    }
    if (method === "attention.foreground") {
      if (!Array.isArray(p["subjects"])) throw new Error("invalid_subjects");
      return this.experience.foreground(scopeId, p["subjects"]);
    }
    if (method === "attention.handoff")
      return this.experience.handoff(scopeId, str("client_id"), str("token"));
    if (method === "attention.turn-end")
      return this.experience.turnEnd(
        scopeId,
        str("client_id"),
        str("token"),
        str("role")
      );
    if (method === "attention.deliveries")
      return this.store.db
        .prepare(
          "SELECT * FROM attention_deliveries WHERE scope_id=? AND delivery_id>? ORDER BY delivery_id LIMIT 100"
        )
        .all(scopeId, typeof p["after"] === "string" ? p["after"] : "");
    if (method === "attention.inventory")
      return this.observer.inventory(
        scopeId,
        Date.now(),
        typeof p["after"] === "string" ? p["after"] : ""
      );
    if (method === "attention.inbox") {
      const items = this.store.listItems(
        scopeId,
        typeof p["after"] === "string" ? p["after"] : ""
      );
      const counts = this.store.db
        .prepare(
          "SELECT state,COUNT(*) total FROM attention_items WHERE scope_id=? GROUP BY state"
        )
        .all(scopeId);
      return AttentionInbox.parse({
        schema_version: 1,
        execution_takeover: false,
        scope_id: scopeId,
        items,
        counts,
        next_after: items.length === 100 ? items.at(-1)!.item_id : null,
        coverage: this.observer.inventory(scopeId).coverage,
      });
    }
    if (method === "attention.get") {
      const item = this.store.getItem(scopeId, str("item_id"));
      if (!item) throw new Error("not_found");
      return item;
    }
    throw new Error("unsupported_attention_method");
  }
}
